#!/bin/bash
# workstationadmin-test-harness.txt - synthetic fixture harness (test host only).
# Account: any ordinary non-root user on a NON-macOS test host.
# Root: refused. macOS: refused. All files live in a fresh mktemp dir under /tmp,
#   deleted on exit. Commands are replaced by stubs only inside the fixture.
# This harness proves script logic against synthetic stub output only.
# It is NOT Mac validation: stub output formats are assumptions.
# Usage: bash workstationadmin-test-harness.txt [suite-dir] | -h
# CONTAINMENT (S07, review item 15): this harness does not sandbox itself. It must only be
# run inside the test runner's isolated container command (bwrap, read-only suite, private
# /tmp, no network). Passing it is synthetic author/test-runner evidence only; it does
# not prove privacy, Mac behaviour or Bash 3.2 runtime. Privacy scan rules are
# documented at scan_fixture/san_out below and have their own negative tests.
set -u
case "${1:-}" in -h|--help) sed -n '2,15p' "$0" | sed 's/^# \{0,1\}//'; exit 0;; esac
[ "$EUID" -ne 0 ] || { echo "harness refuses root" >&2; exit 4; }
[ "$(uname -s)" != "Darwin" ] || { echo "harness refuses macOS" >&2; exit 4; }
SUITE=$(cd "${1:-$(dirname "$0")}" && pwd -P)
T=$(mktemp -d /tmp/wsp-fx.XXXXXXXX) || exit 4
T=$(cd "$T" && pwd -P) || exit 4      # canonical fixture root (review item 15)
case "$T" in /tmp/wsp-fx.*) ;; *) echo "fixture root not canonical under /tmp" >&2; exit 4;; esac
TICKPID=""
trap '[ -z "$TICKPID" ] || kill "$TICKPID" 2>/dev/null; [ -n "${KEEP:-}" ] || rm -rf "$T"' EXIT INT TERM
PASS=0; FAIL=0
ok() { PASS=$((PASS+1)); echo "ok   - $1"; }
no() { FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); echo "FAIL - $1"; }
CANARY_USER="zzcanaryuser"; CANARY_HOST="ZZCANARYHOST"; CANARY_KEY="QQQQ-WWWW-EEEE-RRRR-TTTT-YYYY"
CANARY_URL="https://zzcanary.invalid/x"; CANARY_TAG="ZZTAG42"

setup() { # fresh fixture tree
  F="$T/fx$1"; mkdir -p "$F/stubs" "$F/data" "$F/bin" "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning" \
    "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning-Session" "$F/Library/LaunchAgents" "$F/Users/Shared" "$F/Applications" "$F/out"
  chmod 700 "$F/out"
  cp "$SUITE"/workstationadmin-*.txt "$SUITE"/UserSpace-*.txt "$F/bin/"; chmod 644 "$F/bin/"*
  # generic stub: output data/<cmd>__<args>.out, exit data/<cmd>__<args>.rc, log call
  cat > "$F/stubs/_stub" <<'S'
#!/bin/bash
n=$(basename "$0"); k=$(printf '%s_' "$n" "$@" | tr -c 'A-Za-z0-9._-' '_' | cut -c1-120)
d="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/data"
if env | grep -q '^WSP_RID_SALT='; then echo ENVLEAK-salt >> "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/envleak.log"; fi
printf '%s %s %s\n' "$n" "${1:-}" "${2:-}" >> "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/calls.log"   # name + first two args only
if [ "$n" = defaults ] && [ "${1:-}" = write ]; then printf '%s' "${5:-$4}" > "$d/defaults_read_${2}_${3}_.out.w"; exit 0; fi
if [ "$n" = defaults ] && [ "${1:-}" = read ] && [ -f "$d/defaults_read_${2}_${3}_.out.w" ]; then
  v=$(cat "$d/defaults_read_${2}_${3}_.out.w"); case "$v" in true) v=1;; false) v=0;; esac; echo "$v"; exit 0; fi
[ -f "$d/$k.err" ] && cat "$d/$k.err" >&2
[ -f "$d/$k.out" ] && cat "$d/$k.out"
if [ -f "$d/$k.rc" ]; then exit "$(cat "$d/$k.rc")"; fi
[ -f "$d/$k.out" ] && exit 0
exit 1
S
  chmod 755 "$F/stubs/_stub"
  mkdir -p "$F/usr/libexec/ApplicationFirewall" "$F/System/Library/CoreServices/RemoteManagement/ARDAgent.app/Contents/Resources" "$F/usr/bin"
  ln -s "$F/stubs/_stub" "$F/usr/libexec/ApplicationFirewall/socketfilterfw"
  ln -s "$F/stubs/_stub" "$F/usr/bin/tty"   # d14 (S1): the library calls "$WSP_ROOT/usr/bin/tty" by absolute path; the fixture root provides the stub there
  ln -s "$F/stubs/_stub" "$F/System/Library/CoreServices/RemoteManagement/ARDAgent.app/Contents/Resources/kickstart"
  for c in sw_vers scutil dscl dsmemberutil defaults socketfilterfw fdesetup diskutil ioreg profiles csrutil \
     systemsetup dseditgroup sysadminctl spctl codesign lipo pkgutil installer pmset plutil killall stat_mac sudo uname id_ctx stat_ctx softwareupdate who tty sleep; do
    ln -s _stub "$F/stubs/$c"; done
  # socketfilterfw is called by absolute path; provide a matching file inside PATH-only fixture
  echo 15.5 > "$F/data/sw_vers_-productVersion_.out"; echo 24F74 > "$F/data/sw_vers_-buildVersion_.out"; echo x86_64 > "$F/data/uname_-m_.out"
  echo "$CANARY_HOST" > "$F/data/scutil_--get_LocalHostName_.out"; echo "$CANARY_HOST Mac" > "$F/data/scutil_--get_ComputerName_.out"
  printf 'GroupMembership: workstationadmin\n' > "$F/data/dscl_._-read__Groups_com.apple.access_ssh_GroupMembership_.out"
  printf 'UniqueID: 501\n' > "$F/data/dscl_._-read__Users_workstationadmin_UniqueID_.out"
  # identity/console context (D7-02): read by wsp_read_user/wsp_read_console via id_ctx/stat_ctx
  echo workstationadmin > "$F/data/id_ctx_-un_.out"; echo workstationadmin > "$F/data/stat_ctx_-f__Su__dev_console_.out"
  : > "$F/data/who_.out"
  echo "FileVault is On." > "$F/data/fdesetup_status_.out"
  printf 'There are no configuration profiles installed\n' > "$F/data/profiles_show_-type_configuration_.out"; echo "Automatic check is on" > "$F/data/softwareupdate_--schedule_.out"
  # write prerequisites (D2-02): SIP, MDM state, operator admin
  echo "System Integrity Protection status: enabled." > "$F/data/csrutil_status_.out"
  printf 'Enrolled via DEP: No\nMDM enrollment: No\n' > "$F/data/profiles_status_-type_enrollment_.out"
  echo "user is a member of the group" > "$F/data/dsmemberutil_checkmembership_-U_workstationadmin_-G_admin_.out"
  printf 'TARGET_OS_MAJOR=15\nALLOWED_BUILDS=24F74\nAPP_LIST=ExampleChat/ExampleChat:Example Sheets/Example Sheets/store\nUPDATES_AUTO_CHECK=on\nUPDATES_AUTO_DOWNLOAD=on\nUPDATES_CONFIG_DATA=on\nUPDATES_CRITICAL=on\nUPDATES_MACOS_INSTALL=off\nUPDATES_APP_STORE=on\n' > "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/policy.conf"
  printf 'USERS=%s\nUSER_PREFS=scrollbars clock24\nTARGET_OS_MAJOR=15\nAPP_LIST=ExampleChat/ExampleChat:Example Sheets/Example Sheets/store\n' "$(id -un)" > "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning-Session/session.conf"
  : > "$T/decl-${F##*/}"
  : > "$F/calls.log"; : > "$F/envleak.log"
  find "$F" -mindepth 1 ! -type d ! -path "$F/bin/*" ! -name calls.log ! -name envleak.log -print0 | while IFS= read -r -d '' x; do declare_input "$x"; done
  decl_seal
}
# ---- privacy scan rules (test review d3/d4) -------------------------------------
# TRUST BOUNDARY: this detector guards against ACCIDENTAL leaks by the suite
# under test. The declaration manifest lives outside the fixture and its hash
# is also held in harness memory (tamper check), but the suite runs as the same
# uid, so deliberately malicious suite code could still subvert it. Containing
# malicious code is the job of the test runner's bwrap sandbox, not of this detector.
# Rules (every object under $F, NUL-framed, symlinks NOT followed):
#   - any path component name containing the canary               -> hit
#   - a symlink: hit unless declared with that exact target; link text
#     containing the canary is always a hit
#   - any other non-regular object (FIFO, socket, device)          -> hit
#   - a regular file that cannot be read (grep exit >1)            -> hit
#   - a regular file containing the canary is exempt ONLY if
#       (a) its exact path is declared and its sha256 is unchanged, or
#       (b) it is bin/<name>, byte-identical to the suite file <name>.
# Declaring a path containing a newline or tab is refused.
DECL_SUM=""
decl_file() { printf '%s' "$T/decl-${F##*/}"; }
decl_seal() { DECL_SUM=$(sha256sum < "$(decl_file)" | cut -c1-64); }
declare_input() {
  case "$1" in *$'\n'*|*$'\t'*) no "declaration refused: path has newline/tab"; return 1;; esac
  if [ -L "$1" ]; then printf 'L\t%s\t%s\n' "$(readlink "$1")" "$1" >> "$(decl_file)"
  else printf 'F\t%s\t%s\n' "$(sha256sum < "$1" | cut -c1-64)" "$1" >> "$(decl_file)"; fi
  decl_seal
}
is_declared() { local h; h=$(sha256sum < "$1" | cut -c1-64); grep -qxF "F	$h	$1" "$(decl_file)"; }
is_declared_link() { grep -qxF "L	$(readlink "$1")	$1" "$(decl_file)"; }
# scan_fixture CANARY -> prints offending objects (empty = clean)
scan_fixture() {
  local c="$1" x b r
  if [ "$(sha256sum < "$(decl_file)" | cut -c1-64)" != "$DECL_SUM" ]; then echo "MANIFEST-TAMPERED"; return; fi
  find "$F" -mindepth 1 -print0 | while IFS= read -r -d '' x; do
    case "${x#"$F"}" in *"$c"*) printf 'NAME:%s\n' "$x"; continue;; esac
    [ -d "$x" ] && [ ! -L "$x" ] && continue
    if [ -L "$x" ]; then
      case "$(readlink "$x")" in *"$c"*) printf 'LINKTEXT:%s\n' "$x"; continue;; esac
      is_declared_link "$x" || printf 'LINK:%s\n' "$x"
      continue
    fi
    if [ ! -f "$x" ]; then printf 'NONREG:%s\n' "$x"; continue; fi
    grep -qF -- "$c" "$x" 2>/dev/null; r=$?
    [ $r -eq 1 ] && continue
    if [ $r -gt 1 ]; then printf 'UNREADABLE:%s\n' "$x"; continue; fi
    is_declared "$x" && continue
    case "$x" in "$F/bin/"*) b=${x#"$F/bin/"}; case "$b" in */*) ;; *) cmp -s "$x" "$SUITE/$b" && continue;; esac;; esac
    printf 'CONTENT:%s\n' "$x"
  done
}
# Terminal output: the ONLY sanitisation is replacing the exact prompt + the exact
# typed destination + CRLF (one occurrence) with a placeholder. Anything else on
# that line (or elsewhere) stays and is scanned.
DPROMPT='Destination directory for the key file (local disk, not cloud-synced): '
san_out() { local o="$1" d="$2"; printf '%s' "${o/"$DPROMPT$d"$'\r\n'/"$DPROMPT<typed>"$'\r\n'}"; }
# Bounded runtime (test review D6): every suite invocation has a hard per-run timeout;
# a timeout is a FAIL naming the script, never a silent stall. Progress marks
# (# t=...) go to stdout so a killed run shows where it was.
RUN_LIMIT=${WSP_HARNESS_RUN_LIMIT:-60}; RUNS=0; RUN_TIMEOUTS=0
# D7-03: a timeout is recorded in a FILE and announced on fd 9 (the harness's
# real stdout), so a run() inside $(...) cannot lose it; finish() reconciles.
exec 9>&1; : > "$T/run-timeouts.log"
# D9 (S9): 1 s wall-clock ticker (epoch seconds) so a timeout can be told apart from a stalled test host
: > "$T/ticker.log"; ( while :; do date +%s >> "$T/ticker.log"; sleep 1; done ) >/dev/null 2>&1 &
TICKPID=$!
HARNESS_USER=$(id -un)
run() { # run SCRIPT args... -> sets RC, OUT
  _s="$1"; shift; RUNS=$((RUNS+1))
  # context default per script unless a test locked it: UserSpace = target user, else operator
  if [ ! -f "$F/data/ctx.lock" ]; then
    case "$_s" in UserSpace-*) _cu=$HARNESS_USER;; *) _cu=workstationadmin;; esac
    printf '%s\n' "$_cu" > "$F/data/id_ctx_-un_.out"; printf '%s\n' "$_cu" > "$F/data/stat_ctx_-f__Su__dev_console_.out"
    rm -f "$F/data/id_ctx_-un_.rc" "$F/data/stat_ctx_-f__Su__dev_console_.rc"
  fi
  _in=/dev/null; case "$_s" in UserSpace-preferences.txt) [ -f "$F/data/tty.in" ] && _in="$F/data/tty.in";; esac   # d13: typed answers reach ONLY the module that asks (as stdin, like a terminal)
  OUT=$(cd "$F" && ptimeout "$RUN_LIMIT" env -i HOME="$F/home" TMPDIR="$F" WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT="$F" /bin/bash "$F/bin/$_s" "$@" 2>"$F/stderr" <"$_in"); RC=$?
  if [ "$RC" -eq 124 ] || [ "$RC" -eq 137 ]; then RUN_TIMEOUTS=$((RUN_TIMEOUTS+1)); FAIL=$((FAIL+1))
    printf '%s\n' "$_s" >> "$T/run-timeouts.log"; echo "FAIL - TIMEOUT ${RUN_LIMIT}s: $_s $*" | cut -c1-120 >&9
    diag_timeout "$_s" "$*"; fi
}
# D9 (S9): evidence for 60 s timeouts. Each timeout line says when it happened (wall and
# harness seconds), what the in-harness 1 s ticker saw (a gap means the whole machine
# stalled: paused test host or clock jump, not a hung script), and how long the run really took.
diag_timeout() { local now gap mx
  now=$(date +%s) || now=0; gap=-; mx=-
  if [ -f "$T/ticker.log" ]; then
    mx=$(tail -n 1 "$T/ticker.log" 2>/dev/null) || mx=-
    gap=$((now - ${mx:-0})) 2>/dev/null || gap=-
  fi
  echo "# DIAG timeout: script=$1 args=$(printf '%s' "$2" | cut -c1-40) wall=$(date '+%H:%M:%S') harness_t=${SECONDS}s limit=${RUN_LIMIT}s ticker_last_age=${gap}s ticker_max_gap=$(ticker_max_gap)s" | cut -c1-260 >&9; }
ticker_max_gap() { # largest gap between consecutive 1 s ticks so far (0 = machine ran continuously)
  [ -s "$T/ticker.log" ] || { echo 0; return; }
  awk 'NR>1 && $1-p>m {m=$1-p} {p=$1} END{print m+0}' "$T/ticker.log" 2>/dev/null || echo "?"; }
# ---- d15 HARNESS CHANGE (pause-aware limits; flagged for the reviewer; no assertion is changed or weakened) ----
# A paused test host or a jumped clock makes the 1 s ticker skip. stall_total = the seconds of every gap between two ticks that is longer than 5 s (a dead
# ticker earns NO credit: only gaps between two ticks count). The whole-harness budget and every per-run limit count ACTIVE time = wall time minus stall.
# A real hang still hits the limit (a hung script keeps the ticker running, so no stall is credited). STALL is always reported, never hidden.
# PT_TICKER lets the self-test point at a synthetic ticker file.
stall_total() { awk 'NR>1 && $1-p>5 {s+=$1-p-1} {p=$1} END{print s+0}' "${PT_TICKER:-$T/ticker.log}" 2>/dev/null || echo 0; }
# d15 fix round 2 (gate 15:35): the H self-tests time the run with SECONDS and point ptimeout at a synthetic ticker, so a REAL host pause inside a test
# breaks their wall-time bounds (H6 took=105s, H6b took=782s with the right rc). pt_stalled EPOCH: true if the real ticker shows a >5 s gap since EPOCH.
# A test that was paused is run again (at most 3 tries) instead of judged on paused wall time; the 3rd try is judged as is.
pt_stalled() { sleep 1.2; awk -v t0="$1" 'NR>1 && $1>=t0-1 && $1-p>5 {f=1} {p=$1} END{exit !f}' "$T/ticker.log" 2>/dev/null; }
ptimeout() { # LIMIT cmd args... : like "timeout -k 5 LIMIT cmd" (exit 124 on expiry), but LIMIT counts ACTIVE seconds (wall time minus ticker-measured stalls inside the run window)
  local lim=$1 pid e0 now el st lt l2 w n=0; shift
  printf -v e0 '%(%s)T' -1
  if command -v setsid >/dev/null 2>&1; then setsid -w "$@" <&0 & pid=$!; else "$@" <&0 & pid=$!; fi
  while kill -0 "$pid" 2>/dev/null; do
    printf -v now '%(%s)T' -1; el=$((now-e0))
    if [ "$el" -ge "$lim" ]; then
      # d15 triage (gate 13:24): after a host pause this loop and the ticker wake together; the tick that closes the gap may not be written yet,
      # so the gap would not be credited and a paused run was killed as a 60 s TIMEOUT. If the last tick is older than 5 s (a gap that would earn
      # credit), wait up to 3 s for the next tick before deciding. A dead ticker still earns no credit (it only delays the kill by 3 s).
      lt=$(tail -n 1 "${PT_TICKER:-$T/ticker.log}" 2>/dev/null); case "$lt" in ''|*[!0-9]*) lt=$now;; esac
      w=0; while [ $((now-lt)) -gt 5 ] && [ "$w" -lt 30 ]; do sleep 0.1; w=$((w+1))
        l2=$(tail -n 1 "${PT_TICKER:-$T/ticker.log}" 2>/dev/null); [ "$l2" = "$lt" ] || break; done
      st=$(awk -v t0="$e0" 'NR>1 && $1>=t0-1 && $1-p>5 {s+=$1-p-1} {p=$1} END{print s+0}' "${PT_TICKER:-$T/ticker.log}" 2>/dev/null) || st=0
      if [ $((el-st)) -ge "$lim" ]; then
        kill -TERM -- "-$pid" 2>/dev/null || kill -TERM "$pid" 2>/dev/null
        n=0; while kill -0 "$pid" 2>/dev/null && [ "$n" -lt 50 ]; do sleep 0.1; n=$((n+1)); done
        kill -KILL -- "-$pid" 2>/dev/null || kill -KILL "$pid" 2>/dev/null
        wait "$pid" 2>/dev/null; return 124
      fi
    fi
    n=$((n+1)); if [ "$n" -lt 40 ]; then sleep 0.05; else sleep 0.5; fi
  done
  wait "$pid"; }
finish() { # single exit path: reconcile file-recorded timeouts, print SUMMARY, exit status
  local ft; ft=$(wc -l < "$T/run-timeouts.log") || ft=999
  if [ "$ft" -gt "$RUN_TIMEOUTS" ]; then FAIL=$((FAIL + ft - RUN_TIMEOUTS))
    echo "FAIL - $((ft - RUN_TIMEOUTS)) timeout(s) occurred inside a subshell (recovered from file)"; RUN_TIMEOUTS=$ft; fi
  mark "end"; echo "# runs=$RUNS timeouts=$RUN_TIMEOUTS ticker_max_gap=$(ticker_max_gap)s stall_total=$(stall_total)s (>5 means the test host itself stalled; a stalled run is NOT a clean pass: record it)"; echo "# times (shell user/sys, children user/sys):"; times > "$T/times.txt"; sed "s/^/#   /" "$T/times.txt"
  echo "SUMMARY pass=$PASS fail=$FAIL (synthetic fixtures; NOT Mac validation)"
  [ "$FAIL" -eq 0 ] && exit 0; exit 1; }
HARNESS_BUDGET=2100   # seconds of ACTIVE time (wall time minus ticker-measured stalls, d15); exceeded -> FAIL + SUMMARY + exit 1 (never a silent stall). d16: 1500 -> 2100 (the d15f gate used ~1437 s active; module 5 adds a section). d17: unchanged (the d17 dev full run used 1398 s active including the D17 FileVault section and AP21)
mark() { local _st _act; echo "# t=${SECONDS}s runs=$RUNS $1"
  _st=$(stall_total) || _st=0; _act=$((SECONDS-_st)); [ "$_st" -le 0 ] || echo "# DIAG stall=${_st}s active=${_act}s (host paused or clock jumped: the budget counts active time)"
  if [ "$_act" -gt "$HARNESS_BUDGET" ]; then FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); echo "FAIL - harness budget ${HARNESS_BUDGET}s of ACTIVE time exceeded (wall ${SECONDS}s, stall ${_st}s) before: $1"
    echo "SUMMARY pass=$PASS fail=$FAIL (INCOMPLETE: budget exceeded; synthetic fixtures; NOT Mac validation)"; exit 1; fi
  # D10 (mutation runs only; both unset in every normal run): FAILFAST ends the run at the first mark after a FAIL;
  # STOP_AT ends it (exit 1 with a FAIL, else exit 2) at the first mark containing that text. A PARTIAL summary is never a pass.
  if [ "${WSP_HARNESS_FAILFAST:-0}" = 1 ] && [ "$FAIL" -gt 0 ]; then
    echo "SUMMARY pass=$PASS fail=$FAIL (PARTIAL: stopped at the first failing section before: $1; NOT a full run)"; exit 1; fi
  if [ -n "${WSP_HARNESS_STOP_AT:-}" ]; then case "$1" in *"$WSP_HARNESS_STOP_AT"*)
    echo "SUMMARY pass=$PASS fail=$FAIL (PARTIAL: stopped before: $1; NOT a full run)"; [ "$FAIL" -gt 0 ] && exit 1; exit 2;; esac; fi; }
mkdir -p "$T/fx0"
# Sweep: a NUL inside EVERY stub output; no tool-evidenced positive may remain.
# Control first: the same sweep on clean outputs must HAVE such positives.
# sweep writes its positives to $T/sweep.pos (no $(...) needed by callers).
SWEEP_EXPECT="fv-state gui-context mdm operator-admin operator-record sip target-os"
sweep() { : > "$T/sweep.log"
  for m in preflight accounts names firewall ssh ard filevault updates userprefs power shared apps admin-session firstlogin gates; do
    run "workstationadmin-mod-$m.txt" --mode inspect; printf '%s\n' "$OUT" >> "$T/sweep.log"; done
  run UserSpace-audit.txt; printf '%s\n' "$OUT" >> "$T/sweep.log"
  run UserSpace-preferences.txt; printf '%s\n' "$OUT" >> "$T/sweep.log"
  grep -E 'status=(ALREADY_CORRECT|CHANGED_VERIFIED)' "$T/sweep.log" | grep -E 'evidence=(effective-cli|record|stored-pref)' | sed -n 's/.*\tcheck=\([^\t]*\).*/\1/p' | sort -u > "$T/sweep.pos"
  tr '\n' ' ' < "$T/sweep.pos"; }
# Harness self-test (D7-03): a timeout inside a $(...) sweep must still give
# FAIL + SUMMARY + nonzero exit. sweep-timeout: one stub blocks (open of a FIFO
# with no writer) and is contained by the per-run timeout. sweep-clean: same
# path without the hang (control: the failure comes from the timeout).
case "${WSP_HARNESS_SELFTEST:-}" in
  sweep-timeout|sweep-clean)
    setup st; mkdir -p "$F/home/Library/Preferences"; chmod 700 "$F/home"
    if [ "$WSP_HARNESS_SELFTEST" = sweep-timeout ]; then
      mkfifo "$F/data/hang.fifo"; rm -f "$F/stubs/csrutil"
      printf '#!/bin/bash\nexec cat "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/data/hang.fifo"\n' > "$F/stubs/csrutil"; chmod 755 "$F/stubs/csrutil"
    fi
    _x=$(sweep); echo "# selftest sweep positives: $_x"; finish;;
  '') ;;
  *) echo "unknown selftest"; exit 4;;
esac
has() { printf '%s' "$OUT" | grep -q -- "$1"; }

# --- T1 fixture hook refused outside /tmp ---
mark "T1 fixture hook refused outside /tmp ---"
setup 1
OUT=$(env -i WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT=/opt/wsp-not-tmp/x /bin/bash "$F/bin/workstationadmin-mod-gates.txt" 2>&1); RC=$?
[ $RC -eq 4 ] && ok "fixture hook refused outside /tmp" || no "fixture hook outside /tmp rc=$RC"
OUT=$(env -i WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT=/usr /bin/bash "$F/bin/workstationadmin-mod-gates.txt" 2>&1); RC=$?   # d18 draft2 (SUG-J): an EXISTING folder outside /tmp
[ $RC -eq 4 ] && has "must canonically be under /tmp" && ok "T1b fixture hook refused for an existing folder outside /tmp (exit 4, canonical-path message)" || no "fixture hook /usr rc=$RC"
# --- T2 unknown option not echoed, exit 64 ---
mark "T2 unknown option not echoed, exit 64 ---"
ARGCANARY="ZZARGMARKER77"   # non-key marker: the synthetic key is never placed in argv
run UserSpace-audit.txt --bogus-"$ARGCANARY"
if [ $RC -eq 64 ] && ! grep -q "$ARGCANARY" "$F/stderr" && ! has "$ARGCANARY"; then ok "unknown option exit 64, value not echoed"; else no "unknown option rc=$RC"; fi
# --- T3 audit schema, privacy canaries, exit incomplete ---
mark "T3 audit schema, privacy canaries, exit incomplete ---"
mkdir -p "$F/home/Library/Preferences"; chmod 700 "$F/home"
echo "$CANARY_URL $CANARY_USER" > "$F/data/defaults_read_NSGlobalDomain_AppleShowScrollBars_.out"
echo "Firewall is enabled. (State = 1)" > "$F/data/socketfilterfw_--getglobalstate_.out"
run UserSpace-audit.txt
H=$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'type=header')
for f in schema=wsp-report/1 sha256= run= tz= build= arch= plan_run=not-provided apply_run=not-provided report=audit; do
  printf '%s' "$H" | grep -q "$f" && ok "header has $f" || no "header missing $f"; done
for c in "$CANARY_HOST" "$CANARY_URL" "$F/home" "$(id -un)"; do has "$c" && no "canary leaked: class $(echo "$c" | cut -c1-3)..." || ok "canary redacted"; done
[ $RC -eq 3 ] && has 'status=FAILED' && ok "audit with FAILED records exits 3" || no "audit exit $RC (want 3)"
printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=scrollbars' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' && ok "differing stored pref is UNKNOWN not FAILED/PASS" || no "scrollbars status"
printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'status=ALREADY_CORRECT.*evidence=stored-pref' && no "stored-pref certified" || ok "no stored-pref certified as correct"
run workstationadmin-mod-gates.txt --mode inspect
[ $RC -eq 2 ] && ! has 'status=FAILED' && ok "pending-only module exits 2 (incomplete, not complete)" || no "gates exit $RC (want 2)"
has 'complete=no' && ok "module-end says complete=no" || no "complete flag"

  setup "15-$m"; : > "$F/calls.log"; run "workstationadmin-mod-$m.txt" --mode apply
  if printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'check=apply-disabled' || printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'check=apply-gate'; then g=1; else g=0; fi
  grep -Eq '^(defaults write|installer|ditto|cp|launchctl|pkgutil --install|softwareupdate -i|killall)' "$F/calls.log" && w=1 || w=0
  [ $g -eq 1 ] && [ $w -eq 0 ] && ok "disabled $m: apply refused/disabled, no mutating call" || no "disabled $m g=$g w=$w"
done
# --- D4-01: whole-output single-line contract (version, build, GUID) ---
mark "D4-01: whole-output single-line contract (version, build, GU"
for v in '15.5\nUNEXPECTED\n' 'UNEXPECTED\n15.5\n' '15.5\n15.5\n' '15.5\n\n' '15.5\r\n' '\n15.5\n' '' '15..5\n'; do

  run workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt --mode inspect
  printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=target-os' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' && ok "preflight: malformed version -> UNKNOWN" || no "preflight accepted malformed version"
  mkdir -p "$F/home/Library/Preferences"; chmod 700 "$F/home"; run UserSpace-audit.txt
  [ $RC -eq 4 ] && ok "audit refuses malformed version output" || no "audit accepted malformed version rc=$RC"
  rm -rf "$T/fx41"
done

# --- D5-01: raw capture framing; tool stdout can never act as a control record ---
mark "D5-01: raw capture framing; tool stdout can never act as a c"
# fx PATH VALUE-BYTES [RC]: replace one stub output (printf %b bytes) and rc.
fx() { printf '%b' "$2" > "$F/data/$1.out"; if [ -n "${3:-}" ]; then echo "$3" > "$F/data/$1.rc"; else rm -f "$F/data/$1.rc"; fi; }
fld() { printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "type=$1" | head -1 | tr '\t' '\n' | sed -n "s/^$2=//p"; }
# BAD V: marker-looking lines at every position, several markers, same-line
# junk, ordinary malformed framing, and a plausible value with nonzero exit.
badset() { V="$1"
  printf '%s\n' "$V\\nWSPCLASS:none\\n" "WSPCLASS:none\\n$V\\n" "$V\\nWSPRC:0\\n" "WSPRC:0\\n$V\\n" \
   "$V\\nWSPOCT:\\n" "WSPOCT:061\\n$V\\n" "WSPRC:0\\nWSPCLASS:none\\n$V\\n" "$V\\nWSPRC:0\\nWSPCLASS:none\\n" \
   "WSPOCT:\\n$V\\nWSPRC:0\\nWSPCLASS:none\\n" "${V}WSPCLASS:none\\n" "${V}\\nWSPCLASS:none" \
   "$V" "\\n" "" "\\r\\n" "$V\\r\\n" "$V junk\\n" "\\n$V\\n" "$V\\n\\n" "$V\\n$V\\n" "RC1:$V\\n" "${V}\\0\\n" "${V}\\0x\\n"; }
d51() { # NAME KEY VALUE CHECKFN
  local b rc
  badset "$3" > "$T/badset.txt"
  while IFS= read -r b; do   # current shell: RUNS/FAIL counters stay accurate
    rc=""; case "$b" in RC1:*) b=${b#RC1:}; rc=1;; esac
    setup 61; fx "$2" "$b" "$rc"; "$4" "$1 [$b rc=${rc:-0}]" > "$T/d51.one"; rm -rf "$T/fx61"
    r=$(cat "$T/d51.one"); printf '%s\n' "$r" >> "$T/d51.log"; echo "# t=${SECONDS}s runs=$RUNS d51 ${r%% *} $1"
  done < "$T/badset.txt"
}
# shellcheck disable=SC2329 # chk_* are invoked indirectly as "chk_$x"
chk_ver() {
  run workstationadmin-mod-firewall.txt --mode apply
  printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'check=apply-gate' && ! grep -q '^socketfilterfw --setglobalstate' "$F/calls.log" || { echo "FAIL gate $1"; return; }
  [ "$(fld module-start os)" = unknown ] || { echo "FAIL module-start $1"; return; }
  run workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt --mode inspect
  printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=target-os' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' || { echo "FAIL preflight $1"; return; }
  mkdir -p "$F/home/Library/Preferences"; chmod 700 "$F/home"; run UserSpace-audit.txt
  [ "$RC" -eq 4 ] && [ "$(fld header os)" = unknown ] || { echo "FAIL audit/header $1"; return; }
  echo "PASS $1"; }
# shellcheck disable=SC2329 # invoked indirectly
chk_build() {
  run workstationadmin-mod-firewall.txt --mode apply
  printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'check=apply-gate' && ! grep -q '^socketfilterfw --setglobalstate' "$F/calls.log" || { echo "FAIL gate $1"; return; }
  [ "$(fld module-start build)" = unknown ] || { echo "FAIL module-start $1"; return; }
  mkdir -p "$F/home/Library/Preferences"; chmod 700 "$F/home"; run UserSpace-audit.txt
  [ "$(fld header build)" = unknown ] || { echo "FAIL header $1"; return; }
  run workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt --mode inspect
  printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=target-os' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' || { echo "FAIL preflight $1"; return; }
  echo "PASS $1"; }
# shellcheck disable=SC2329 # invoked indirectly
chk_arch() {
  run workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt --mode inspect
  [ "$(fld module-start arch)" = unknown ] || { echo "FAIL module-start $1"; return; }
  printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=architecture' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' || { echo "FAIL preflight $1"; return; }
  run workstationadmin-mod-filevault.txt --mode inspect
  printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=volume-owner' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' || { echo "FAIL filevault $1"; return; }
  mkdir -p "$F/home/Library/Preferences"; chmod 700 "$F/home"; run UserSpace-audit.txt
  [ "$(fld header arch)" = unknown ] || { echo "FAIL header $1"; return; }
  echo "PASS $1"; }
G='GeneratedUID: ABCDEF01-2345-6789-ABCD-EF0123456789'

  printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=ssh-allowlist' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' && echo "PASS $1" || echo "FAIL ssh $1"; }
: > "$T/d51.log"
d51 version sw_vers_-productVersion_ 15.5 chk_ver

[ "$n" -eq 92 ] && [ "$f" -eq 0 ] && ok "D5-01/D6-02: 92 malformed/marker/NUL/nonzero cases rejected (version, build, arch, guid x 23)" || { no "D5-01 pass=$n fail=$f"; grep '^FAIL' "$T/d51.log" | head -20; }
# valid controls: exact single line accepted everywhere and reported as metadata
setup 62; fx sw_vers_-productVersion_ '15.5\n'; fx uname_-m_ 'arm64\n'
run workstationadmin-mod-firewall.txt --mode apply
{ ! printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'check=apply-gate'; } && [ "$(fld module-start os)" = 15.5 ] && [ "$(fld module-start build)" = 24F74 ] && [ "$(fld module-start arch)" = arm64 ] && ok "valid version/build/arch accepted by gate and reported in module-start" || no "valid control rejected: $(fld module-start os)/$(fld module-start build)/$(fld module-start arch)"
run workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt --mode inspect
printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=target-os' | grep -q 'status=ALREADY_CORRECT' && printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=architecture' | grep -q 'status=ALREADY_CORRECT' && ok "preflight accepts valid single-line facts" || no "preflight valid control"
mkdir -p "$F/home/Library/Preferences"; chmod 700 "$F/home"; run UserSpace-audit.txt
[ $RC -ne 4 ] && [ "$(fld header os)" = 15.5 ] && [ "$(fld header build)" = 24F74 ] && [ "$(fld header arch)" = arm64 ] && ok "audit accepts valid baseline; header reports validated os/build/arch" || no "audit valid control rc=$RC $(fld header os)/$(fld header build)/$(fld header arch)"
fx dscl_._-read__Users_workstationadmin_GeneratedUID_ "$G\\n"; ssh_data; run workstationadmin-mod-ssh.txt --mode inspect
printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=ssh-allowlist' | grep -q 'status=PENDING_CONSENT' && ok "ssh accepts one valid GUID line" || no "ssh valid GUID control"
# a non-scalar consumer still sees marker-looking lines verbatim (nothing stripped)
fx dscl_._-read__Groups_com.apple.access_ssh_GroupMembership_ 'GroupMembership: workstationadmin\nWSPRC:0\n'
run workstationadmin-mod-ssh.txt --mode inspect
if printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=ssh-allowlist' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' && ! printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=ssh-allowlist' | grep -Eq 'status=(ALREADY_CORRECT|CHANGED_VERIFIED|PENDING_CONSENT)'; then ok "marker-looking line in list output preserved -> allowlist UNKNOWN, no positive"; else no "list marker: $(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=ssh-allowlist' | tr '\t' ' ' | cut -c1-200)"; fi
rm -rf "$T/fx62"
# --- D6-01: capture transform failures fail closed (od/tee/wc injected) ---
mark "D6-01: capture transform failures fail closed (od/tee/wc inj"
# Pass-through wrappers; misbehave only when data/<tool>.mode exists.
mkwrap() { cat > "$F/stubs/$1" <<W
#!/bin/bash
m=\$(cat "\$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/data/$1.mode" 2>/dev/null)
case "\$m" in
$2
  *) exec /usr/bin/$1 "\$@";;
esac
W
chmod 755 "$F/stubs/$1"; }
# D9 (S2): the injection applies only when the wrapped stage carries the VERSION fixture
# (its padded second line), so identity/console captures pass through untouched and the
# fault really reaches the version reader. Every other stage is a plain pass-through.
mkwrap_s() { cat > "$F/stubs/$1" <<W
#!/bin/bash
m=\$(cat "\$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/data/$1.mode" 2>/dev/null)
b=\$(mktemp "\$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/.wrapin.XXXXXX") || exit 99
/usr/bin/cat > "\$b"
case "\$(/usr/bin/head -c 4096 "\$b")" in *UNEXPECTED-TRAILING-LINE-PADDING*) ;; *) m="";; esac
exec < "\$b"; rm -f "\$b"
case "\$m" in
$2
  *) exec /usr/bin/$1 "\$@";;
esac
W
chmod 755 "$F/stubs/$1"; }
d61() { # TOOL MODE expectation: version read must fail closed everywhere
  setup 63
  printf '15.5\nUNEXPECTED-TRAILING-LINE-PADDING\n' > "$F/data/sw_vers_-productVersion_.out"
  mkwrap_s od '  prefix-rc1) head -c 5 | /usr/bin/od "$@"; exit 1;;
  notrailer) /usr/bin/od "$@" | sed '"'"'$d'"'"';;
  prefix-rc0) head -c 5 | /usr/bin/od "$@";;
  dropline) /usr/bin/od "$@" | sed 2d;;
  range) /usr/bin/od "$@" | sed '"'"'1s/ 061/ 461/'"'"';;
  junk) /usr/bin/od "$@"; echo "WSPRC:0 0 0 0";;'
  mkwrap_s tee '  rc1) /usr/bin/tee "$@"; exit 1;;
  split) /usr/bin/tee "$@" | head -c 5;;'
  mkwrap_s wc '  lie) /usr/bin/wc "$@" >/dev/null; echo 5;;
  rc1) /usr/bin/wc "$@"; exit 1;;'
  mkwrap_s head '  rc1) /usr/bin/head "$@"; exit 1;;'
  echo "$2" > "$F/data/$1.mode"
  run workstationadmin-mod-firewall.txt --mode apply
  # d8: identity is now also read through the capture, so a broken transform
  # may refuse BEFORE the report opens (REFUSED, exit 4, no writes). Counted
  # separately as PASS-REFUSED: the version-reader path was not reached then.
  if [ "$RC" -eq 4 ] && grep -q REFUSED "$F/stderr"; then echo "FAIL refused-before-version-reader $1/$2"
  elif printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'check=apply-gate' && ! grep -q '^socketfilterfw --setglobalstate' "$F/calls.log" && [ "$(fld module-start os)" = unknown ]; then
    run workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt --mode inspect
    printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=target-os' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' && echo "PASS $1/$2" || echo "FAIL preflight $1/$2"
  else echo "FAIL gate $1/$2"; fi
  rm -rf "$T/fx63"; }
: > "$T/d61.log"
for m in prefix-rc1 notrailer prefix-rc0 dropline range junk; do d61 od $m >> "$T/d61.log"; mark "d61 od $m"; done
for m in rc1 split; do d61 tee $m >> "$T/d61.log"; done
for m in lie rc1; do d61 wc $m >> "$T/d61.log"; done
d61 head rc1 >> "$T/d61.log"
n=$(grep -c '^PASS' "$T/d61.log"); f=$(grep -c '^FAIL' "$T/d61.log"); nr=$(grep -c '^PASS-REFUSED' "$T/d61.log")
[ "$n" -eq 11 ] && [ "$f" -eq 0 ] && [ "$nr" -eq 0 ] && ok "D6-01: 11 od/tee/wc/head injections scoped to the version capture all reach the version reader and fail closed (none refused earlier by the identity guard)" || { no "D6-01 pass=$n fail=$f"; grep '^FAIL' "$T/d61.log"; }
# wrappers in pass-through mode change nothing (control)
setup 64; printf '15.5\n' > "$F/data/sw_vers_-productVersion_.out"
mkwrap od '  never) :;;'; mkwrap tee '  never) :;;'; mkwrap wc '  never) :;;'
run workstationadmin-mod-firewall.txt --mode apply
{ ! printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'check=apply-gate'; } && [ "$(fld module-start os)" = 15.5 ] && ok "pass-through transform wrappers: valid capture still accepted" || no "pass-through control"
# bounded capture: >1 MiB output fails closed, exactly 1 MiB of text is accepted
head -c 1048577 /dev/zero | tr '\0' 'a' > "$F/data/sw_vers_-productVersion_.out"
run workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt --mode inspect
printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=target-os' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' && ok "over-limit output -> UNKNOWN (no prefix accepted)" || no "over-limit"
{ head -c 1048570 /dev/zero | tr '\0' 'a'; printf '\n15.5\n'; } > "$F/data/sw_vers_-productVersion_.out"
run workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt --mode inspect
printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=target-os' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' && ok "large multi-line output rejected by version reader" || no "large multiline"
rm -rf "$T/fx64"

mark "D6-02: NUL in otherwise-positive outputs, every positive fac"

rm -rf "$T/fx65"; setup 65; nulfx profiles_status_-type_enrollment_ 'Enrolled via DEP: No\nMDM enrollment: N\0o\n'

printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=operator-admin' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' && ok "admin membership with NUL -> preflight UNKNOWN" || no "admin NUL preflight"

printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=global' | grep -q 'status=ALREADY_CORRECT' || echo "Firewall is enabled. (State = 1)" > "$F/data/socketfilterfw_--getglobalstate_.out"

mkdir -p "$F/home/Library/Preferences"; chmod 700 "$F/home"; run UserSpace-audit.txt
