run workstationadmin-mod-filevault.txt --mode inspect
printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=fv-state' | grep -q 'status=ALREADY_CORRECT' && ok "FileVault control: clean On is ALREADY_CORRECT" || no "FV control"
nulfx fdesetup_status_ 'FileVault is O\0n.\n'
run workstationadmin-mod-filevault.txt --mode inspect
printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=fv-state' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' && ok "FileVault On with NUL -> UNKNOWN" || no "FV NUL"
rm -rf "$T/fx65"
setup 67; mkdir -p "$F/home/Library/Preferences"; chmod 700 "$F/home"; sweep >/dev/null
miss=""; for c in $SWEEP_EXPECT; do grep -qx "$c" "$T/sweep.pos" || miss="$miss $c"; done
[ -z "$miss" ] && ok "NUL sweep control: clean outputs give the expected tool-evidenced positives ($(tr '\n' ' ' < "$T/sweep.pos"))" || no "NUL sweep control missing:$miss"
rm -rf "$T/fx67"
setup 66; mkdir -p "$F/home/Library/Preferences"; chmod 700 "$F/home"
for x in "$F"/data/*.out; do { head -c 1 "$x"; printf '\0'; tail -c +2 "$x"; } > "$x.n" && mv "$x.n" "$x"; done
sweep >/dev/null; pos=$(tr '\n' ' ' < "$T/sweep.pos")
[ ! -s "$T/sweep.pos" ] && ok "NUL sweep: no tool-evidenced ALREADY_CORRECT/CHANGED_VERIFIED in any module or audit" || no "NUL sweep positives: $pos"
rm -rf "$T/fx66"
# --- D10: SOFTWARE UPDATES writer (STEP 2, module 1) -----------------------------------------
# One writer restored: defaults write -bool on six documented keys, as root, after a checked
# backup, with typed read-back. Policy decides the values; nothing installs or restarts.
mark "D10 updates writer"
SU_D=/Library/Preferences/com.apple.SoftwareUpdate; CM_D=/Library/Preferences/com.apple.commerce
UKEYS="AutomaticCheckEnabled AutomaticDownload ConfigDataInstall CriticalUpdateInstall AutoInstallOSUpdates AutoUpdate"   # check labels
TAB=$(printf '\t')
mkdefs() { rm -f "$F/stubs/defaults"; cat > "$F/stubs/defaults" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; d="$r/data"
printf 'defaults %s %s\n' "${1:-}" "${2:-}" >> "$r/calls.log"
f="$d/defs/$(printf '%s__%s' "${2:-}" "${3:-}" | tr -c 'A-Za-z0-9._-' '_')"
case "${1:-}" in
  write) printf 'write %s %s %s %s\n' "${2:-}" "${3:-}" "${4:-}" "${5:-}" >> "$r/writes.log"
    [ -f "$d/defaults.write.rc" ] && exit "$(cat "$d/defaults.write.rc")"
    [ -f "$d/defaults.write.noop" ] && exit 0
    [ "${4:-}" = -bool ] || exit 1
    case "${5:-}" in true) v=1;; false) v=0;; *) exit 1;; esac
    echo "$v" > "$f"
    if [ "${3:-}" = AutomaticCheckEnabled ] && [ ! -f "$d/sched.pin" ]; then
      if [ "$v" = 1 ]; then echo "Automatic check is on"; else echo "Automatic check is off"; fi > "$d/softwareupdate_--schedule_.out"; fi
    exit 0;;
  read) m=$(cat "$d/defaults.read.mode" 2>/dev/null)
    case "$m" in
      rc1) exit 1;; empty) exit 0;; junk) echo garbage; exit 0;; twoline) printf '1\n1\n'; exit 0;;
      nul) printf '1\0\n'; exit 0;; trunc) printf '1'; exit 1;; lie) echo 1; exit 0;;
    esac
    if [ -f "$f" ]; then cat "$f"; exit 0; fi
    echo "The domain/default pair of (${2:-}, ${3:-}) does not exist" >&2; exit 1;;
esac
exit 1
W
chmod 755 "$F/stubs/defaults"; mkdir -p "$F/data/defs"; }
defseed() { printf '%s\n' "$3" > "$F/data/defs/$(printf '%s__%s' "$1" "$2" | tr -c 'A-Za-z0-9._-' '_')"; }
upd_fix() { # TAG: fresh fixture, every key at the WRONG value, plists present, no profiles, schedule off
  setup "u$1"; mkdefs
  mkdir -p "$F/Library/Preferences"; printf 'PLIST-SU-ORIG\n' > "$F/Library/Preferences/com.apple.SoftwareUpdate.plist"; printf 'PLIST-CM-ORIG\n' > "$F/Library/Preferences/com.apple.commerce.plist"
  defseed $SU_D AutomaticCheckEnabled 0; defseed $SU_D AutomaticDownload 0; defseed $SU_D ConfigDataInstall 0; defseed $SU_D CriticalUpdateInstall 0
  defseed $SU_D AutomaticallyInstallMacOSUpdates 1; defseed $CM_D AutoUpdate 0
  echo "Automatic check is off" > "$F/data/softwareupdate_--schedule_.out"
  printf 'There are no configuration profiles installed\n' > "$F/data/profiles_show_-type_configuration_.out"
  : > "$F/writes.log"; }
ust() { printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${TAB}module=updates${TAB}.*${TAB}check=$1${TAB}" | head -1 | sed -n 's/.*status=\([A-Z_]*\).*/\1/p'; }
ustall() { local k o=""; for k in $UKEYS; do o="$o$(ust "$k") "; done; printf '%s' "${o% }"; }
nwrites() { wc -l < "$F/writes.log"; }
nbk() { ls "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/backup" 2>/dev/null | wc -l; }
SIX() { local o="" i; for i in 1 2 3 4 5 6; do o="$o $1"; done; printf '%s' "${o# }"; }
# U1 dry run (default and explicit inspect): PROPOSED, zero writes, no backup, files untouched
upd_fix 1; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt
s1=$(ustall); w1=$(nwrites); b1=$(nbk)
{ [ "$s1" = "$(SIX PROPOSED)" ] && [ "$w1" -eq 0 ] && [ "$b1" -eq 0 ] && ! grep -q 'defaults write' "$F/calls.log" && grep -q 'PLIST-SU-ORIG' "$F/Library/Preferences/com.apple.SoftwareUpdate.plist" && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ]; } \
  && ok "D10 dry run (default mode): 6 PROPOSED, no defaults write, no backup, plist untouched, exit 2" || no "D10 dry run: [$s1] writes=$w1 backups=$b1 rc=$RC"
# U2 apply: 6 CHANGED_VERIFIED through the typed reader, -bool writes with policy values, checked backup of both plists
run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply
s2=$(ustall); w2=$(nwrites); b2=$(nbk)
wl=$(LC_ALL=C sort "$F/writes.log" | tr '\n' ';')
exp="write $SU_D AutomaticCheckEnabled -bool true;write $SU_D AutomaticDownload -bool true;write $SU_D AutomaticallyInstallMacOSUpdates -bool false;write $SU_D ConfigDataInstall -bool true;write $SU_D CriticalUpdateInstall -bool true;write $CM_D AutoUpdate -bool true;"
{ [ "$s2" = "$(SIX CHANGED_VERIFIED)" ] && [ "$wl" = "$exp" ] && [ "$b2" -ge 1 ] && [ "$(grep -rl 'PLIST-SU-ORIG' "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/backup" | wc -l)" -ge 1 ] && [ "$(grep -rl 'PLIST-CM-ORIG' "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/backup" | wc -l)" -ge 1 ] && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ] && ! printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'status=FAILED'; } \
  && ok "D10 apply: 6 CHANGED_VERIFIED; exactly the 6 policy-driven -bool writes (R16: check/download/config-data/critical on, macOS auto-install off, App Store on); both plists backed up first" || no "D10 apply: [$s2] writes=[$wl] backups=$b2 rc=$RC"
printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${TAB}check=AutomaticCheckEnabled${TAB}" | grep -q 'evidence=effective-cli' && ok "D10 apply: AutomaticCheckEnabled verified against 'softwareupdate --schedule' (effective-cli)" || no "D10 apply: schedule cross-check evidence missing"
# U3 idempotent re-apply: ALREADY_CORRECT, no new write, no new backup
run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply
s3=$(ustall); w3=$(nwrites); b3=$(nbk)
{ [ "$s3" = "$(SIX ALREADY_CORRECT)" ] && [ "$w3" -eq "$w2" ] && [ "$b3" -eq "$b2" ]; } && ok "D10 idempotent re-apply: 6 ALREADY_CORRECT, no write, no new backup" || no "D10 re-apply: [$s3] writes $w2->$w3 backups $b2->$b3"
# U4 verify: clean after apply; a drifted value is FAILED (not PROPOSED)
run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode verify
s4=$(ustall); w4=$(nwrites)
defseed $SU_D AutomaticDownload 0; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode verify
s4b=$(ust AutomaticDownload)
{ [ "$s4" = "$(SIX ALREADY_CORRECT)" ] && [ "$s4b" = FAILED ] && [ "$RC" -eq 3 ] && [ "$(nwrites)" -eq "$w4" ]; } && ok "D10 verify: clean after apply; a drifted key is FAILED (exit 3); verify never writes" || no "D10 verify: clean=[$s4] drift=$s4b rc=$RC"
rm -rf "$T/fxu1"
# U5 read-back mismatch / write failure -> FAILED, never CHANGED_VERIFIED
upd_fix 5; : > "$F/data/defaults.write.noop"; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply
s5=$(ustall)
upd_fix 5b; echo 1 > "$F/data/defaults.write.rc"; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply
s5b=$(ustall); rc5b=$RC
{ [ "$s5" = "$(SIX FAILED)" ] && [ "$s5b" = "$(SIX FAILED)" ] && [ "$rc5b" -eq 3 ]; } && ok "D10 write that does not stick (read-back mismatch) or fails -> FAILED x6, never CHANGED_VERIFIED, exit 3" || no "D10 readback mismatch: noop=[$s5] rc1=[$s5b] rc=$rc5b"
upd_fix 5c; echo lie > "$F/data/defaults.read.mode"; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply
{ [ "$(ust AutoInstallOSUpdates)" = FAILED ] && [ "$(ust AutomaticCheckEnabled)" = FAILED ]; } && ok "D10 a reader that claims 'on' after every write: keys wanted 'off' are FAILED, and the check key contradicts the schedule reader -> FAILED (a consistent liar is a documented limit)" || no "D10 lying reader: macos=$(ust AutoInstallOSUpdates) check=$(ust AutomaticCheckEnabled)"
rm -rf "$T/fxu5" "$T/fxu5b" "$T/fxu5c"
# U6 schedule cross-check: contradiction -> FAILED; unreadable -> UNKNOWN; both never CHANGED_VERIFIED
upd_fix 6; echo "Automatic check is off" > "$F/data/softwareupdate_--schedule_.out"; : > "$F/data/sched.pin"; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply
c6=$(ust AutomaticCheckEnabled); o6=$(ust AutomaticDownload)
upd_fix 6b; : > "$F/data/sched.pin"; printf 'garbage\n' > "$F/data/softwareupdate_--schedule_.out"; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply
c6b=$(ust AutomaticCheckEnabled)
{ [ "$c6" = FAILED ] && [ "$o6" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ "$c6b" = UNKNOWN ]; } && ok "D10 stored value written but 'softwareupdate --schedule' disagrees -> FAILED; unreadable -> UNKNOWN (other keys unaffected)" || no "D10 schedule: contradict=$c6 other=$o6 garbage=$c6b"
rm -rf "$T/fxu6" "$T/fxu6b"
# U7 policy: unset, malformed, injection characters, macOS auto-install other than off -> BLOCKED_POLICY, nothing written for that key
upd_fix 7; printf 'TARGET_OS_MAJOR=15\nALLOWED_BUILDS=24F74\n' > "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/policy.conf"
run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply; s7=$(ustall); w7=$(nwrites)
{ [ "$s7" = "$(SIX BLOCKED_POLICY)" ] && [ "$w7" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "D10 no UPDATES_* policy: 6 BLOCKED_POLICY, zero writes (no built-in defaults)" || no "D10 policy unset: [$s7] writes=$w7"
upd_fix 7b; printf 'TARGET_OS_MAJOR=15\nALLOWED_BUILDS=24F74\nUPDATES_AUTO_CHECK=maybe\nUPDATES_AUTO_DOWNLOAD=ON\nUPDATES_CONFIG_DATA=on;\nUPDATES_CRITICAL=\nUPDATES_MACOS_INSTALL=on\nUPDATES_APP_STORE=on $(x)\n' > "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/policy.conf"
run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply; s7b=$(ustall); w7b=$(nwrites)
{ [ "$s7b" = "$(SIX BLOCKED_POLICY)" ] && [ "$w7b" -eq 0 ] && ! printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'maybe'; } && ok "D10 malformed policy (word, case, ';', empty, macOS auto-install on, command text): 6 BLOCKED_POLICY, zero writes, values not echoed" || no "D10 malformed policy: [$s7b] writes=$w7b"
# D11 (B2, owner decision): the five security-update settings ALWAYS stay on: policy "off" is BLOCKED_POLICY with zero
# writes; UPDATES_MACOS_INSTALL accepts only "off". Every key is already at its required value, so a wrongly accepted
# value would show up as a write (apply mode) and a PROPOSED/CHANGED status.
mkpol() { # KEY VALUE: a valid policy with exactly one UPDATES_* key overridden
  local k v; { printf 'TARGET_OS_MAJOR=15\nALLOWED_BUILDS=24F74\n'
  for k in UPDATES_AUTO_CHECK UPDATES_AUTO_DOWNLOAD UPDATES_CONFIG_DATA UPDATES_CRITICAL UPDATES_MACOS_INSTALL UPDATES_APP_STORE; do
    case "$k" in UPDATES_MACOS_INSTALL) v=off;; *) v=on;; esac; [ "$k" = "$1" ] && v=$2; printf '%s=%s\n' "$k" "$v"; done; } > "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/policy.conf"; }
upd_right() { upd_fix "$1"; defseed $SU_D AutomaticCheckEnabled 1; defseed $SU_D AutomaticDownload 1; defseed $SU_D ConfigDataInstall 1; defseed $SU_D CriticalUpdateInstall 1
  defseed $SU_D AutomaticallyInstallMacOSUpdates 0; defseed $CM_D AutoUpdate 1; echo "Automatic check is on" > "$F/data/softwareupdate_--schedule_.out"; }
b2bad=""; b2n=0
for ent in UPDATES_AUTO_CHECK:AutomaticCheckEnabled:off UPDATES_AUTO_DOWNLOAD:AutomaticDownload:off UPDATES_CONFIG_DATA:ConfigDataInstall:off UPDATES_CRITICAL:CriticalUpdateInstall:off UPDATES_APP_STORE:AutoUpdate:off UPDATES_MACOS_INSTALL:AutoInstallOSUpdates:on; do
  pk=${ent%%:*}; r=${ent#*:}; lb=${r%%:*}; bv=${r#*:}
  upd_right 7c; mkpol "$pk" "$bv"; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply; b2n=$((b2n+1))
  st=$(ust "$lb"); oth=0; for k in $UKEYS; do [ "$k" = "$lb" ] && continue; [ "$(ust "$k")" = ALREADY_CORRECT ] && oth=$((oth+1)); done
  { [ "$st" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$oth" -eq 5 ] && [ "$(nwrites)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(nbk)" -eq 0 ]; } || b2bad="$b2bad $pk=$bv:[$st] others=$oth writes=$(nwrites)"
  rm -rf "$T/fxu7c"
done
[ -z "$b2bad" ] && ok "D11 B2 (owner decision): 'off' for check, download, config-data, critical and App Store -> BLOCKED_POLICY, zero writes, no backup; UPDATES_MACOS_INSTALL 'on' -> BLOCKED_POLICY, zero writes ($b2n cases, the other five keys stay ALREADY_CORRECT)" || no "D11 B2 on-only rule:$b2bad"
upd_right 7d; mkpol UPDATES_AUTO_CHECK on; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply
{ [ "$(ustall)" = "$(SIX ALREADY_CORRECT)" ] && [ "$(nwrites)" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "D11 B2 control: the required values (five 'on', macOS install 'off') on correct keys -> 6 ALREADY_CORRECT, zero writes" || no "D11 B2 control: [$(ustall)]"
rm -rf "$T/fxu7d"
# U8 (D11, B3) management state, with the real "profiles show" listing grammar (per-payload "payload[N] type = ..." lines,
# issuer-chosen UUID identifiers): a payload of a controlled domain or any MDM enrollment -> BLOCKED_POLICY; anything that is
# not positively the empty listing with no enrollment -> UNKNOWN; in every case zero writes. Grammar = Mac-confirmation item.
pfx() { # PAYLOADTYPE: a one-profile listing in the per-payload grammar
  printf '_computerlevel[1] attribute: profileIdentifier: 3F2A1C9E-5B7D-4C1E-9A60-0D2E4B6F8A10\n_computerlevel[1] attribute: name: Example Policy\n_computerlevel[1] attribute: organization: Example Org\n_computerlevel[1] payload[1] type = %s\n_computerlevel[1] payload[1] attribute: name: Example Payload\n' "$1"; }
u8bad=""; u8n=0
for pt in com.apple.SoftwareUpdate com.apple.commerce com.apple.appstore com.apple.applicationaccess com.apple.ManagedClient.preferences; do
  upd_fix 8; pfx "$pt" > "$F/data/profiles_show_-type_configuration_.out"
  run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply; u8n=$((u8n+1))
  { [ "$(ustall)" = "$(SIX BLOCKED_POLICY)" ] && [ "$(nwrites)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(nbk)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(ust management-overrides)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ]; } || u8bad="$u8bad $pt:[$(ustall)]w=$(nwrites)"
  rm -rf "$T/fxu8"
done
[ -z "$u8bad" ] && ok "D11 B3: a profile with a SoftwareUpdate, commerce, appstore, applicationaccess or ManagedClient payload -> 6 BLOCKED_POLICY + management-overrides BLOCKED_POLICY, zero writes, no backup ($u8n payload types, real listing grammar)" || no "D11 managed payloads:$u8bad"
upd_fix 8e; printf 'There are no configuration profiles installed\n' > "$F/data/profiles_show_-type_configuration_.out"; printf 'Enrolled via DEP: No\nMDM enrollment: Yes (User Approved)\n' > "$F/data/profiles_status_-type_enrollment_.out"
run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply; s8e=$(ustall)
{ [ "$s8e" = "$(SIX BLOCKED_POLICY)" ] && [ "$(nwrites)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(ust management-overrides)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ]; } && ok "D11 B3: MDM-enrolled Mac with an empty profile listing (a DDM-only policy is invisible there) -> 6 BLOCKED_POLICY, zero writes" || no "D11 MDM enrolled: [$s8e] writes=$(nwrites)"
upd_fix 8f; echo 1 > "$F/data/profiles_status_-type_enrollment_.rc"
run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply; s8f=$(ustall)
{ [ "$s8f" = "$(SIX UNKNOWN)" ] && printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'check=apply-gate' && [ "$(ust management-overrides)" = UNKNOWN ]; } && [ "$(nwrites)" -eq 0 ] && ok "D11 B3 (d12: tightened, only UNKNOWN is right): enrollment state unreadable -> the gate drops apply to inspect, 6 UNKNOWN + management-overrides UNKNOWN, nothing is written ([$s8f])" || no "D11 enrollment unreadable: [$s8f] writes=$(nwrites)"
upd_fix 8g; echo 1 > "$F/data/profiles_status_-type_enrollment_.rc"; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode inspect
{ [ "$(ustall)" = "$(SIX UNKNOWN)" ] && [ "$(ust management-overrides)" = UNKNOWN ]; } && ok "D11 B3: enrollment unreadable in inspect mode -> 6 UNKNOWN + management-overrides UNKNOWN (never 'none')" || no "D11 enrollment unreadable inspect: [$(ustall)]"
upd_fix 8b; echo 1 > "$F/data/profiles_show_-type_configuration_.rc"; rm -f "$F/data/profiles_show_-type_configuration_.out"
run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply; s8c=$(ustall); w8c=$(nwrites)
{ [ "$s8c" = "$(SIX UNKNOWN)" ] && [ "$w8c" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(ust management-overrides)" = UNKNOWN ]; } && ok "D10 profile state unreadable: 6 UNKNOWN, zero writes" || no "D10 profile unreadable: [$s8c] writes=$w8c"
u8bad=""
for txt in 'garbage' 'There is 1 configuration profile installed' 'There are no configuration profiles installed for this user' "$(pfx org.example.wifi)"; do
  upd_fix 8c; printf '%s\n' "$txt" > "$F/data/profiles_show_-type_configuration_.out"
  run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply
  { [ "$(ustall)" = "$(SIX UNKNOWN)" ] && [ "$(nwrites)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(ust management-overrides)" = UNKNOWN ]; } || u8bad="$u8bad [$(ustall)]w=$(nwrites)"
  rm -rf "$T/fxu8c"
done
[ -z "$u8bad" ] && ok "D11 B3: unrecognised output, a near-miss sentence and a listing with only unrelated profiles are UNKNOWN (not 'none'), zero writes" || no "D11 unclassified profile text:$u8bad"
upd_fix 8h; printf 'There are no configuration profiles installed in the system domain\n' > "$F/data/profiles_show_-type_configuration_.out"; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode inspect
{ [ "$(ust management-overrides)" = ALREADY_CORRECT ] && [ "$(ustall)" = "$(SIX PROPOSED)" ]; } && ok "D11 B3: only the explicit empty-listing sentences (with enrollment No) give 'none'" || no "D11 empty listing: mgmt=$(ust management-overrides) [$(ustall)]"
upd_fix 8i; printf 'Enrolled via DEP: Yes\nMDM enrollment: No\n' > "$F/data/profiles_status_-type_enrollment_.out"; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply
{ [ "$(ustall)" = "$(SIX BLOCKED_POLICY)" ] && [ "$(nwrites)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(nbk)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(ust management-overrides)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ]; } && ok "d12 S-g: 'Enrolled via DEP: Yes' with 'MDM enrollment: No' and an empty listing is treated as managed -> 6 BLOCKED_POLICY, zero writes, no backup" || no "d12 DEP yes: [$(ustall)] w=$(nwrites)"
upd_fix 8j; printf 'Enrolled via DEP: Maybe\nMDM enrollment: No\n' > "$F/data/profiles_status_-type_enrollment_.out"; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply
{ [ "$(ustall)" = "$(SIX UNKNOWN)" ] && [ "$(nwrites)" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "d12 S-g: an unparseable 'Enrolled via DEP' value is UNKNOWN, zero writes" || no "d12 DEP odd: [$(ustall)] w=$(nwrites)"
upd_fix 8k; printf 'Enrolled via DEP: No\nMDM enrollment: No\n' > "$F/data/profiles_status_-type_enrollment_.out"; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode inspect
[ "$(ust management-overrides)" = ALREADY_CORRECT ] && ok "d12 S-g control: 'Enrolled via DEP: No' + 'MDM enrollment: No' + empty listing still gives 'none'" || no "d12 DEP no: mgmt=$(ust management-overrides)"
rm -rf "$T/fxu8" "$T/fxu8b" "$T/fxu8e" "$T/fxu8f" "$T/fxu8g" "$T/fxu8h" "$T/fxu8c" "$T/fxu8i" "$T/fxu8j" "$T/fxu8k"
# U9 (D11, B1) the backup is verified and symlink-safe. Any refusal -> the keys of that plist FAILED, nothing written to it;
# the other plist is unaffected (the App Store key still proceeds).
# d12 R2: the first refused key of a plist carries the PRIMARY refusal text (not the identity re-check text)
r2_primary() { local ln; ln=$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${TAB}check=$1${TAB}" | head -1)
  case "$ln" in *"backup of the preference file failed or was refused"*) ;; *) return 1;; esac
  case "$ln" in *"changed between backup and write"*) return 1;; esac; return 0; }
PSU="$F/Library/Preferences/com.apple.SoftwareUpdate.plist"
upd_fix 9; printf 'x' > "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/backup"
run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply; s9=$(ustall); w9=$(nwrites)
{ [ "$s9" = "$(SIX FAILED)" ] && [ "$w9" -eq 0 ] && r2_primary AutomaticCheckEnabled; } && ok "D10 checked backup: unwritable backup location -> FAILED x6, zero writes" || no "D10 backup location: [$s9] w=$w9 line=[$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${TAB}check=AutomaticCheckEnabled${TAB}" | head -1 | cut -c1-300)]"
five="FAILED FAILED FAILED FAILED FAILED"; u9bad=""
# d12 (R2): the PRIMARY refusal is asserted by its detail text, because the identity re-check (second layer) would give the
# same statuses and the same write count when the first guard of wsp_backup is broken (bk-order, bk-cmp). The first check line
# of the refused key must carry the text of wsp_backup's own refusal, and must NOT carry the second layer's text.
for shape in symlink dangling dir fifo; do
  upd_fix 9b; PSU="$F/Library/Preferences/com.apple.SoftwareUpdate.plist"; rm -f "$PSU"
  case "$shape" in
    symlink) ln -s "$F/Library/Preferences/com.apple.commerce.plist" "$PSU";;
    dangling) ln -s "$F/Library/Preferences/does-not-exist.plist" "$PSU";;
    dir) mkdir "$PSU";;
    fifo) mkfifo "$PSU";;
  esac
  run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply; s9b=$(ustall)
  { [ "$(echo $s9b | cut -d' ' -f1-5)" = "$five" ] && [ "$(ust AutoUpdate)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ "$(nwrites)" -eq 1 ] && ! grep -q "SoftwareUpdate AutomaticCheckEnabled" "$F/writes.log" && r2_primary AutomaticCheckEnabled; } || u9bad="$u9bad $shape:[$s9b] w=$(nwrites)"
  rm -rf "$T/fxu9b"
done
[ -z "$u9bad" ] && ok "D11 B1 (d12 R2: the primary refusal text, not the identity re-check text, is on the first refused key): the SoftwareUpdate plist as a symlink, a DANGLING symlink, a directory or a FIFO is refused (its 5 keys FAILED, nothing written to it; the App Store key still proceeds)" || no "D11 backup refusal shapes:$u9bad"
upd_fix 9c; rm -f "$F/stubs/cp"; printf '#!/bin/bash\nfor a; do d=$a; done\nprintf BAD > "$d"\nexit 0\n' > "$F/stubs/cp"; chmod 755 "$F/stubs/cp"
run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply; s9c=$(ustall)
{ [ "$s9c" = "$(SIX FAILED)" ] && [ "$(nwrites)" -eq 0 ] && r2_primary AutomaticCheckEnabled && r2_primary AutoUpdate; } && ok "D11 B1 (d12 R2: primary refusal text asserted): a backup copy that differs from the source (cp exits 0 but writes other bytes) -> cmp mismatch -> FAILED x6, zero writes" || no "D11 bad copy: [$s9c] w=$(nwrites)"
rm -rf "$T/fxu9c"
upd_fix 9d; rm -f "$F/stubs/cmp"; printf '#!/bin/bash\nc="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/data/cmp.count"; n=$(cat "$c" 2>/dev/null || echo 0); n=$((n+1)); echo $n > "$c"\n[ "$n" = 2 ] && printf x >> "$2"\nexec /usr/bin/cmp "$@"\n' > "$F/stubs/cmp"; chmod 755 "$F/stubs/cmp"
run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply; s9d=$(ustall)
{ [ "$(echo $s9d | cut -d' ' -f1-5)" = "$five" ] && [ "$(ust AutoUpdate)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ "$(nwrites)" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'changed between backup and write'; } && ok "D11 B1: the plist changes between the backup and the write (2nd cmp, the identity re-check) -> its 5 keys FAILED 'changed between backup and write', nothing written to it" || no "D11 change between backup and write: [$s9d] w=$(nwrites)"
rm -rf "$T/fxu9d"
upd_fix 9e; rm -f "$F/Library/Preferences/com.apple.commerce.plist"
run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply; s9e=$(ustall)
{ [ "$s9e" = "$(SIX CHANGED_VERIFIED)" ] && [ -n "$(find "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/backup" -name 'com.apple.commerce.plist.ABSENT')" ]; } && ok "D11 B1: a plist that does not exist gets an explicit ABSENT marker in its backup directory" || no "D11 absent marker: [$s9e]"
rm -rf "$T/fxu9e"
upd_fix 9f; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply; nb1=$(nbk)
bkn=$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${TAB}check=AutomaticCheckEnabled${TAB}" | sed -n 's/.*backup \([0-9][0-9]*\.[A-Za-z0-9]*\).*/\1/p' | head -1)
defseed $SU_D AutomaticDownload 0; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply; nb2=$(nbk)
{ [ "$nb1" -eq 2 ] && [ "$nb2" -eq 3 ] && [ -n "$bkn" ] && [ -f "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/backup/$bkn/com.apple.SoftwareUpdate.plist" ] && grep -q 'PLIST-SU-ORIG' "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/backup/$bkn/com.apple.SoftwareUpdate.plist" && printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'check=rollback-manual'; } && ok "D11 S4/B1: every apply gets its own unique backup directory (2 then 3, the first pristine copy kept), the report names the directory (backup $bkn) and a rollback-manual record says the restore is manual" || no "D11 unique backups: $nb1 $nb2 name=[$bkn]"
bkf=$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${TAB}check=AutomaticDownload${TAB}" | sed -n 's/.*'"$TAB"'backup=\([^'"$TAB"']*\).*/\1/p' | head -1)
{ [ -n "$bkf" ] && [ -d "$bkf" ] && [ "$bkf" = "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/backup/${bkf##*/}" ] && printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${TAB}check=AutomaticDownload${TAB}" | grep -q "backup ${bkf##*/}"; } && ok "d12 (documentation): the written-key record carries the FULL backup directory path in the backup= field (an existing directory under the backup root), and its last component in the detail" || no "d12 full backup path: [$bkf]"
rm -rf "$T/fxu9f"
# U10 apply gate: SIP unreadable -> gate blocks, zero writes; no install/restart calls ever
upd_fix 10; echo 1 > "$F/data/csrutil_status_.rc"; rm -f "$F/data/csrutil_status_.out"
run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply; s10=$(ustall)
{ printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'check=apply-gate' && [ "$s10" = "$(SIX PROPOSED)" ] && [ "$(nwrites)" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "D10 apply gate refuses (SIP unreadable): runs inspect only, 6 PROPOSED, zero writes" || no "D10 gate: [$s10] writes=$(nwrites)"
upd_fix 10b; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply
if grep -Eq '^(installer|softwareupdate (-i|--install|-R|--restart|--schedule (on|off))|shutdown|reboot|killall|launchctl|pkgutil|mas|sudo|open) ' "$F/calls.log"; then no "D10 apply made an install/restart/other mutating call: $(grep -E '^(installer|softwareupdate|shutdown|reboot|killall|launchctl|pkgutil|mas|sudo|open)' "$F/calls.log" | sort -u | tr '\n' ';')"
else ok "D10 apply never installs, restarts or changes the update schedule (calls: $(cut -d' ' -f1 "$F/calls.log" | sort | uniq -c | awk '{printf "%s=%s ", $2, $1}'))"; fi
# production text: no install/restart verb anywhere in code lines; the only softwareupdate call is the read-only --schedule reader
sw=$(grep -h 'wsp_run [A-Za-z_]* *softwareupdate' "$SUITE"/workstationadmin-lib.txt "$SUITE"/workstationadmin-mod-*.txt "$SUITE"/workstationadmin-provision.txt "$SUITE"/UserSpace-*.txt | grep -v '^ *#' | grep -vc 'wsp_run _s softwareupdate --schedule$')
bad=$(grep -hE 'softwareupdate.*(--install|-i |--restart|-R |--download|--schedule (on|off)|--agree|--all|--recommended)|(^|[&; |])(shutdown|reboot|installer) ' "$SUITE"/workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt | grep -v '^ *#' | wc -l)
[ "$sw" -eq 0 ] && [ "$bad" -eq 0 ] && ok "D10 text: the only softwareupdate call in production code is the read-only --schedule reader; no install/restart verb in the updates module" || no "D10 text: softwareupdate lines=$sw forbidden verbs=$bad"
cfn=$(grep -c 'wsp_confirm' "$SUITE"/workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt) || cfn=0
{ [ "$cfn" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "D11 text: the updates module never calls wsp_confirm; the apply gate is a non-interactive prerequisite/policy check (no prompt, no TTY needed)" || no "D11 text: wsp_confirm lines in the updates module=$cfn"
rm -rf "$T/fxu10" "$T/fxu10b"
# U11 tool-fault sweep over the writer: faults on defaults (read), profiles, softwareupdate and the generic text tools
mark "D10 tool-fault sweep"
mkfault() { rm -f "$F/stubs/$1"; cat > "$F/stubs/$1" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
m=$(cat "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/data/fault.mode" 2>/dev/null)
n=$(basename "$0")
case "$m" in
  rc1) exit 1;; empty) exit 0;; junk) echo junk; exit 0;; trunc) printf 'A'; exit 0;;
esac
exec /bin/true
W
chmod 755 "$F/stubs/$1"; }
u11bad=0; u11n=0; u11diff=0
upd_fix 110; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply; clean11=$(ustall); [ "$clean11" = "$(SIX CHANGED_VERIFIED)" ] || no "D10 sweep control: clean apply gave [$clean11]"
rm -rf "$T/fxu110"
for m in rc1 empty junk twoline nul trunc; do
  upd_fix 111; echo "$m" > "$F/data/defaults.read.mode"; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply
  sa=$(ustall); u11n=$((u11n+1))
  case "$sa" in *CHANGED_VERIFIED*|*ALREADY_CORRECT*) u11bad=$((u11bad+1)); echo "# D10 sweep: defaults read/$m -> [$sa]";; esac
  [ "$(nwrites)" -eq 0 ] || { u11bad=$((u11bad+1)); echo "# D10 sweep: defaults read/$m wrote"; }
  [ "$sa" != "$clean11" ] && u11diff=$((u11diff+1)); rm -rf "$T/fxu111"
done
for t in profiles softwareupdate; do for m in rc1 empty junk trunc; do
  upd_fix 112; mkfault "$t"; echo "$m" > "$F/data/fault.mode"; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply
  sa=$(ustall); u11n=$((u11n+1)); [ "$sa" != "$clean11" ] && u11diff=$((u11diff+1))
  if [ "$t" = profiles ]; then
    case "$sa" in *CHANGED_VERIFIED*|*ALREADY_CORRECT*) u11bad=$((u11bad+1)); echo "# D10 sweep: profiles/$m -> [$sa]";; esac
    [ "$(nwrites)" -eq 0 ] || { u11bad=$((u11bad+1)); echo "# D10 sweep: profiles/$m wrote"; }
  else
    [ "$(ust AutomaticCheckEnabled)" = UNKNOWN ] || { u11bad=$((u11bad+1)); echo "# D10 sweep: schedule/$m -> check=$(ust AutomaticCheckEnabled)"; }
  fi
  rm -rf "$T/fxu112"
done; done
for t in cp cmp mktemp date chmod mkdir; do
  upd_fix 114; mkfault "$t"; echo rc1 > "$F/data/fault.mode"; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply
  sa=$(ustall); u11n=$((u11n+1)); [ "$sa" != "$clean11" ] && u11diff=$((u11diff+1))
  case "$sa" in *CHANGED_VERIFIED*|*ALREADY_CORRECT*) u11bad=$((u11bad+1)); echo "# D11 sweep: backup tool $t/rc1 -> [$sa]";; esac
  [ "$(nwrites)" -eq 0 ] || { u11bad=$((u11bad+1)); echo "# D11 sweep: backup tool $t/rc1 wrote"; }
  rm -rf "$T/fxu114"
done
for t in tr sed cut sort uniq paste wc awk grep head tee od; do for m in rc1 trunc; do
  upd_fix 113; mkfault "$t"; echo "$m" > "$F/data/fault.mode"; run workstationadmin-mod-updates.txt --mode apply
  sa=$(ustall); u11n=$((u11n+1)); [ "$sa" != "$clean11" ] && u11diff=$((u11diff+1))
  for st in $sa; do case "$st" in CHANGED_VERIFIED|UNKNOWN|FAILED|BLOCKED_POLICY|PROPOSED) ;; *) u11bad=$((u11bad+1)); echo "# D10 sweep: $t/$m -> [$sa]";; esac; done
  rm -rf "$T/fxu113"
done; done
[ "$u11bad" -eq 0 ] && ok "D10 tool-fault sweep ($u11n runs: defaults read x6 modes, profiles/softwareupdate x4, 6 backup tools x rc1, 12 text/capture tools x2): no CHANGED_VERIFIED/ALREADY_CORRECT or write from a faulted reader; schedule faults never verify the check key; text-tool faults only yield the clean value, UNKNOWN, FAILED, or a fail-closed drop to inspect (PROPOSED, nothing written)" || no "D10 tool-fault sweep: $u11bad violations"
[ "$u11diff" -ge 15 ] && ok "D10 sweep non-vacuous: $u11diff of $u11n faulted runs changed the report" || no "D10 sweep vacuous: only $u11diff changed"
# --- D12: USER PREFERENCES (module 2) -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# UserSpace-preferences.txt (the user's own session, never root) and workstationadmin-mod-userprefs.txt (offline home of a
# user who is NOT logged in, written AS that user). The list is operator config (user-prefs.conf); there is no default list.
mark "D12 user preferences"
PTAB=$(printf '\t'); HARNESS_USER=$(id -un)
mkpdefs() { rm -f "$F/stubs/defaults" "$F/stubs/sudo" "$F/stubs/plutil" "$F/stubs/ps"; cat > "$F/stubs/defaults" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; d="$r/data/pdefs"
printf 'defaults %s %s\n' "${1:-}" "${2:-}" >> "$r/calls.log"
f="$d/$(printf '%s__%s' "${2:-}" "${3:-}" | tr -c 'A-Za-z0-9._-' '_')"
case "${1:-}" in
  write) printf 'write %s %s %s %s as=%s\n' "${2:-}" "${3:-}" "${4:-}" "${5:-}" "${PSTUB_AS:-self}" >> "$r/writes.log"
    [ -f "$d/write.rc" ] && exit "$(cat "$d/write.rc")"
    [ -f "$d/write.noop" ] && exit 0
    case "${4:-}" in
      -bool) t=boolean; case "${5:-}" in true) v=1;; false) v=0;; *) exit 1;; esac;;
      -int) t=integer; v="${5:-}";; -string) t=string; v="${5:-}";; *) exit 1;;
    esac
    printf '%s\n' "$v" > "$f"; echo "Type is $t" > "$f.type"
    case "${2:-}" in /*) touch "${2}.plist"; [ -f "$d/write.chmod" ] && chmod 666 "${2}.plist"
      echo 0 > "$d/lateidx"   # d13 S5 retry model: plutil.late = how many plutil reads after each write still show the old file
      if [ ! -f "$d/disk.skip" ]; then   # d13 disk model: what plutil will find in the file (independent of the "daemon" store above)
        case "${4:-}" in -bool) el="<${5:-}/>";; -int) el="<integer>${5:-}</integer>";; *) el="<string>${5:-}</string>";; esac
        [ -f "$d/disk.wrongtype" ] && el='<array/>'
        printf '\t<key>%s</key>\n\t%s\n' "${3:-}" "$el" >> "$d/disk.$(printf '%s' "${2}.plist" | tr -c 'A-Za-z0-9._-' '_')"
      fi;; esac
    exit 0;;
  read) [ -f "$d/read.rc1" ] && { echo "boom" >&2; exit 1; }
    if [ -f "$f" ]; then cat "$f"; exit 0; fi
    echo "The domain/default pair of (${2:-}, ${3:-}) does not exist" >&2; exit 1;;
  read-type) if [ -f "$f.type" ]; then cat "$f.type"; exit 0; fi; echo "does not exist" >&2; exit 1;;
esac
exit 1
W
cat > "$F/stubs/sudo" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
printf 'sudo %s %s %s\n' "${1:-}" "${2:-}" "${3:-}" >> "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/calls.log"
# d14 fuse (condition 4): a disk-verify read loop without a bound must END THE RUN here, as a named assertion failure, not as a 60 s TIMEOUT. The fuse is in this
# double because it sees every "sudo -n -u USER plutil" attempt, also when no plutil exists: after 120 of them it records plutil.fuse and terminates its own
# process group (timeout, the script, this stub; the harness is not in it). A bounded run needs at most 27.
if [ "${4:-}" = plutil ]; then fn=$(cat "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/data/sudo-plutil.n" 2>/dev/null || echo 0); fn=$((fn+1)); echo "$fn" > "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/data/sudo-plutil.n"
  if [ "$fn" -gt 120 ]; then echo fuse > "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/plutil.fuse"; kill -TERM 0; exit 1; fi; fi
[ "${1:-}" = -n ] && [ "${2:-}" = -u ] || exit 1
u=$3; shift 3
PSTUB_AS=$u exec "$@"
W
cat > "$F/stubs/plutil" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
# d13: only "-convert xml1 -o - FILE" is accepted (anything else, in particular an in-place convert, is logged as a write and fails)
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; d="$r/data/pdefs"
printf 'plutil %s %s\n' "${1:-}" "${2:-}" >> "$r/calls.log"
if [ "${1:-}" = -convert ] && [ "${2:-}" = xml1 ] && [ "${3:-}" = -o ] && [ "${4:-}" = - ] && [ $# -eq 5 ]; then
  echo "plutil-as=${PSTUB_AS:-self}" >> "$r/plutil.log"
  [ -f "$d/plutil.rc" ] && exit "$(cat "$d/plutil.rc")"
  lt=0; [ -f "$d/plutil.late" ] && lt=$(cat "$d/plutil.late"); li=0; [ -f "$d/lateidx" ] && li=$(cat "$d/lateidx")
  stale=0; if [ "$li" -lt "$lt" ]; then stale=1; echo $((li+1)) > "$d/lateidx"; fi
  printf '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n<!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd">\n<plist version="1.0">\n<dict>\n'
  dk="$d/disk.$(printf '%s' "$5" | tr -c 'A-Za-z0-9._-' '_')"; [ "$stale" -eq 0 ] && [ -f "$dk" ] && cat "$dk"
  printf '</dict>\n</plist>\n'; exit 0
fi
echo "plutil-unexpected $*" >> "$r/writes.log"; exit 1
W
cat > "$F/stubs/ps" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
# d13: "ps -axo uid=" double: data/pdefs/ps.out replaces the default listing (uid 0, uid 1, uid 225); ps.rc is the exit status AFTER the listing
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; d="$r/data/pdefs"
printf 'ps %s\n' "${1:-}" >> "$r/calls.log"
if [ -f "$d/ps.out" ]; then cat "$d/ps.out"; else printf '    0\n    1\n  225\n'; fi
[ -f "$d/ps.rc" ] && exit "$(cat "$d/ps.rc")"
exit 0
W
chmod 755 "$F/stubs/defaults" "$F/stubs/sudo" "$F/stubs/plutil" "$F/stubs/ps"; mkdir -p "$F/data/pdefs"; }
PCONF='# example list (generic Apple per-user keys)
example-bool|com.apple.dock|autohide|bool|true
example-int|com.apple.dock|tilesize|int|48
example-str|NSGlobalDomain|AppleShowScrollBars|string|Always'
pseed() { local f="$F/data/pdefs/$(printf '%s__%s' "$1" "$2" | tr -c 'A-Za-z0-9._-' '_')"; printf '%s\n' "$4" > "$f"; echo "Type is $3" > "$f.type"; }
SESS="Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning-Session"
pf_fix() { # TAG KIND(user|off): every entry at the WRONG stored value, plists present, consent given, nobody logged in
  local dd gd
  setup "p$1"; mkpdefs; : > "$F/writes.log"
  printf '%s\n' "$PCONF" > "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf"
  printf 'USERS=%s\nUSER_PREFS_CONSENT=yes\nTARGET_OS_MAJOR=15\n' "$HARNESS_USER" > "$F/$SESS/session.conf"
  printf 'APPLY\n' > "$F/data/tty.in"; echo /dev/pts/0 > "$F/data/tty_.out"   # d13: the typed-APPLY "terminal": a "tty" stub on PATH (exit 0, prints a /dev name; without data/tty_.out it exits 1 = not a terminal) plus the stdin file data/tty.in (run gives it to UserSpace-preferences); library has no test branch
  if [ "$2" = user ]; then PD="$F/home/Library/Preferences"; dd=com.apple.dock; gd=NSGlobalDomain; mkdir -p "$PD"
    echo "NFSHomeDirectory: /home" > "$F/data/dscl_._-read__Users_${HARNESS_USER}_NFSHomeDirectory_.out"   # d13 S9: the directory record of the fixture HOME ($F/home)
  else PD="$F/Users/$HARNESS_USER/Library/Preferences"; dd="$PD/com.apple.dock"; gd="$PD/.GlobalPreferences"; mkdir -p "$PD"
    echo "UniqueID: 502" > "$F/data/dscl_._-read__Users_${HARNESS_USER}_UniqueID_.out"
    echo "NFSHomeDirectory: /Users/$HARNESS_USER" > "$F/data/dscl_._-read__Users_${HARNESS_USER}_NFSHomeDirectory_.out"; fi
  printf 'PLIST-DOCK-ORIG\n' > "$PD/com.apple.dock.plist"; printf 'PLIST-GLOBAL-ORIG\n' > "$PD/.GlobalPreferences.plist"
  pseed "$dd" autohide boolean 0; pseed "$dd" tilesize integer 16; pseed "$gd" AppleShowScrollBars string Automatic
  PUBK="$F/home/Library/Application Support/WorkstationProvisioning/backup"; ORBK="$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/backup"; }
pst() { printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}module=userspace-preferences${PTAB}.*${PTAB}check=$1${PTAB}" | head -1 | sed -n 's/.*status=\([A-Z_]*\).*/\1/p'; }
pso() { printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}module=userprefs${PTAB}.*${PTAB}check=acct-[0-9a-f]*-$1${PTAB}" | head -1 | sed -n 's/.*status=\([A-Z_]*\).*/\1/p'; }
pall() { local i o=""; for i in example-bool example-int example-str; do if [ "$1" = user ]; then o="$o$(pst $i) "; else o="$o$(pso $i) "; fi; done; printf '%s' "${o% }"; }
P3() { printf '%s %s %s' "$1" "$1" "$1"; }
pnw() { wc -l < "$F/writes.log"; }
pbk() { find "$1" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type d 2>/dev/null | wc -l; }
pcfg() { local m; m=$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}check=preferences-config${PTAB}" | head -1 | sed -n 's/.*status=\([A-Z_]*\).*/\1/p'); printf '%s' "$m"; }
UPS=UserSpace-preferences.txt; OFM=workstationadmin-mod-userprefs.txt
# --- user path ---
# PU1 dry run (default): PROPOSED x3, nothing written, no backup, plists untouched, exit 2
pf_fix 1 user; run $UPS; a=$(pall user)
{ [ "$a" = "$(P3 PROPOSED)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(pbk "$PUBK")" -eq 0 ] && grep -q 'PLIST-DOCK-ORIG' "$PD/com.apple.dock.plist" && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ] && ! grep -q 'defaults write' "$F/calls.log"; } && ok "D12 PU1 dry run (default): 3 PROPOSED from the operator list, no write, no backup, exit 2" || no "D12 PU1: [$a] w=$(pnw) rc=$RC"
# PU2 apply: CHANGED_VERIFIED x3 with typed writes, one backup per plist, relative backup path, no root
run $UPS --mode apply; a=$(pall user); wl=$(LC_ALL=C sort "$F/writes.log" | tr '\n' ';')
bkr=$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}check=example-int${PTAB}" | sed -n 's/.*'"$PTAB"'backup=\([^'"$PTAB"']*\).*/\1/p' | head -1)
expw="write NSGlobalDomain AppleShowScrollBars -string Always as=self;write com.apple.dock autohide -bool true as=self;write com.apple.dock tilesize -int 48 as=self;"
{ [ "$a" = "$(P3 CHANGED_VERIFIED)" ] && [ "$wl" = "$expw" ] && [ "$(pbk "$PUBK")" -eq 2 ] && [ "$(grep -rl 'PLIST-DOCK-ORIG' "$PUBK" | wc -l)" -eq 1 ] && [ "$(grep -rl 'PLIST-GLOBAL-ORIG' "$PUBK" | wc -l)" -eq 1 ] \
  && [ "${bkr%/*}" = "~/Library/Application Support/WorkstationProvisioning/backup" ] && [ -d "$PUBK/${bkr##*/}" ] && ! printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q "$F/home" && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ] && ! printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'sudo'; } && ok "D12 PU2 apply (consent, typed APPLY, session, readable MDM): 3 CHANGED_VERIFIED with -bool/-int/-string writes in the user's own session; one backup per plist under the user's Library (2); d13: backup= is ~/Library/Application Support/WorkstationProvisioning/backup/DIR (no home path, no account name; the directory exists under the real home); exit 2 (PENDING_LOGIN keeps the run incomplete); no sudo" || no "D12 PU2: [$a] writes=[$wl] bk=$(pbk "$PUBK") path=[$bkr] dir=$([ -d "$PUBK/${bkr##*/}" ] && echo yes || echo no) leak=[$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "$F/home" | head -2 | cut -c1-300)] rc=$RC"
# PU3 idempotent re-apply, PU4 verify
run $UPS --mode apply; a=$(pall user); w=$(pnw); b=$(pbk "$PUBK")
{ [ "$a" = "$(P3 ALREADY_CORRECT)" ] && [ "$w" -eq 3 ] && [ "$b" -eq 2 ]; } && ok "D12 PU3 idempotent re-apply: 3 ALREADY_CORRECT, no new write, no new backup" || no "D12 PU3: [$a] w=$w bk=$b"
pseed com.apple.dock tilesize integer 16; run $UPS --mode verify; a=$(pall user)
{ [ "$(pst example-int)" = FAILED ] && [ "$(pst example-bool)" = ALREADY_CORRECT ] && [ "$RC" -eq 3 ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 3 ]; } && ok "D12 PU4 verify: a drifted value is FAILED (exit 3) and verify writes nothing" || no "D12 PU4: [$a] rc=$RC w=$(pnw)"
rm -rf "$T/fxp1"
# PU5 gate: no consent -> inspect only, BLOCKED_POLICY apply-gate, zero writes
pf_fix 5 user; printf 'USERS=%s\nTARGET_OS_MAJOR=15\n' "$HARNESS_USER" > "$F/$SESS/session.conf"; run $UPS --mode apply
{ [ "$(pall user)" = "$(P3 PROPOSED)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'check=apply-gate' && [ "$(pbk "$PUBK")" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "D12 PU5 no USER_PREFS_CONSENT in the root-owned session policy: apply drops to inspect (BLOCKED_POLICY apply-gate), 3 PROPOSED, zero writes" || no "D12 PU5: [$(pall user)] w=$(pnw)"
printf 'USERS=%s\nUSER_PREFS_CONSENT=yes\nTARGET_OS_MAJOR=15\n' "$HARNESS_USER" > "$F/$SESS/session.conf"; echo 1 > "$F/data/profiles_status_-type_enrollment_.rc"; run $UPS --mode apply
{ printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'check=apply-gate' && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(pall user)" = "$(P3 UNKNOWN)" ]; } && ok "D12 PU5b MDM enrollment unreadable: apply gate refuses, 3 UNKNOWN, zero writes" || no "D12 PU5b: [$(pall user)] w=$(pnw)"
rm -rf "$T/fxp5"
# PU6 config absent / empty / malformed -> BLOCKED_POLICY preferences-config, no entry processed, zero writes
pf_fix 6 user; rm -f "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf"; run $UPS --mode apply
{ [ "$(pcfg)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ -z "$(pst example-bool)" ]; } && ok "D12 PU6 no preference list configured -> BLOCKED_POLICY preferences-config, no built-in default, zero writes" || no "D12 PU6 absent: cfg=$(pcfg) w=$(pnw)"
pu6bad=""; n6=0
while IFS= read -r bad; do
  pf_fix 6b user; printf '%b\n' "ok-1|com.apple.dock|autohide|bool|true\n$bad" > "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf"; run $UPS --mode apply; n6=$((n6+1))
  { [ "$(pcfg)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ -z "$(pst ok-1)" ]; } || pu6bad="$pu6bad [$bad]cfg=$(pcfg)w=$(pnw)"; rm -rf "$T/fxp6b"
done <<'BADL'
only|four|fields|bool
six|com.apple.dock|key|bool|true|extra
Upper|com.apple.dock|key|bool|true
bad-type|com.apple.dock|key|array|true
bad-bool|com.apple.dock|key|bool|yes
bad-int|com.apple.dock|key|int|1.5
big-int|com.apple.dock|key|int|1234567890
neg-str|com.apple.dock|key|string|-x
space-str|com.apple.dock|key|string|a b
slash-dom|/etc/passwd|key|bool|true
dotdot|com.apple..dock|key|bool|true
empty-key|com.apple.dock||bool|true
dollar|com.apple.dock|key|string|$(x)
semi|com.apple.dock|key|string|a;b
ok-1|com.apple.dock|other|bool|true
dup-pair|com.apple.dock|autohide|bool|false
lead0|com.apple.dock|key|int|007
negzero|com.apple.dock|key|int|-0
plusint|com.apple.dock|key|int|+5
crlf|com.apple.dock|key|bool|true\r
aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa|com.apple.dock|key|bool|true
under_score|com.apple.dock|key|bool|true
dashkey|com.apple.dock|-key|bool|true
dupkey-other-type|com.apple.dock|autohide|string|x
BADL
[ -z "$pu6bad" ] && ok "D12 PU6b malformed list ($n6 variants: field count, upper-case id, bad type/bool/int/string value, path or '..' domain, empty key, shell text, duplicate id; each next to a VALID first line): the WHOLE list is rejected (BLOCKED_POLICY preferences-config), the valid line is not applied, zero writes" || no "D12 PU6b:$pu6bad"
pf_fix 6c user; printf '# only comments\n\n' > "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf"; run $UPS --mode apply
{ [ "$(pcfg)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "D12 PU6c an empty list is BLOCKED_POLICY, never 'nothing to do = complete'" || no "D12 PU6c: cfg=$(pcfg)"
rm -rf "$T/fxp6" "$T/fxp6c"
# PU7 unknown domain: that entry only is BLOCKED_POLICY, the others proceed; nothing is written to the unknown domain
pf_fix 7 user; printf '%s\ncontrol-login|com.apple.loginwindow|LoginwindowText|string|Hello\nsec-pref|com.apple.security.agent|key|bool|true\n' "$PCONF" > "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf"; run $UPS --mode apply
{ [ "$(pst control-login)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(pst sec-pref)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(pall user)" = "$(P3 CHANGED_VERIFIED)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 3 ] && ! grep -q 'loginwindow\|security.agent' "$F/writes.log" && printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}check=control-login${PTAB}" | grep -q 'not in the suite allowlist'; } && ok "D12 PU7 domains outside the suite allowlist (loginwindow, security agent): each BLOCKED_POLICY 'not in the suite allowlist', never written; the three allowed entries still CHANGED_VERIFIED" || no "D12 PU7: login=$(pst control-login) sec=$(pst sec-pref) [$(pall user)] w=$(pnw)"
rm -rf "$T/fxp7"
# PU8 managed or profile-controlled: BLOCKED_POLICY with zero writes and no backup; domain-precise; unknown otherwise
ppfx() { printf '_computerlevel[1] attribute: profileIdentifier: 3F2A1C9E-5B7D-4C1E-9A60-0D2E4B6F8A10\n_computerlevel[1] payload[1] type = %s\n' "$1"; }
pu8bad=""; n8=0
for pt in com.apple.ManagedClient.preferences com.apple.mcx com.apple.applicationaccess com.apple.SoftwareUpdate; do
  pf_fix 8 user; ppfx "$pt" > "$F/data/profiles_show_-type_configuration_.out"; run $UPS --mode apply; n8=$((n8+1))
  { [ "$(pall user)" = "$(P3 BLOCKED_POLICY)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(pbk "$PUBK")" -eq 0 ]; } || pu8bad="$pu8bad $pt:[$(pall user)]w=$(pnw)"; rm -rf "$T/fxp8"
done

pu11bad=""

  pf_fix 11 user; rm -f "$PD/com.apple.dock.plist"

# PO4 home layout: symlinked home / Library / Preferences, world-writable Preferences, foreign-owned (other target user), wrong home record
# d13: a stat wrapper shows a symlink with the mode of its TARGET (755, as a Mac does; Linux always reports 777 for a link), so the writable
# check cannot stop a symlink: only the -L test can, and the reason text must say so (kills pref-symlink).
po4bad=""; hm="$HARNESS_USER"
for shape in homelink liblink preflink worldw; do
  pf_fix o4 off; H="$F/Users/$hm"
  printf '#!/bin/bash\nif [ "${1:-}" = -c ]; then exec /usr/bin/stat -L "$@"; fi\nexec /usr/bin/stat "$@"\n' > "$F/stubs/stat"; chmod 755 "$F/stubs/stat"
  case "$shape" in
    homelink) mv "$H" "$F/realhome"; ln -s "$F/realhome" "$H"; why='missing or a symlink';;
    liblink) mv "$H/Library" "$F/reallib"; ln -s "$F/reallib" "$H/Library"; why='missing or a symlink';;
    preflink) mv "$H/Library/Preferences" "$F/realpref"; ln -s "$F/realpref" "$H/Library/Preferences"; why='missing or a symlink';;
    worldw) chmod 777 "$H/Library/Preferences"; why='group/other writable';;
  esac
  run $OFM --mode apply; hs=$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}check=acct-[0-9a-f]*-home${PTAB}" | head -1 | sed -n 's/.*status=\([A-Z_]*\).*/\1/p')
  hm4=$(stat -L -c %a "$F/realpref" "$F/reallib" "$F/realhome" 2>/dev/null | LC_ALL=C sort -u | tr '\n' ' ')
  { [ "$hs" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}check=acct-[0-9a-f]*-home${PTAB}" | grep -q "$why" && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(pbk "$ORBK")" -eq 0 ] && [ -z "$(pso example-bool)" ]; } || po4bad="$po4bad $shape:[$hs]w=$(pnw)modes=$hm4"
  case "$shape" in *link) case "$hm4" in *7?7*|*77*) po4bad="$po4bad $shape:fixture-link-target-mode=$hm4";; esac;; esac
  rm -rf "$T/fxpo4"
done
[ -z "$po4bad" ] && ok "D12 PO4 (d13: reason asserted, link seen as mode 755 via a stat wrapper): a symlinked home, Library or Preferences directory -> BLOCKED_POLICY 'missing or a symlink' (only the -L test can stop it), a group/other-writable Preferences directory -> 'group/other writable'; zero writes, no backup, no entry processed" || no "D12 PO4:$po4bad"

{ [ "$(pall off)" = "$(P3 FAILED)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ -z "$(ls -A "$F/elsewhere")" ]; } && ok "D12 PO8b offline: a symlinked backup root is refused (3 FAILED, zero writes, nothing behind the link)" || no "D12 PO8b: [$(pall off)] w=$(pnw)"
rm -rf "$T/fxpo8b"
pf_fix o8c off; ln -s "$F/nowhere-o" "$ORBK"; run $OFM --mode apply
{ [ "$(pall off)" = "$(P3 FAILED)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ ! -e "$F/nowhere-o" ] && [ ! -L "$F/nowhere-o" ] && [ -L "$ORBK" ] && [ "$(readlink "$ORBK")" = "$F/nowhere-o" ] && printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'backup of the preference file failed or was refused'; } && ok "D13 PO8c (Reviewer): a DANGLING symlinked backup root is refused offline (3 FAILED, zero writes); nothing created at the link target, link unchanged" || no "D13 PO8c: [$(pall off)] w=$(pnw) target-exists=$([ -e "$F/nowhere-o" ] && echo yes || echo no)"
rm -rf "$T/fxpo8c"
# PT text: production code has exactly one defaults-write path for preferences (typed, three forms) and it lives in the library
pw=$(grep -h 'wsp_def_run [A-Za-z_]* write ' "$SUITE"/workstationadmin-lib.txt "$SUITE"/workstationadmin-mod-*.txt "$SUITE"/UserSpace-*.txt | grep -vc '^ *#') || pw=0
pu=$(grep -hE 'defaults write|wsp_run [A-Za-z_]* defaults write|-currentHost' "$SUITE"/UserSpace-preferences.txt "$SUITE"/workstationadmin-mod-userprefs.txt | grep -v '^ *#' | wc -l) || pu=9
pa=$(grep -c 'userprefs' "$SUITE"/workstationadmin-provision.txt) || pa=0
{ [ "$pw" -eq 3 ] && [ "$pu" -eq 0 ] && [ "$pa" -ge 1 ]; } && ok "D12 PT text: the only preference write is the typed wsp_def_run write in wsp_pref_entry (bool, int, string); neither module has its own defaults write, no -currentHost; the launcher knows userprefs" || no "D12 PT text: writes=$pw modulewrites=$pu launcher=$pa"
mark "D12 end"
# --- D13: MODULE-2 HARDENING (reviewer d12 C3, S2-S5, S9-S12; Documentation items: typed APPLY, full backup path) ----------------------------
mark "D13 module-2 hardening"
CV=CHANGED_VERIFIED; BP=BLOCKED_POLICY; UK=UNKNOWN
pstat() { if [ "$1" = user ]; then pst "$2"; else pso "$2"; fi; }
ptext() { # KIND ID TEXT: 0 if the line of that entry's check contains TEXT
  if [ "$1" = user ]; then printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}module=userspace-preferences${PTAB}.*${PTAB}check=$2${PTAB}" | grep -q -- "$3"
  else printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}module=userprefs${PTAB}.*${PTAB}check=acct-[0-9a-f]*-$2${PTAB}" | grep -q -- "$3"; fi; }
psess() { printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}check=acct-[0-9a-f]*-$1${PTAB}" | head -1 | sed -n 's/.*status=\([A-Z_]*\).*/\1/p'; }
rund() { if [ "$1" = user ]; then run $UPS --mode apply; else run $OFM --mode apply; fi; }
dkname() { printf 'disk.%s' "$(printf '%s' "$1" | tr -c 'A-Za-z0-9._-' '_')"; }
# D13-1 C3: ints are canonical (0, -5 accepted; 007, -0, +5 rejected at load - see PU6b), a stored 007 is never "equal", 32-char ids work
H32=abcdefghijklm-nopqrstuvwxyz-0123   # 32 characters; hyphens keep every letter/digit run under 24 (an unbroken run of 24+ is redacted by wsp_norm as [opaque-token] in the check name)
pf_fix d1 user; printf 'a0|com.apple.dock|tilesize|int|0\na1|com.apple.dock|largesize|int|-5\n%s|com.apple.dock|autohide-delay|int|10\n' "$H32" > "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf"; run $UPS --mode inspect
{ [ "$(pst a0)" = PROPOSED ] && [ "$(pst a1)" = PROPOSED ] && [ "$(pst $H32)" = PROPOSED ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "D13 C3 canonical ints 0 and -5 and a 32-character id are accepted (3 PROPOSED, zero writes)" || no "D13 C3 positive: [$(pst a0)] [$(pst a1)] [$(pst $H32)] cfg=$(pcfg)"
rm -rf "$T/fxpd1"
pf_fix d1b user; pseed com.apple.dock tilesize integer 007; run $UPS --mode inspect
{ [ "$(pst example-int)" = UNSUPPORTED ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "D13 C3 a stored integer 007 (leading zeros) is outside the grammar: UNSUPPORTED, never compared as equal, never overwritten" || no "D13 C3 stored 007: [$(pst example-int)]"
rm -rf "$T/fxpd1b"
pf_fix d1c off; printf 'a-1|com.apple.dock|autohide|bool|true\na-2|com.apple.dock|autohide|bool|false\n' > "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf"; run $OFM --mode apply
{ [ "$(pcfg)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "D13 C3 offline: two entries for the same (domain,key) with contradictory values reject the whole list, zero writes" || no "D13 C3 dup offline: cfg=$(pcfg) w=$(pnw)"
rm -rf "$T/fxpd1c"
# D13-2 S2 table: keys outside the fixed domain->key table are refused entry by entry (both paths), the table entries proceed
BAD13='nsg-icloud|NSGlobalDomain|NSDocumentSaveNewDocumentsToCloud|bool|false
dock-corner|com.apple.dock|wvous-tl-corner|int|0
dock-immut|com.apple.dock|contents-immutable|bool|true
fnd-trash|com.apple.finder|FXRemoveOldTrashItems|bool|false
fnd-warn|com.apple.finder|WarnOnEmptyTrash|bool|false
nsg-kbd|NSGlobalDomain|AppleKeyboardUIMode|int|3
login-x|com.apple.loginwindow|LoginwindowText|string|Hello'
t2bad=""
for kd in user off; do
  pf_fix d2$kd $kd; printf '%s\n%s\n' "$PCONF" "$BAD13" > "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf"; rund $kd
  for id in nsg-icloud dock-corner dock-immut fnd-trash fnd-warn nsg-kbd login-x; do
    [ "$(pstat $kd $id)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] || t2bad="$t2bad $kd/$id:$(pstat $kd $id)"
    if [ "$id" = login-x ]; then ptext $kd $id 'domain is not in the suite allowlist' || t2bad="$t2bad $kd/$id:text"
    else ptext $kd $id 'key is not in the suite allowlist for this domain' || t2bad="$t2bad $kd/$id:text"; fi
  done
  { [ "$(pall $kd)" = "$(P3 $CV)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 3 ] && ! grep -q 'NSDocument\|wvous\|immutable\|FXRemove\|WarnOnEmpty\|AppleKeyboardUIMode\|LoginwindowText' "$F/writes.log"; } || t2bad="$t2bad $kd:[$(pall $kd)]w=$(pnw)"
  rm -rf "$T/fxpd2$kd"
done
[ -z "$t2bad" ] && ok "D13 S2 the fixed domain->key table: NSGlobalDomain iCloud-save and keyboard-UI keys, a Dock hot corner and an *-immutable key, Finder FXRemoveOldTrashItems and WarnOnEmptyTrash are BLOCKED_POLICY 'key is not in the suite allowlist for this domain'; loginwindow 'domain is not ...'; none is ever written; the three table entries still CHANGED_VERIFIED (user and offline path)" || no "D13 S2 table:$t2bad"
# D13-3 S2 Managed Preferences: a file under Library/Managed Preferences (global or per user) blocks that domain; odd folder shapes -> UNKNOWN
mcxbad=""
mcxset() { # TAG: the fixture shape of Library/Managed Preferences for this case (a case list, no eval)
  case "$1" in
    a) mkdir -p "$F/Library/Managed Preferences"; : > "$F/Library/Managed Preferences/com.apple.dock.plist";;
    b) mkdir -p "$F/Library/Managed Preferences"; : > "$F/Library/Managed Preferences/.GlobalPreferences.plist";;
    c) mkdir -p "$F/Library/Managed Preferences/$HARNESS_USER"; : > "$F/Library/Managed Preferences/$HARNESS_USER/com.apple.dock.plist";;
    d) mkdir -p "$F/Library/Managed Preferences/zzother"; : > "$F/Library/Managed Preferences/zzother/com.apple.dock.plist";;
    e) mkdir -p "$F/Library/Managed Preferences"; ln -s /nonexistent-target "$F/Library/Managed Preferences/com.apple.dock.plist";;
    f) mkdir -p "$F/Library/Managed Preferences";;
    g) : > "$F/Library/Managed Preferences";;
    h) mkdir -p "$F/mpreal"; ln -s "$F/mpreal" "$F/Library/Managed Preferences";;
    i) mkdir -p "$F/Library/Managed Preferences"; : > "$F/Library/Managed Preferences/$HARNESS_USER";;
    j) mkdir -p "$F/Library/Managed Preferences" "$F/mpu"; ln -s "$F/mpu" "$F/Library/Managed Preferences/$HARNESS_USER";;
    k) mkdir -p "$F/Library/Managed Preferences/$HARNESS_USER"; : > "$F/Library/Managed Preferences/$HARNESS_USER/com.apple.dock.plist";;
    l) mkdir -p "$F/Library/Managed Preferences"; : > "$F/Library/Managed Preferences/.GlobalPreferences.plist";;
    m) : > "$F/Library/Managed Preferences";;
  esac; }
mcxcase() { # TAG KIND EXPECT(bool int str) NWRITES
  local tg=$1 kd=$2 ex=$3 nw=$4 got
  pf_fix "m$tg" $kd; mcxset "$tg"; rund $kd
  got="$(pstat $kd example-bool) $(pstat $kd example-int) $(pstat $kd example-str)"
  { [ "$got" = "$ex" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq "$nw" ]; } || mcxbad="$mcxbad $tg/$kd:[$got]w=$(pnw)"
  rm -rf "$T/fxpm$tg"; }
mcxcase a user "$BP $BP $CV" 1
mcxcase b user "$CV $CV $BP" 2
mcxcase c user "$BP $BP $CV" 1
mcxcase d user "$CV $CV $CV" 3
mcxcase e user "$BP $BP $CV" 1
mcxcase f user "$CV $CV $CV" 3
mcxcase g user "$UK $UK $UK" 0
mcxcase h user "$UK $UK $UK" 0
mcxcase i user "$UK $UK $UK" 0
mcxcase j user "$UK $UK $UK" 0
mcxcase k off "$BP $BP $CV" 1
mcxcase l off "$CV $CV $BP" 2
mcxcase m off "$UK $UK $UK" 0
[ -z "$mcxbad" ] && ok "D13 S2 Managed Preferences: a global or per-user file (also a dangling symlink) for a domain blocks that domain only (NSGlobalDomain = .GlobalPreferences.plist); another user's folder and an empty folder do not; the folder or the per-user folder as a plain file or symlink -> UNKNOWN, zero writes (13 cases, user and offline path)" || no "D13 S2 managed prefs:$mcxbad"
# D13-4 S5 on-disk verification (offline): plutil as the target user, typed; the daemon-style read-back alone is not enough
pf_fix d5a off; run $OFM --mode apply
n5=$(grep -c '^plutil -convert' "$F/calls.log"); pa5=$(grep -c "^plutil-as=$HARNESS_USER\$" "$F/plutil.log"); pt5=$(wc -l < "$F/plutil.log")
{ [ "$(pall off)" = "$(P3 $CV)" ] && [ "$n5" -eq 3 ] && [ "$pa5" -eq 3 ] && [ "$pt5" -eq 3 ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 3 ] && ! grep -q 'plutil-unexpected' "$F/writes.log"; } && ok "D13 S5 offline apply: every CHANGED_VERIFIED is backed by one 'plutil -convert xml1 -o - <plist>' run AS the target user (3 runs, as=user, no in-place convert), typed comparison" || no "D13 S5 control: [$(pall off)] runs=$n5 as=$pa5/$pt5 w=$(pnw)"
rm -rf "$T/fxpd5a"
t5bad=""
for sc in stale wrongtype rc1 noplutil; do
  pf_fix d5$sc off
  case "$sc" in
    stale) : > "$F/data/pdefs/disk.skip"
      printf '\t<key>autohide</key>\n\t<false/>\n\t<key>tilesize</key>\n\t<integer>16</integer>\n' > "$F/data/pdefs/$(dkname "$PD/com.apple.dock.plist")"
      printf '\t<key>AppleShowScrollBars</key>\n\t<string>Automatic</string>\n' > "$F/data/pdefs/$(dkname "$PD/.GlobalPreferences.plist")";;
    wrongtype) : > "$F/data/pdefs/disk.wrongtype";;
    rc1) echo 1 > "$F/data/pdefs/plutil.rc";;
    noplutil) rm -f "$F/stubs/plutil";;
  esac
  run $OFM --mode apply
  { [ "$(pall off)" = "$(P3 FAILED)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 3 ] && ptext off example-bool 'not found, with the right type, in the file on disk'; } || t5bad="$t5bad $sc:[$(pall off)]w=$(pnw)"
  rm -rf "$T/fxpd5$sc"
done
[ -z "$t5bad" ] && ok "D13 S5 the 'daemon' says written but the file on disk shows the OLD values (cache lie), a WRONG TYPE on disk, plutil exiting 1, or no plutil: 3 FAILED each, never CHANGED_VERIFIED" || no "D13 S5 failures:$t5bad"
pf_fix d5f user; run $UPS --mode apply
{ [ "$(pall user)" = "$(P3 $CV)" ] && ! grep -q '^plutil' "$F/calls.log"; } && ok "D13 S5 scope: the online (own-session) path does not run plutil (disk verification is offline-only; recorded as a design question)" || no "D13 S5 online: [$(pall user)]"
rm -rf "$T/fxpd5f"
# D13 S5 bounded retry (owner decision): at most 3 reads of the file, 1 s apart; a late flush is tolerated, a missing value is not
for lt in 1 2 3; do
  pf_fix d5l$lt off; echo "$lt" > "$F/data/pdefs/plutil.late"; run $OFM --mode apply
  nr=$(grep -c '^plutil -convert' "$F/calls.log"); ns=$(grep -c '^sleep 1 ' "$F/calls.log")
  case "$lt" in
    1) exp="$(P3 $CV)"; enr=6; ens=3;;
    2) exp="$(P3 $CV)"; enr=9; ens=6;;
    3) exp="$(P3 FAILED)"; enr=9; ens=6;;
  esac
  { [ "$(pall off)" = "$exp" ] && [ "$nr" -eq "$enr" ] && [ "$ns" -eq "$ens" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 3 ]; } && ok "D13 S5 retry late=$lt: the old file is shown for $lt read(s) after each write -> $(case $lt in 3) echo 'FAILED after exactly 3 reads and 2 one-second waits per value (9 reads, 6 waits)';; *) echo "CHANGED_VERIFIED, $((lt+1)) reads per value ($enr reads, $ens waits)";; esac)" || no "D13 S5 retry late=$lt: [$(pall off)] reads=$nr (want $enr) sleeps=$ns (want $ens)"
  rm -rf "$T/fxpd5l$lt"
done
pf_fix d5s off; : > "$F/data/pdefs/disk.skip"; run $OFM --mode apply
nr=$(grep -c '^plutil -convert' "$F/calls.log"); ns=$(grep -c '^sleep 1 ' "$F/calls.log"); sa=$(grep '^sleep ' "$F/calls.log" | LC_ALL=C sort -u | tr '\n' ';')
{ [ "$(pall off)" = "$(P3 FAILED)" ] && [ "$nr" -eq 9 ] && [ "$ns" -eq 6 ] && [ "$sa" = "sleep 1 ;" ]; } && ok "D13 S5 retry bound: a value that never shows up on disk costs exactly 3 reads and 2 waits of 1 second per value (never more; no other sleep argument)" || no "D13 S5 retry bound: [$(pall off)] reads=$nr sleeps=$ns args=[$sa]"
rm -rf "$T/fxpd5s"
pf_fix d5c off; run $OFM --mode apply
ns=$(grep -c '^sleep ' "$F/calls.log")
{ [ "$(pall off)" = "$(P3 $CV)" ] && [ "$ns" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "D13 S5 retry control: a file that is already right on the first read costs no wait" || no "D13 S5 retry control: [$(pall off)] sleeps=$ns"
rm -rf "$T/fxpd5c"
# D13-5 S4 process / S3 re-read / S10 uid (offline)
ps13bad=""
pscase() { # TAG PS-OUT(printf) PS-RC EXPECT(none|BLOCKED_POLICY|UNKNOWN)
  local tg=$1 po=$2 rc=$3 es=$4 ss
  pf_fix "s$tg" off
  if [ "$po" != - ]; then printf "$po" > "$F/data/pdefs/ps.out"; fi
  if [ "$rc" != - ]; then echo "$rc" > "$F/data/pdefs/ps.rc"; fi
  run $OFM --mode apply; ss=$(psess session)
  if [ "$es" = none ]; then { [ -z "$ss" ] && [ "$(pall off)" = "$(P3 $CV)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 3 ]; } || ps13bad="$ps13bad $tg:ss=[$ss]all=[$(pall off)]"
  else { [ "$ss" = "$es" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ -z "$(pso example-bool)" ] && [ "$(pbk "$ORBK")" -eq 0 ]; } || ps13bad="$ps13bad $tg:ss=[$ss]w=$(pnw)"; fi
  rm -rf "$T/fxps$tg"; }
pscase a '    0\n  502\n' - BLOCKED_POLICY
pscase b '    0\n    1\n  503\n' - none
pscase c '  225\n  300\n' - UNKNOWN
pscase d '    0\nabc\n' - UNKNOWN
pscase e '' - UNKNOWN
pscase f '    0\n    1\n' 1 UNKNOWN
pscase g '    0\n 502x\n' - UNKNOWN
pscase h '    0\n 5020\n   50\n' - none
pscase i '    0\n  502 \n' - BLOCKED_POLICY
pf_fix s0 off; printf '    0\n  502\n' > "$F/data/pdefs/ps.out"; run $OFM --mode apply
printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'a process owned by the target user is running' || ps13bad="$ps13bad text"
rm -rf "$T/fxps0"
[ -z "$ps13bad" ] && ok "D13 S4 process proof (ps -axo uid=): a process of the target UID -> BLOCKED_POLICY (also with padding); other UIDs, UIDs that merely start or end like it -> proceed; a listing without any UID-0 process, a non-numeric line, an empty listing, ps exiting 1 after a valid listing -> UNKNOWN; zero writes and no backup whenever refused" || no "D13 S4:$ps13bad"
pf_fix s3 off; printf 'USERS=%s %s\nUSER_PREFS_CONSENT=yes\nTARGET_OS_MAJOR=15\n' "$HARNESS_USER" "$HARNESS_USER" > "$F/$SESS/session.conf"
rm -f "$F/stubs/who"; cat > "$F/stubs/who" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; c="$r/data/who.count"; n=$(cat "$c" 2>/dev/null || echo 0); n=$((n+1)); echo $n > "$c"

[ "$n" -ge 3 ] && printf 'WHOUSER console  Nov 10 01:00\n'
exit 0
W
sed -i "s/WHOUSER/$HARNESS_USER/" "$F/stubs/who"; chmod 755 "$F/stubs/who"

{ [ "$(grep -c '^who$' "$F/calls.log")" -eq 3 ] && [ "$(pall off)" = "$(P3 $CV)" ] && [ "$(psess session)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 3 ] && printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'user is logged in'; } && ok "D13 S3 the login state is re-read inside the per-user loop (d14: 3 'who' reads: pass 1, the S6 re-read before its first write, pass 2): a user who logs in after the first pass's reads is refused on the second pass, whose entries are not processed; the first pass is unaffected" || no "D13 S3: who=$(grep -c '^who$' "$F/calls.log") sess=$(psess session) w=$(pnw)"
rm -rf "$T/fxps3"
u10bad=""

  { [ "$(psess account)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'UniqueID below 500'; } || u10bad="$u10bad $uu:[$(psess account)]"; rm -rf "$T/fxps10$uu"; done

# D13-7 typed APPLY: apply needs the consent key AND the typed word APPLY on a terminal (here: the data/tty.in double)
t7bad=""
for tv in none eof lower trail empty yes; do
  pf_fix d7$tv user
  case "$tv" in none) rm -f "$F/data/tty_.out";; eof) rm -f "$F/data/tty.in";; lower) printf 'apply\n' > "$F/data/tty.in";; trail) printf 'APPLY \n' > "$F/data/tty.in";; empty) : > "$F/data/tty.in";; yes) printf 'YES\n' > "$F/data/tty.in";; esac

hs() { wsp_plist_has "$1" "$2" "$3" "$4"; printf '%s' "$?"; }

a1 0 autohide bool true

a4 1 autohide string true

# helpers (D14): the status of one module-3 check, the five install targets, a snapshot of the install tree (name, type, mode, link text, size)
FLM=workstationadmin-mod-firstlogin.txt
fls() { printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}module=firstlogin${PTAB}.*${PTAB}check=$1${PTAB}" | head -1 | sed -n 's/.*status=\([A-Z_]*\).*/\1/p'; }
fla() { local c o=""; for c in fl-bin fl-lib fl-script fl-plist fl-consent; do o="$o$(fls $c) "; done; printf '%s' "${o% }"; }
fltree() { find "$F/Library/LaunchAgents" "$F/$SESS" "$F/elsewhere" -printf '%p %y %m %l %s\n' 2>/dev/null | LC_ALL=C sort | sha256sum | cut -c1-16; }
fl_fix() { # TAG: the user-path fixture of module 2 plus the root policy of module 3 and a stub plutil that knows -lint
  pf_fix "$1" user; mkdir -p "$F/elsewhere"
  printf 'FIRSTLOGIN_CONSENT=yes\n' >> "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/policy.conf"
  FLB="$F/$SESS/bin"; FLLA="$F/Library/LaunchAgents"; FLPL="$FLLA/com.example.workstationprovisioning.firstlogin.plist"
  FLCR="$F/$SESS/firstlogin-consent.conf"; FLBK="$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/backup"
  FLU="$F/$SESS/bin/UserSpace-preferences.txt"; MKR="$F/home/Library/Application Support/WorkstationProvisioning"; MK="$MKR/firstlogin.done"
  rm -f "$F/stubs/plutil"; cat > "$F/stubs/plutil" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; printf 'plutil %s %s\n' "${1:-}" "${2:-}" >> "$r/calls.log"
if [ "${1:-}" = -lint ] && [ $# -eq 2 ]; then [ -f "$r/data/lint.rc" ] && exit "$(cat "$r/data/lint.rc")"; printf '%s: OK\n' "$2"; exit 0; fi
exit 1
W
  chmod 755 "$F/stubs/plutil"; }
runi() { # run the INSTALLED copy as the target user: stdin is /dev/null (no terminal, no typed answer is possible) unless fl_tty gave it a typed answer (d15, Q9)
  RUNS=$((RUNS+1)); _ri=/dev/null; [ ! -f "$F/data/tty.in" ] || _ri="$F/data/tty.in"; printf '%s\n' "$HARNESS_USER" > "$F/data/id_ctx_-un_.out"; printf '%s\n' "$HARNESS_USER" > "$F/data/stat_ctx_-f__Su__dev_console_.out"
  OUT=$(cd "$F" && ptimeout "$RUN_LIMIT" env -i HOME="$F/home" TMPDIR="$F" WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT="$F" /bin/bash "$FLU" "$@" 2>"$F/stderr" <"$_ri"); RC=$?
  [ "$RC" -ne 124 ] || { no "TIMEOUT installed copy $*"; RUN_TIMEOUTS=$((RUN_TIMEOUTS+1)); }; }
fl_tty() { echo /dev/pts/0 > "$F/data/tty_.out"; printf '%s\n' "$1" > "$F/data/tty.in"; }   # d15 (Q9): the agent's own typed answer: a "terminal" (tty double) and the typed line on stdin
exp_plist() { printf '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n<!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd">\n<plist version="1.0">\n<dict>\n\t<key>Label</key>\n\t<string>%s</string>\n\t<key>ProgramArguments</key>\n\t<array>\n\t\t<string>/bin/bash</string>\n\t\t<string>/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning-Session/bin/UserSpace-preferences.txt</string>\n\t\t<string>--mode</string>\n\t\t<string>apply</string>\n\t\t<string>--firstlogin</string>\n\t</array>\n\t<key>RunAtLoad</key>\n\t<true/>\n\t<key>LimitLoadToSessionType</key>\n\t<string>Aqua</string>\n</dict>\n</plist>\n' "$1"; }
mark "D14 S1 static"
# D14-S1a: every entry script discards imported functions and BASH_ENV BEFORE the PATH line (and so before any tool is resolved)
s1bad=""
for f in "$SUITE"/workstationadmin-*.txt "$SUITE"/UserSpace-*.txt; do b=${f##*/}
  case "$b" in workstationadmin-lib.txt|workstationadmin-test-harness.txt|workstationadmin-static-check.txt|workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt) continue;; esac
  pl=$(grep -n 'for _wsp_f in \$(compgen -A function); do unset -f "\$_wsp_f"; done' "$f" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
  ul=$(grep -n '^unset BASH_ENV ENV$' "$f" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1); xl=$(grep -n '^PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin; export PATH' "$f" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
  { [ -n "$pl" ] && [ -n "$ul" ] && [ -n "$xl" ] && [ "$pl" -lt "$xl" ] && [ "$ul" -lt "$xl" ]; } || s1bad="$s1bad $b"
done
[ -z "$s1bad" ] && ok "D14 S1 static: every entry script unsets every imported function and BASH_ENV/ENV before its PATH line" || no "D14 S1 static:$s1bad"
# D14-S1b: behaviour. An exported function named like a tool the script runs by name ("defaults"), and a BASH_ENV file defining it, must NOT win:
# the stubs on the fixture PATH are used, the fake is never called (fake.log stays absent), the run is the normal one (3 CHANGED_VERIFIED, 3 writes)
s1b=""
for kind in func benv; do
  pf_fix s1$kind user; printf 'defaults() { echo called >> "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/fake.log"; return 1; }\n' > "$F/data/benv.sh"
  if [ "$kind" = func ]; then xe='BASH_FUNC_defaults%%=() {  echo called >> "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/fake.log"; return 1; }'; else xe="BASH_ENV=$F/data/benv.sh"; fi
  printf '%s\n' "$HARNESS_USER" > "$F/data/id_ctx_-un_.out"; printf '%s\n' "$HARNESS_USER" > "$F/data/stat_ctx_-f__Su__dev_console_.out"   # run() sets the target-user context; this direct call must too
  OUT=$(cd "$F" && ptimeout "$RUN_LIMIT" env -i HOME="$F/home" TMPDIR="$F" WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT="$F" "$xe" /bin/bash "$F/bin/$UPS" --mode apply 2>"$F/stderr" <"$F/data/tty.in"); RC=$?; RUNS=$((RUNS+1))
  { [ ! -e "$F/fake.log" ] && [ "$(pall user)" = "$(P3 CHANGED_VERIFIED)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 3 ]; } || s1b="$s1b $kind:fake=$([ -e "$F/fake.log" ] && echo yes || echo no)all=[$(pall user)]w=$(pnw)"
  rm -rf "$T/fxps1$kind"
done
[ -z "$s1b" ] && ok "D14 S1 behaviour: an exported function (BASH_FUNC_) and a BASH_ENV file that define 'defaults' are discarded; the real tool path is used (3 CHANGED_VERIFIED, fake never called)" || no "D14 S1 behaviour:$s1b"
# D14-S2: stdin reaches the tty tool through wsp_run: the tool stub (at the absolute-path seam) says "terminal" only if ITS stdin is the stdin file of the run
pf_fix s2 user; rm -f "$F/usr/bin/tty"
cat > "$F/usr/bin/tty" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"
if [ "$(readlink /proc/self/fd/0)" = "$r/data/tty.in" ]; then echo tty-saw-stdin >> "$r/tty.log"; echo /dev/pts/0; exit 0; fi
echo tty-stdin-wrong >> "$r/tty.log"; exit 1
W
chmod 755 "$F/usr/bin/tty"; run $UPS --mode apply
{ [ "$(pall user)" = "$(P3 CHANGED_VERIFIED)" ] && [ "$(cat "$F/tty.log" 2>/dev/null)" = tty-saw-stdin ]; } && ok "D14 S2 stdin reaches the tty tool through wsp_run (the stub saw the run's stdin file as fd 0; apply then proceeds with the typed word)" || no "D14 S2: all=[$(pall user)] tty.log=[$(cat "$F/tty.log" 2>/dev/null | tr '\n' ' ')]"
