{ printf '%s\n' "$q1" | grep -qF "account: $sum1." && printf '%s\n' "$q1" | grep -qF "Type APPLY to continue" && printf '%s\n' "$q2" | grep -qF "account: $sum2." && [ "$a2" = "$(P3 CHANGED_VERIFIED)" ] && [ "$x1" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$x2" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] \
  && [ "$c3" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$w3" -eq 0 ] && ! printf '%s\n' "$q3" | grep -q 'Type APPLY'; } && ok "D14 S3 the list is validated first and the typed question shows 'N entries (A in the suite table, R refused) for the domains: ...'; a malformed list is refused (BLOCKED_POLICY preferences-config) before any question, zero writes" || no "D14 S3: q1=[$q1] q2=[$q2] a2=[$a2] x=$x1/$x2 c3=$c3 w3=$w3 q3=[$q3]"
rm -rf "$T/fxps3a" "$T/fxps3b" "$T/fxps3c"
# D14-S4: a key may start with underscores (Q5: any number in the grammar; the table entry _FXShowPosixPathInTitle is reachable); a domain may not; keys not in the table are refused by the table
pf_fix s4 user; printf 'fx-1|com.apple.finder|_FXShowPosixPathInTitle|bool|true\nfx-2|com.apple.dock|__x|bool|true\n' > "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf"; run $UPS --mode apply
s4a=$(pst fx-1); s4b=$(pst fx-2); s4w=$(pnw)
pf_fix s4b user; printf 'ok-1|_com.apple.dock|autohide|bool|true\n' > "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf"; run $UPS --mode apply; s4c=$(pcfg); s4d=$(pnw)
pf_fix s4c user; printf 'ok-1|com.apple.dock|-autohide|bool|true\n' > "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf"; run $UPS --mode apply; s4e=$(pcfg); s4f=$(pnw)
{ [ "$s4a" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ "$s4b" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$s4w" -eq 1 ] && [ "$s4c" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$s4d" -eq 0 ] && [ "$s4e" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$s4f" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "D14 S4 key grammar: _FXShowPosixPathInTitle (leading underscore) loads and is written; '__x' is refused by the table (BLOCKED_POLICY); a domain with a leading underscore and a key with a leading hyphen make the whole list malformed (zero writes)" || no "D14 S4: $s4a/$s4b w=$s4w cfg=$s4c/$s4e w=$s4d/$s4f"
rm -rf "$T/fxps4" "$T/fxps4b" "$T/fxps4c"
# D14-S6: offline, the login state is read AGAIN right before the FIRST write of a user (who and ps are counted per call; later entries do not re-read)
s6case() { # TAG KIND(who|ps) N CONTENT RC EXPECT(ok|blocked)  : the Nth call of the stub gets CONTENT (and exit RC)
  pf_fix "$1" off; rm -f "$F/stubs/who" "$F/stubs/ps"
  cat > "$F/stubs/who" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; d="$r/data"; n=$(cat "$d/who.count" 2>/dev/null || echo 0); n=$((n+1)); echo $n > "$d/who.count"
[ -f "$d/who.$n.out" ] && cat "$d/who.$n.out"
[ -f "$d/who.$n.rc" ] && exit "$(cat "$d/who.$n.rc")"
exit 0
W
  cat > "$F/stubs/ps" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; d="$r/data"; n=$(cat "$d/ps.count" 2>/dev/null || echo 0); n=$((n+1)); echo $n > "$d/ps.count"
if [ -f "$d/ps.$n.out" ]; then cat "$d/ps.$n.out"; else printf '    0\n    1\n  225\n'; fi
[ -f "$d/ps.$n.rc" ] && exit "$(cat "$d/ps.$n.rc")"
exit 0
W
  chmod 755 "$F/stubs/who" "$F/stubs/ps"
  if [ "$2" != none ]; then k=$3; while [ "$k" -le $(($3+5)) ]; do printf '%b' "$4" > "$F/data/$2.$k.out"; [ -n "$5" ] && echo "$5" > "$F/data/$2.$k.rc"; k=$((k+1)); done; fi   # the reading persists from call N on (a user who logged in stays logged in; every later entry re-reads and is refused too)
  run $OFM --mode apply; }
s6bad=""
s6case s6a none 0 '' ''; { [ "$(pall off)" = "$(P3 $CV)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 3 ] && [ "$(cat "$F/data/who.count")" -eq 2 ] && [ "$(cat "$F/data/ps.count")" -eq 2 ]; } || s6bad="$s6bad control:[$(pall off)]who=$(cat "$F/data/who.count" 2>/dev/null)ps=$(cat "$F/data/ps.count" 2>/dev/null)"; rm -rf "$T/fxps6a"
s6case s6b who 2 "$HARNESS_USER console  Nov 10 08:00\n" ''; { [ "$(pall off)" = "$(P3 $BP)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && ptext off example-bool 'right before the first write'; } || s6bad="$s6bad who2:[$(pall off)]w=$(pnw)"; rm -rf "$T/fxps6b"
s6case s6c ps 2 '    0\n  502\n' ''; { [ "$(pall off)" = "$(P3 $BP)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && ptext off example-bool 'right before the first write'; } || s6bad="$s6bad ps2:[$(pall off)]w=$(pnw)"; rm -rf "$T/fxps6c"
s6case s6d who 2 '' 1; { [ "$(pall off)" = "$(P3 $BP)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ]; } || s6bad="$s6bad whorc:[$(pall off)]w=$(pnw)"; rm -rf "$T/fxps6d"
s6case s6e ps 2 '  225\n' ''; { [ "$(pall off)" = "$(P3 $BP)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ]; } || s6bad="$s6bad noroot:[$(pall off)]w=$(pnw)"; rm -rf "$T/fxps6e"
s6case s6f ps 2 '    0\nabc\n' ''; { [ "$(pall off)" = "$(P3 $BP)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ]; } || s6bad="$s6bad badline:[$(pall off)]w=$(pnw)"; rm -rf "$T/fxps6f"
[ -z "$s6bad" ] && ok "D14 S6 the login state is read again right before the FIRST write of a user (control: exactly 2 who and 2 ps reads for 3 writes, not one per write): a user who appeared in who, a process of the target uid, a failing who, a listing without a root process or a bad line at that moment -> BLOCKED_POLICY 'right before the first write', zero writes" || no "D14 S6:$s6bad"
# D14 CTF (condition 1, mutant ctf-tty-check): the terminal proof needs the tty tool's EXIT STATUS 0 and a clean capture, not just a /dev name on stdout.
# rc1: the double prints /dev/ttys000 but exits 1; nul: it exits 0 but its output carries a NUL byte (not one clean line). Both: PENDING_CONSENT, 3 PROPOSED, zero writes.
ctfbad=""
for kind in rc1 nul; do
  pf_fix ctf$kind user
  case "$kind" in rc1) echo /dev/ttys000 > "$F/data/tty_.out"; echo 1 > "$F/data/tty_.rc";; nul) printf '/dev/ttys000\000x\n' > "$F/data/tty_.out";; esac
  run $UPS --mode apply
  { printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}check=apply-gate${PTAB}" | grep -q 'status=PENDING_CONSENT' && [ "$(pall user)" = "$(P3 PROPOSED)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(pbk "$PUBK")" -eq 0 ] && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ]; } || ctfbad="$ctfbad $kind:[$(pall user)]w=$(pnw)rc=$RC"
  rm -rf "$T/fxpctf$kind"
done
[ -z "$ctfbad" ] && ok "D14 CTF a tty double that prints /dev/ttys000 but exits 1, or exits 0 with a NUL in its output, is not a terminal: PENDING_CONSENT, 3 PROPOSED, zero writes, no backup, exit 2 (kills ctf-tty-check)" || no "D14 CTF:$ctfbad"
# D14 CR3 (condition 3, mutant root-refusal): BEHAVIOUR of the root refusal. The root double is a user namespace in which the harness user is uid 0 (EUID 0, files owned
# by that user are root-owned, so the trust checks pass); no fixture hook is given (the library refuses that as root), so the script's OWN line must refuse. The run
# must exit 4 with that exact refusal text, and the fixture tree (every path, type, mode, size) and the writes log stay unchanged: zero writes.
rrbad=""
if [ "$(timeout -k 5 20 unshare -Ur /bin/bash -c 'echo $EUID' 2>/dev/null)" != 0 ]; then rrbad=" root double unavailable (unshare -Ur does not give EUID 0)"; else
  for rm_ in apply inspect; do
    pf_fix rr$rm_ user; b0=$(find "$F" -mindepth 1 -printf '%p %y %m %s\n' | LC_ALL=C sort | sha256sum | cut -c1-16)
    ro=$(cd "$F" && ptimeout "$RUN_LIMIT" env -i HOME="$F/home" TMPDIR="$F" unshare -Ur /bin/bash "$F/bin/$UPS" --mode $rm_ 2>&1 </dev/null); rrc=$?; RUNS=$((RUNS+1))
    b1=$(find "$F" -mindepth 1 -printf '%p %y %m %s\n' | LC_ALL=C sort | sha256sum | cut -c1-16)
    { [ "$rrc" -eq 4 ] && printf '%s\n' "$ro" | grep -q '^REFUSED: userspace-preferences: must not run as root or with sudo$' && [ "$b0" = "$b1" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ ! -e /Library ]; } || rrbad="$rrbad $rm_:rc=$rrc same=$([ "$b0" = "$b1" ] && echo y || echo n) text=[$(printf '%s' "$ro" | grep -a REFUSED | head -1 | cut -c1-80)]"
    rm -rf "$T/fxprr$rm_"
  done
fi
[ -z "$rrbad" ] && ok "D14 CR3 root refusal (behaviour): UserSpace-preferences run as uid 0 (user-namespace root double), in apply and in inspect mode, exits 4 with 'must not run as root or with sudo', and nothing in the fixture tree changes (zero writes)" || no "D14 CR3 root refusal:$rrbad"
# D14 S5 read cap (condition 4, mutant s5-read-cap-removed): a value that never shows up on disk ends after exactly 3 reads per value (9 in all) with the plutil
# fuse (see the double) NOT blown: a loop without its cap would reach the fuse and end the run, which is a failing assertion here, not a 60 s timeout
pf_fix s5cap off; : > "$F/data/pdefs/disk.skip"; run $OFM --mode apply
nr=$(grep -c '^plutil -convert' "$F/calls.log")
{ [ ! -e "$F/plutil.fuse" ] && [ "$nr" -eq 9 ] && [ "$(pall off)" = "$(P3 FAILED)" ] && [ "$RC" -ne 143 ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 3 ]; } && ok "D14 S5 read cap: a value that never reaches the disk file costs exactly 9 plutil reads (3 per value) and 3 FAILED; the plutil fuse (120 sudo-plutil calls) was not reached" || no "D14 S5 read cap: reads=$nr fuse=$([ -e "$F/plutil.fuse" ] && echo blown || echo intact) rc=$RC all=[$(pall off)]"
rm -rf "$T/fxps5cap"
mark "D14 module 3"
# ---- module 3: install (inspect, apply, idempotent, stale record, refusals, failures, plist grammar, labels) ----
fl_fix f1; t0=$(fltree); run $FLM; a=$(fla)
{ [ "$a" = "PROPOSED PROPOSED PROPOSED PROPOSED PROPOSED" ] && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ] && [ "$(fltree)" = "$t0" ] && [ ! -e "$FLB" ] && [ ! -e "$FLPL" ] && [ ! -e "$FLCR" ] && [ "$(pbk "$FLBK")" -eq 0 ] && ! grep -q 'launchctl' "$F/calls.log" && [ "$(fls first-login-run)" = PENDING_LOGIN ]; } && ok "D14 FL1 inspect (default): 5 targets PROPOSED, nothing created, no backup, no launchctl call, exit 2" || no "D14 FL1: [$a] rc=$RC"
rm -rf "$T/fxpf1"
fl_fix f2; run $FLM --mode apply; a=$(fla); exp_plist com.example.workstationprovisioning.firstlogin > "$T/exp.plist"
psum=$(sha256sum < "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf" | cut -c1-64)
printf 'FIRSTLOGIN_CONSENT_RECORD=1\nPREFS_SHA256=%s\nUSERS=%s\n' "$psum" "$HARNESS_USER" > "$T/exp.consent"
nk=$(grep -c '<key>' "$FLPL")
{ [ "$a" = "$(printf '%s %s %s %s %s' $CV $CV $CV $CV $CV)" ] && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ] && cmp -s "$T/exp.plist" "$FLPL" && cmp -s "$T/exp.consent" "$FLCR" && [ "$nk" -eq 4 ] && ! grep -q 'KeepAlive\|StartInterval\|WatchPaths' "$FLPL" \
  && cmp -s "$SUITE/UserSpace-preferences.txt" "$FLU" && cmp -s "$SUITE/workstationadmin-lib.txt" "$FLB/workstationadmin-lib.txt" \
  && [ "$(stat -c %a "$FLB")" = 755 ] && [ "$(stat -c %a "$FLU")" = 444 ] && [ "$(stat -c %a "$FLB/workstationadmin-lib.txt")" = 444 ] && [ "$(stat -c %a "$FLPL")" = 644 ] && [ "$(stat -c %a "$FLCR")" = 644 ] \
  && [ "$(pbk "$FLBK")" -eq 4 ] && [ "$(find "$FLBK" -name '*.ABSENT' | wc -l)" -eq 4 ] && ! grep -q 'launchctl\|^sudo' "$F/calls.log" && grep -q '^plutil -lint' "$F/calls.log" && [ "$(fls fl-plist-lint)" = ALREADY_CORRECT ] \
  && ! ls -A "$FLLA" "$FLB" | grep -q 'wsp-new'; } && ok "D14 FL2 apply: folder, library, script, plist and consent record installed and verified (exact plist text with 4 keys and no KeepAlive/StartInterval/WatchPaths; record = sha256 of the preference list + USERS; modes 755/444/444/644/644; 4 ABSENT backups; no launchctl, no sudo, no temp file left; plutil -lint OK)" || no "D14 FL2: [$a] rc=$RC nk=$nk bk=$(pbk "$FLBK")"
# FL3 idempotent re-run: nothing changes, no new backup
t1=$(fltree); run $FLM --mode apply; a=$(fla)
{ [ "$a" = "ALREADY_CORRECT ALREADY_CORRECT ALREADY_CORRECT ALREADY_CORRECT ALREADY_CORRECT" ] && [ "$(fltree)" = "$t1" ] && [ "$(pbk "$FLBK")" -eq 4 ]; } && ok "D14 FL3 idempotent re-run: 5 ALREADY_CORRECT, the tree is byte-for-byte unchanged, no new backup" || no "D14 FL3: [$a]"
# FL4 stale record: the list changes after the decision -> inspect shows it; apply is a NEW decision: record rewritten (old record backed up)
printf 'ex-4|com.apple.dock|largesize|int|64\n' >> "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf"; t2=$(fltree); run $FLM; s1=$(fls fl-consent); s2=$(fls fl-consent-stale); [ "$(fltree)" = "$t2" ] && s3=same || s3=changed
run $FLM --mode apply; a=$(fla); psum2=$(sha256sum < "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf" | cut -c1-64)
{ [ "$s1" = PROPOSED ] && [ "$s2" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && [ "$s3" = same ] && [ "$a" = "ALREADY_CORRECT ALREADY_CORRECT ALREADY_CORRECT ALREADY_CORRECT $CV" ] && grep -q "PREFS_SHA256=$psum2" "$FLCR" && ! grep -q "PREFS_SHA256=$psum" "$FLCR" \
  && [ "$(pbk "$FLBK")" -eq 5 ] && [ "$(grep -rl "PREFS_SHA256=$psum" "$FLBK" | wc -l)" -eq 1 ]; } && ok "D14 FL4 a changed preference list makes the record stale (inspect: PROPOSED + PENDING_CONSENT, no change); apply is a new decision: only the record is rewritten, the old record is in a backup" || no "D14 FL4: $s1/$s2/$s3 [$a] bk=$(pbk "$FLBK")"
rm -rf "$T/fxpf2"
# FL5 refusals: each shape -> BLOCKED_POLICY, the install tree is byte-for-byte unchanged, no backup, no record
flbad=""
for shape in nopol noprefcons nousers badlist linkplist danglepl dirplist linkbin linkscript linkcons foreignplist foreignbin bingw laaccess; do
  fl_fix "r$shape"; why='a target is a symlink, a non-regular object'
  case "$shape" in
    nopol) sed -i '/FIRSTLOGIN_CONSENT/d' "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/policy.conf"; why='FIRSTLOGIN_CONSENT=yes is not set';;
    noprefcons) printf 'USERS=%s\nTARGET_OS_MAJOR=15\n' "$HARNESS_USER" > "$F/$SESS/session.conf"; why='USER_PREFS_CONSENT=yes is not set';;
    nousers) printf 'USER_PREFS_CONSENT=yes\nTARGET_OS_MAJOR=15\n' > "$F/$SESS/session.conf"; why='USERS is unset';;
    badlist) printf 'ok-1|com.apple.dock|autohide|bool|true\nok-2|com.apple.dock|autohide|bool|false\n' > "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf"; why='malformed';;
    linkplist) echo X > "$F/elsewhere/x"; ln -s "$F/elsewhere/x" "$FLPL";;
    danglepl) ln -s "$F/elsewhere/nowhere" "$FLPL";;
    dirplist) mkdir "$FLPL";;
    linkbin) ln -s "$F/elsewhere" "$FLB";;
    linkscript) mkdir "$FLB"; chmod 755 "$FLB"; echo X > "$F/elsewhere/x"; ln -s "$F/elsewhere/x" "$FLU";;
    linkcons) echo X > "$F/elsewhere/x"; ln -s "$F/elsewhere/x" "$FLCR";;
    foreignplist) echo X > "$FLPL"; chmod 644 "$FLPL"; printf '#!/bin/bash\ncase "$*" in "-c %%U %s") echo zzother; exit 0;; esac\nexec /usr/bin/stat "$@"\n' "$FLPL" > "$F/stubs/stat"; chmod 755 "$F/stubs/stat";;
    foreignbin) mkdir "$FLB"; chmod 755 "$FLB"; printf '#!/bin/bash\ncase "$*" in "-c %%U %s") echo zzother; exit 0;; esac\nexec /usr/bin/stat "$@"\n' "$FLB" > "$F/stubs/stat"; chmod 755 "$F/stubs/stat";;
    bingw) mkdir "$FLB"; chmod 777 "$FLB";;
    laaccess) rm -rf "$FLLA"; why='not a trusted folder';;
  esac
  t0=$(fltree); run $FLM --mode apply
  g=$(fls apply-gate); gl=$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}check=apply-gate${PTAB}" | head -1)
  [ "$g" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] || flbad="$flbad $shape:gate=[$g]"
  case "$gl" in *"$why"*) ;; *) flbad="$flbad $shape:why";; esac
  [ "$(fltree)" = "$t0" ] || flbad="$flbad $shape:changed"
  [ "$(pbk "$FLBK")" -eq 0 ] || flbad="$flbad $shape:backup"
  [ "$RC" -eq 2 ] || flbad="$flbad $shape:rc=$RC"
  if [ -e "$F/elsewhere/x" ]; then [ "$(cat "$F/elsewhere/x")" = X ] || flbad="$flbad $shape:link-target-written"; fi
  case "$shape" in linkcons) [ -L "$FLCR" ] || flbad="$flbad $shape:link-gone";; *) [ ! -e "$FLCR" ] || flbad="$flbad $shape:record-written";; esac
  chmod -R u+rwx "$F" 2>/dev/null; rm -rf "$F"
done
[ -z "$flbad" ] && ok "D14 FL5 refusals (no FIRSTLOGIN_CONSENT, no USER_PREFS_CONSENT, no USERS, malformed list, symlinked/dangling/directory plist, symlinked folder, script or record, foreign-owned plist or folder, group/other-writable folder, missing LaunchAgents folder): BLOCKED_POLICY apply-gate with the reason, exit 2, the install tree unchanged byte for byte, no backup, no record, nothing written behind a link" || no "D14 FL5:$flbad"
# FL6 a failing step stops the install; the consent record (the arming step) is never written after a failure
fl_fix f6a; rm -rf "$FLBK"; : > "$FLBK"; run $FLM --mode apply; a=$(fla); r6a=$RC
{ [ "$a" = "$CV FAILED FAILED FAILED FAILED" ] && [ "$r6a" -eq 3 ] && [ ! -e "$FLPL" ] && [ ! -e "$FLCR" ] && [ ! -e "$FLU" ] && ! ls -A "$FLB" | grep -q 'wsp-new'; } && ok "D14 FL6a the backup root is unusable: the library step FAILS, the later steps are FAILED 'not attempted', no plist, no script, no consent record, exit 3" || no "D14 FL6a: [$a] rc=$r6a"
rm -rf "$F"
fl_fix f6b; chmod 555 "$FLLA"; run $FLM --mode apply; a=$(fla); r6b=$RC; chmod 755 "$FLLA"
{ [ "$a" = "$CV $CV $CV FAILED FAILED" ] && [ "$r6b" -eq 3 ] && [ ! -e "$FLPL" ] && [ ! -e "$FLCR" ] && [ -f "$FLU" ] && ! ls -A "$FLLA" "$FLB" | grep -q 'wsp-new'; } && ok "D14 FL6b the LaunchAgents folder is not writable: library and script are installed, the plist step FAILS, the consent record is FAILED 'not attempted' and does not exist (the agent stays unarmed), no temp file left, exit 3" || no "D14 FL6b: [$a] rc=$r6b"
rm -rf "$F"
# FL7 a different plist at the target is backed up (its bytes kept) and replaced by the exact text; the other targets stay
fl_fix f7; run $FLM --mode apply; sed -i 's#^\t<key>RunAtLoad</key>#\t<key>KeepAlive</key>\n\t<true/>\n\t<key>RunAtLoad</key>#' "$FLPL"; nb=$(pbk "$FLBK")
run $FLM; i7=$(fls fl-plist); run $FLM --mode apply; a=$(fla)
{ [ "$i7" = PROPOSED ] && [ "$a" = "ALREADY_CORRECT ALREADY_CORRECT ALREADY_CORRECT $CV ALREADY_CORRECT" ] && cmp -s "$T/exp.plist" "$FLPL" && [ "$(pbk "$FLBK")" -eq $((nb+1)) ] && [ "$(grep -rl 'KeepAlive' "$FLBK" | wc -l)" -eq 1 ]; } && ok "D14 FL7 a tampered plist (extra KeepAlive key) is PROPOSED by inspect and replaced by apply after a backup that keeps its bytes; every other target stays" || no "D14 FL7: $i7 [$a]"
rm -rf "$F"
# FL8 plist content grammar: each variation is a difference (PROPOSED), never ALREADY_CORRECT; inspect writes nothing
fl8bad=""; n8=0
for v in runatload session arg-extra shell mode-inspect limit-gone comment label trailer perm666 keepalive prog-path; do
  fl_fix "f8$v"; run $FLM --mode apply; n8=$((n8+1))
  case "$v" in
    runatload) sed -i 's#<true/>#<false/>#' "$FLPL";;
    session) sed -i 's#<string>Aqua</string>#<string>LoginWindow</string>#' "$FLPL";;
    arg-extra) sed -i 's#<string>--firstlogin</string>#<string>--firstlogin</string><string>x</string>#' "$FLPL";;
    shell) sed -i 's#<string>/bin/bash</string>#<string>/bin/zsh</string>#' "$FLPL";;
    mode-inspect) sed -i 's#<string>apply</string>#<string>inspect</string>#' "$FLPL";;
    limit-gone) sed -i '/<key>LimitLoadToSessionType<\/key>/,+1d' "$FLPL";;
    comment) sed -i 's#<plist version="1.0">#<plist version="1.0"><!-- x -->#' "$FLPL";;
    label) sed -i 's#<string>com.example.workstationprovisioning.firstlogin</string>#<string>com.example.other.firstlogin</string>#' "$FLPL";;
    trailer) echo '<!-- tail -->' >> "$FLPL";;
    perm666) chmod 666 "$FLPL";;
    keepalive) sed -i 's#^\t<key>RunAtLoad</key>#\t<key>KeepAlive</key>\n\t<true/>\n\t<key>RunAtLoad</key>#' "$FLPL";;
    prog-path) sed -i 's#/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning-Session/bin/#/tmp/#' "$FLPL";;
  esac
  t0=$(fltree); run $FLM; a=$(fla)
  { [ "$(fls fl-plist)" = PROPOSED ] && [ "$a" = "ALREADY_CORRECT ALREADY_CORRECT ALREADY_CORRECT PROPOSED ALREADY_CORRECT" ] && [ "$(fltree)" = "$t0" ]; } || fl8bad="$fl8bad $v:[$a]"
  rm -rf "$F"
done
[ -z "$fl8bad" ] && ok "D14 FL8 plist grammar ($n8 variations: RunAtLoad false, another session type, an extra or changed argument, another shell, inspect instead of apply, missing LimitLoadToSessionType, a comment, another label, trailing text, mode 0666, an added KeepAlive, another program path): each one is PROPOSED (a difference), the others stay ALREADY_CORRECT, inspect changes nothing" || no "D14 FL8:$fl8bad"
# FL9 labels: the operator's generic label names the plist; invalid labels refuse the whole run (fl-label BLOCKED_POLICY, nothing created)
fl_fix f9; printf 'FIRSTLOGIN_LABEL=org.example.site.firstlogin\n' >> "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/policy.conf"; run $FLM --mode apply

{ [ "$(fla)" = "$(printf '%s %s %s %s %s' $CV $CV $CV $CV $CV)" ] && cmp -s "$T/exp9.plist" "$FLLA/org.example.site.firstlogin.plist" && [ ! -e "$FLPL" ] && [ "$(ls "$FLLA" | wc -l)" -eq 1 ]; } && ok "D14 FL9a FIRSTLOGIN_LABEL from the policy names the plist and is its Label (the default label is not used)" || no "D14 FL9a: [$(fla)]"
rm -rf "$F"
fl9bad=""
for lb in com.apple.finder.agent apple.foo.bar Org.Example.Site a.b a..b.c .a.b.c a.b.c.d.e.f 1abc.def.ghi; do
  fl_fix f9b; printf 'FIRSTLOGIN_LABEL=%s\n' "$lb" >> "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/policy.conf"; t0=$(fltree); run $FLM --mode apply
  { [ "$(fls fl-label)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(fltree)" = "$t0" ] && [ "$(pbk "$FLBK")" -eq 0 ] && [ -z "$(fla | tr -d ' ')" ] && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ]; } || fl9bad="$fl9bad $lb:[$(fls fl-label)]"
  rm -rf "$F"

[ -z "$fl9bad" ] && ok "D14 FL9b invalid labels (an Apple label, a capital, two parts, an empty part, six parts, a digit first) -> BLOCKED_POLICY fl-label, no target is classified or written" || no "D14 FL9b:$fl9bad"
# D14 FL hash (kills fl-hash-fail-open): after a valid list loads, a sha256 double fails on user-prefs.conf.
# Apply must refuse, with no installed copies, no consent record, and no WSP_FL_BIN copy. The double is a stub; the real shasum tool is never called.
mark "D14 FL hash"
fl_fix flhash
cat > "$F/stubs/shasum" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"
printf 'shasum %s\n' "$*" >> "$r/calls.log"
for a in "$@"; do
  case "$a" in
    *user-prefs.conf) echo "shasum: $a: failed" >&2; exit 1;;
  esac
done
last=""
for a in "$@"; do last=$a; done
printf 'aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa  %s\n' "$last"
exit 0

# D14 FL trust (kills fl-trust-script-drop): the suite copy of UserSpace-preferences.txt is made group/other-writable after setup (untrusted).

mark "D14 agent"
# ---- the agent: the INSTALLED copy at the user's first login ----
fl_ready() { fl_fix "$1"; run $FLM --mode apply; rm -f "$F/data/tty_.out" "$F/data/tty.in"; }   # the agent has no terminal and no stdin
# A1 first login: consent record valid AND the agent's own typed word APPLY (d15, Q9) -> the list is applied as the user, no sudo; marker written; ONE reused backup folder (d15, Q4)

{ [ "$a" = "$(P3 CHANGED_VERIFIED)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 3 ] && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ] && [ "$(pst firstlogin-marker)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ -f "$MK" ] && [ ! -L "$MK" ] && [ "$(stat -c %a "$MK")" = 600 ] && printf '%s\n' "$mkc" | grep -Eq '^DONE rc=2 run=[A-Za-z0-9+-]+$' \
  && ! grep -q '^sudo' "$F/calls.log" && grep -q 'Type APPLY to continue' "$F/stderr" && grep -q 'First-login apply for this account' "$F/stderr" && ! grep -q 'as=root' "$F/writes.log" && [ "$(pbk "$PUBK")" -eq 1 ] && [ -d "$PUBK/firstlogin-original" ] && [ "$(ls -A "$PUBK/firstlogin-original" | wc -l)" -eq 2 ]; } && ok "D14 A1 first login: valid consent record plus the agent's own typed APPLY -> 3 CHANGED_VERIFIED as the user (no sudo, the prompt with the summary is shown), ONE backup folder (firstlogin-original) holding both originals, done marker (mode 600, DONE rc=2 run=...) written" || no "D14 A1: [$a] w=$(pnw) rc=$RC marker=[$mkc] bk=$(pbk "$PUBK")"

# A3 the list changed after the decision: PENDING_CONSENT, zero writes, no marker; a new apply of the installer re-arms it, then the next login applies

{ [ "$(pst apply-gate)" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && [ "$(pall user)" = "$(P3 PROPOSED)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ ! -e "$MK" ] && [ "$(pbk "$PUBK")" -eq 0 ] && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ]; } && ok "D14 A3 the preference list changed after the decision (hash mismatch): PENDING_CONSENT apply-gate, entries only PROPOSED, zero writes, no backup, no marker, exit 2" || no "D14 A3: gate=$(pst apply-gate) [$(pall user)] w=$(pnw) rc=$RC"
run $FLM --mode apply; fl_tty APPLY; runi --mode apply --firstlogin

{ [ "$(pst apply-gate)" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ ! -e "$MK" ]; } && ok "D14 A4 USERS changed after the decision: PENDING_CONSENT, zero writes, no marker" || no "D14 A4: gate=$(pst apply-gate) w=$(pnw)"
rm -rf "$F"
# A5 the record in every bad shape: PENDING_CONSENT with the state named, zero writes, no marker, no backup
a5bad=""
for sh in missing link junk extra sha mode666 foreign empty dir; do
  fl_ready "a5$sh"; st=stale
  case "$sh" in
    missing) rm -f "$FLCR"; st=missing;;
    link) mv "$FLCR" "$F/elsewhere/rec"; ln -s "$F/elsewhere/rec" "$FLCR"; st=bad;;
    junk) echo junk > "$FLCR";;
    extra) echo EXTRA >> "$FLCR";;
    sha) sed -i 's/PREFS_SHA256=./PREFS_SHA256=z/' "$FLCR";;
    mode666) chmod 666 "$FLCR"; st=bad;;
    foreign) printf '#!/bin/bash\ncase "$*" in "-c %%U %s") echo zzother; exit 0;; esac\nexec /usr/bin/stat "$@"\n' "$FLCR" > "$F/stubs/stat"; chmod 755 "$F/stubs/stat"; st=bad;;
    empty) : > "$FLCR";;
    dir) rm -f "$FLCR"; mkdir "$FLCR"; st=bad;;
  esac
  runi --mode apply --firstlogin
  { [ "$(pst apply-gate)" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && ptext user apply-gate "record is $st" && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ ! -e "$MK" ] && [ "$(pbk "$PUBK")" -eq 0 ] && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ]; } || a5bad="$a5bad $sh:[$(pst apply-gate)]w=$(pnw)"
  rm -rf "$F"
done
[ -z "$a5bad" ] && ok "D14 A5 consent record missing, a symlink, junk, with an extra line, with a wrong hash, mode 0666, foreign-owned, empty, or a directory: PENDING_CONSENT naming missing/bad/stale, zero writes, no backup, no marker, exit 2" || no "D14 A5:$a5bad"
# A6 not the installed copy / usage
fl_ready a6; run $UPS --mode apply --firstlogin; r6=$RC; w6=$(pnw)

{ [ "$r6" -eq 4 ] && [ "$w6" -eq 0 ] && [ "$r7" -eq 64 ] && [ "$r8" -eq 64 ] && [ "$r9" -eq 64 ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ ! -e "$MK" ]; } && ok "D14 A6 --firstlogin from the suite copy (not the installed one) is refused (exit 4); --firstlogin without --mode apply (none, inspect, verify) is a usage error (exit 64); zero writes, no marker" || no "D14 A6: $r6 w=$w6 usage=$r7/$r8/$r9"

[ -z "$a7bad" ] && ok "D14 A7 a done marker that is a symlink, a directory or foreign-owned, and a report folder that is a symlink: refused (exit 4), zero writes, nothing created behind the link" || no "D14 A7:$a7bad"
# A8 the other gates still apply in first-login mode: USER_PREFS_CONSENT removed, MDM unreadable

{ [ "$g1" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$w1" -eq 0 ] && [ "$m1" = no ] && [ "$g2" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$w2" -eq 0 ] && [ "$m2" = no ]; } && ok "D14 A8 first-login mode keeps the other gates: no USER_PREFS_CONSENT or an unreadable MDM state -> BLOCKED_POLICY apply-gate, zero writes, no marker" || no "D14 A8: $g1/$w1/$m1 $g2/$w2/$m2"

set -u
WSP_NL='
'
WSP_FL_PROG=/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning-Session/bin/UserSpace-preferences.txt
. "$T/cu14-re.sh"; . "$T/cu14-lab.sh"; . "$T/cu14-pl.sh"; . "$T/cu14-ct.sh"; . "$T/cu14-has.sh"
bad=""; n=0
chk() { n=$((n+1)); [ "$2" = "$3" ] || bad="$bad $1:got=$3,want=$2"; }
tl() { wsp_fl_label_ok "$1"; printf '%s' "$?"; }
for l in com.example.workstationprovisioning.firstlogin org.example.site.firstlogin a.b.c x1.y-2.z.w.v site.agent.firstlogin; do chk "label-ok-$l" 0 "$(tl "$l")"; done
long=$(printf 'a%.0s' $(seq 1 31)).$(printf 'b%.0s' $(seq 1 31)).$(printf 'c%.0s' $(seq 1 31)).$(printf 'd%.0s' $(seq 1 31))
for l in com.apple.x.y apple.a.b a.b A.b.c a..b.c a.b.c.d.e.f ' a.b.c' 'a.b.c ' 'a.b.c;x' "$long" '' 1a.b.c; do chk "label-bad-$l" 1 "$(tl "$l")"; done
P=$(wsp_fl_plist_text com.example.t.u)
chk pl-keys 4 "$(printf '%s\n' "$P" | grep -o '<key>' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')"
chk pl-label 1 "$(printf '%s\n' "$P" | grep -c '<string>com.example.t.u</string>')"

chk pl-keepalive 0 "$(printf '%s\n' "$P" | grep -c 'KeepAlive')"

X=$(printf '<dict>\n\t<key>_FXShowPosixPathInTitle</key>\n\t<true/>\n</dict>')
wsp_plist_has "$X" _FXShowPosixPathInTitle bool true; chk has-underscore 0 "$?"
wsp_plist_has "$X" _FXShowPosixPathInTitle bool false; chk has-underscore-value 1 "$?"
wsp_plist_has "$X" -x bool true; chk has-hyphen-key 2 "$?"
wsp_plist_has "$X" __x bool true; chk has-double-underscore-nomatch 1 "$?"
printf 'N=%s BAD=[%s]\n' "$n" "$bad"
W
CU14=$(T="$T" timeout 120 /bin/bash "$T/cu14-unit.sh" 2>&1)

cat > "$F/stubs/systemsetup" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"
printf 'systemsetup %s\n' "$*" >> "$r/calls.log"
k=$(printf '%s_' systemsetup "$@" | tr -c 'A-Za-z0-9._-' '_' | cut -c1-120)

exit 1

cat > "$F/stubs/kickstart" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"
printf 'kickstart %s\n' "$*" >> "$r/calls.log"
# -configure -access -off -users USER
if [ "${2:-}" = -access ] && [ "${3:-}" = -off ] && [ "${4:-}" = -users ]; then
  u=${5:-}
  awk -v u="$u" '$1!=u{print}' "$r/data/ard.naprivs" > "$r/data/ard.naprivs.tmp" && mv "$r/data/ard.naprivs.tmp" "$r/data/ard.naprivs"
  exit 0
fi
if [ "${2:-}" = -allowAccessFor ] && [ "${3:-}" = -specifiedUsers ]; then echo 0 > "$r/data/ard.all"; exit 0; fi
if [ "${2:-}" = -access ] && [ "${3:-}" = -on ] && [ "${4:-}" = -users ]; then
  u=${5:-}
  awk -v u="$u" 'BEGIN{seen=0} $1==u{print u" -1073741569"; seen=1; next} {print} END{if(!seen) print u" -1073741569"}' "$r/data/ard.naprivs" > "$r/data/ard.naprivs.tmp" && mv "$r/data/ard.naprivs.tmp" "$r/data/ard.naprivs"
  exit 0
fi
exit 0
W
chmod 755 "$F/stubs/defaults" "$F/stubs/dscl" "$F/stubs/kickstart"
ln -s "$F/stubs/kickstart" "$F/System/Library/CoreServices/RemoteManagement/ARDAgent.app/Contents/Resources/kickstart"
printf 'workstationadmin\n' > "$F/data/id_ctx_-un_.out"
printf 'workstationadmin\n' > "$F/data/stat_ctx_-f__Su__dev_console_.out"
RUNS=$((RUNS+1))
OUT=$(cd "$F" && { sleep 1; printf 'REMOVE\n'; sleep 1; } | ptimeout "$RUN_LIMIT" env -i HOME="$F/home" TMPDIR="$F" WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT="$F" /usr/bin/script -qec "/bin/bash $F/bin/workstationadmin-mod-ard.txt --mode apply" /dev/null 2>"$F/stderr"); RC=$?
if [ "$RC" -eq 124 ] || [ "$RC" -eq 137 ]; then RUN_TIMEOUTS=$((RUN_TIMEOUTS+1)); FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); echo "FAIL - TIMEOUT ${RUN_LIMIT}s: workstationadmin-mod-ard.txt --mode apply" | cut -c1-120 >&9; fi
ast=$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=ard-allowlist' | head -1 | sed -n 's/.*status=\([A-Z_]*\).*/\1/p')
anap=$(cat "$F/data/ard.naprivs" 2>/dev/null | tr '\n' '|')
aall=$(cat "$F/data/ard.all" 2>/dev/null)
{ [ "$ast" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ "$aall" = 0 ] && ! grep -q 'zzother' "$F/data/ard.naprivs" && grep -q 'workstationadmin -1073741569' "$F/data/ard.naprivs" && grep -q -- '-access -off -users zzother' "$F/calls.log"; } && ok "D14 ARD scope: ard apply ends with operator-only specified users and the call log shows the non-operator removed" || no "D14 ARD scope: status=$ast all=$aall nap=[$anap] log=[$(grep kickstart "$F/calls.log" 2>/dev/null | tr '\n' ';')] rc=$RC"
rm -rf "$F"
mark "D14 remote-access end"
mark "D14 end"
mark "D15 agent"
# ---- d15: the owner's answers Q2 (rejected label value), Q3 (a pre-created marker), Q4 (no marker after a failure, retry, bounded backups) and Q9 (the agent's OWN typed APPLY) ----
# Q9: the agent asks for its own word APPLY on a terminal. Anything but the exact word, no terminal, EOF, an empty answer -> PENDING_CONSENT, entries only PROPOSED, zero writes, no backup, no marker.
q9bad=""
for tv in wrong lower yes trail empty eof none; do
  fl_ready "q9$tv"; crs=$(cksum < "$FLCR")
  case "$tv" in wrong) fl_tty 'APPLY now';; lower) fl_tty apply;; yes) fl_tty YES;; trail) fl_tty 'APPLY ';; empty) fl_tty '';; eof) fl_tty x; : > "$F/data/tty.in";; none) printf 'APPLY\n' > "$F/data/tty.in";; esac
  runi --mode apply --firstlogin
  { [ "$(pst apply-gate)" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && [ "$(pall user)" = "$(P3 PROPOSED)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ ! -e "$MK" ] && [ "$(pbk "$PUBK")" -eq 0 ] && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ] && [ "$(cksum < "$FLCR")" = "$crs" ] && ! printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'CHANGED_VERIFIED'; } || q9bad="$q9bad $tv:[$(pst apply-gate)] w=$(pnw) rc=$RC"
  if [ "$tv" = none ]; then ! grep -q 'Type APPLY to continue' "$F/stderr" || q9bad="$q9bad $tv:prompt-shown-without-tty"; else grep -q 'Type APPLY to continue' "$F/stderr" || q9bad="$q9bad $tv:no-prompt"; fi
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$q9bad" ] && ok "D15 Q9a the first-login agent needs its own typed APPLY: 'APPLY now', 'apply', 'YES', 'APPLY ' (trailing space), an empty answer, EOF, and no terminal (tty tool fails although APPLY is piped) all give PENDING_CONSENT apply-gate, 3 PROPOSED, zero writes, no backup, no marker, exit 2, consent record unchanged" || no "D15 Q9a:$q9bad"
# Q9b: no terminal at all (stdin /dev/null, the real login-time case) never applies; with the right word AND a terminal it does (A1); the installer itself never asked for a word
fl_ready q9b; [ -f "$FLCR" ] && [ "$(pall user)" = "$(P3 PROPOSED)" ] || true; runi --mode apply --firstlogin
{ [ "$(pst apply-gate)" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ ! -e "$MK" ] && printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'own typed word APPLY'; } && ok "D15 Q9b login-time run (stdin /dev/null, no terminal, as launchd would start it): PENDING_CONSENT whose text names the agent's own typed word, zero writes, no marker (never auto-applies); the installer module needed no typed word in the same fixture" || no "D15 Q9b: [$(pst apply-gate)] w=$(pnw)"
rm -rf "$F"
# Q9c: the prompt comes only AFTER the trust gates (a changed list, a consent record for other users): no prompt is shown when the gate already refuses
fl_ready q9c; printf 'ex-4|com.apple.dock|largesize|int|64\n' >> "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf"; fl_tty APPLY; runi --mode apply --firstlogin
{ [ "$(pst apply-gate)" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && ! grep -q 'Type APPLY' "$F/stderr" && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "D15 Q9c a changed preference list is refused by the hash gate BEFORE any prompt: PENDING_CONSENT, no 'Type APPLY' shown, zero writes" || no "D15 Q9c: [$(pst apply-gate)] prompt=$(grep -c 'Type APPLY' "$F/stderr")"
rm -rf "$F"
# Q9d static: the agent never calls the launcher's confirmation (wsp_confirm, /dev/tty); it calls wsp_confirm_apply at least twice (user path and first login), the first-login one only after the consent record is verified
ups="$SUITE/UserSpace-preferences.txt"; ncf=$(grep -v '^ *#' "$ups" | grep -c 'wsp_confirm "\|/dev/tty') || ncf=0; nca=$(grep -v '^ *#' "$ups" | grep -c 'wsp_confirm_apply "')
fa=$(grep -n '^ *if ! wsp_confirm_apply "First-login apply' "$ups" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1); fb=$(grep -n '^ *wsp_fl_consent_state$' "$ups" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
{ [ "$ncf" -eq 0 ] && [ "$nca" -ge 2 ] && [ -n "$fa" ] && [ -n "$fb" ] && [ "$fb" -lt "$fa" ]; } && ok "D15 Q9d text: UserSpace-preferences has no wsp_confirm or /dev/tty (the launcher's prompt is never reused), calls wsp_confirm_apply for the first login after the consent record check" || no "D15 Q9d: ncf=$ncf nca=$nca fa=$fa fb=$fb"
# S-5 (owner, d15 draft4): at a login-time PENDING_CONSENT the agent shows ONE native notification with FIXED text (osascript double in $F/stubs).
# N1 success, N2 osascript exits 1, N3 no osascript, N4 osascript hangs (bounded): always PENDING_CONSENT, 3 PROPOSED, zero writes, no marker,
# exit 2, the notify result in the detail; N1 also checks the exact argv (one call, fixed text, no asset tag, key, user, hash or policy value).
# N5: no notification when the apply goes ahead (record valid + typed APPLY), nor on the user path (no --firstlogin). N6 text.
OSAT='display notification "Workstation setup is waiting for approval. Nothing was changed." with title "Workstation setup"'
s5bad=""
for nk in ok rc1 missing hang; do
  fl_ready "s5$nk"; printf 'WSP-AT-7731\n' > "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/asset-tag"; printf 'FAKEKEY-Q2W3-E4R5-T6Y7\n' > "$F/data/fixture-key.txt"
  case "$nk" in
    ok) printf '#!/bin/bash\nprintf "CALL %%s\\n" "$#" >> "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/data/osa.log"; for a in "$@"; do printf "ARG %%s\\n" "$a" >> "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/data/osa.log"; done\nexit 0\n' > "$F/stubs/osascript";;
    rc1) printf '#!/bin/bash\nprintf "CALL %%s\\n" "$#" >> "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/data/osa.log"\nexit 1\n' > "$F/stubs/osascript";;
    hang) printf '#!/bin/bash\nprintf "CALL %%s\\n" "$#" >> "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/data/osa.log"\nexec /bin/sleep 600\n' > "$F/stubs/osascript";;   # the real sleep (the fixture's sleep is a recording double)
    missing) rm -f "$F/stubs/osascript";;
  esac
  [ ! -f "$F/stubs/osascript" ] || chmod 755 "$F/stubs/osascript"
  crs=$(cksum < "$FLCR"); runi --mode apply --firstlogin
  case "$nk" in ok) wn='notify=shown (none)';; rc1) wn='notify=failed (other)';; missing) wn='notify=failed (not-found)';; hang) wn='notify=failed (timeout)';; esac
  { [ "$(pst apply-gate)" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && [ "$(pall user)" = "$(P3 PROPOSED)" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ ! -e "$MK" ] && [ "$(pbk "$PUBK")" -eq 0 ] && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ] && [ "$(cksum < "$FLCR")" = "$crs" ]; } || s5bad="$s5bad $nk:[$(pst apply-gate)] w=$(pnw) rc=$RC"
  printf "%s\n" "$OUT" | grep "check=apply-gate" | grep -qF "$wn" || s5bad="$s5bad $nk:detail-not-[$wn]:[$(printf "%s\n" "$OUT" | grep -o "notify=[^\t]*" | head -1)]"
  case "$nk" in missing) [ ! -e "$F/data/osa.log" ] || s5bad="$s5bad $nk:called";; *) [ "$(grep -c '^CALL' "$F/data/osa.log" 2>/dev/null)" = 1 ] || s5bad="$s5bad $nk:calls=$(grep -c '^CALL' "$F/data/osa.log" 2>/dev/null)";; esac
  [ "$nk" != hang ] || [ "$(grep -c '^sleep 0.5' "$F/calls.log")" = 20 ] || s5bad="$s5bad hang:polls=$(grep -c '^sleep 0.5' "$F/calls.log")"   # bounded: 20 polls, then TERM/KILL
  if [ "$nk" = ok ]; then
    [ "$(cat "$F/data/osa.log")" = "$(printf 'CALL 2\nARG -e\nARG %s' "$OSAT")" ] || s5bad="$s5bad ok:argv"
    for sv in WSP-AT-7731 FAKEKEY-Q2W3-E4R5-T6Y7 "$HARNESS_USER" "$(sed -n 's/^PREFS_SHA256=//p' "$FLCR")" example-bool com.apple; do
      [ -z "$sv" ] || ! grep -qF -- "$sv" "$F/data/osa.log" || s5bad="$s5bad ok:argv-has-[$sv]"; done
  fi
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$s5bad" ] && ok "D15 S5N1-N4 (owner S-5) a login-time PENDING_CONSENT shows one fixed-text notification (exact argv, no asset tag, key, user, hash or policy value); osascript exit 1, missing or hanging (stopped, bounded) is recorded as notify=failed (errclass) in the detail; always PENDING_CONSENT, 3 PROPOSED, zero writes, no backup, no marker, exit 2" || no "D15 S5N:$s5bad"
fl_ready s5n5; printf '#!/bin/bash\nprintf "CALL\\n" >> "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/data/osa.log"\nexit 0\n' > "$F/stubs/osascript"; chmod 755 "$F/stubs/osascript"
fl_tty APPLY; runi --mode apply --firstlogin; a5=$(pall user); rm -f "$F/data/tty_.out" "$F/data/tty.in"
run UserSpace-preferences.txt --mode apply; u5=$(pst apply-gate)
{ [ "$a5" = "$(P3 CHANGED_VERIFIED)" ] && [ "$u5" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && [ ! -e "$F/data/osa.log" ]; } && ok "D15 S5N5 no notification when the first-login apply goes ahead (record valid + typed APPLY: applied) nor on the user path without --firstlogin (PENDING_CONSENT, no osascript call)" || no "D15 S5N5: a=[$a5] u=[$u5] calls=$(grep -c . "$F/data/osa.log" 2>/dev/null)"
rm -rf "$F"
# S5N7 (d16, reviewer S-11): the notify result is also in the detail of the OTHER PENDING_CONSENT branch (consent record not ok, :155)
fl_ready s5n7; printf '#!/bin/bash\nprintf "CALL\\n" >> "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/data/osa.log"\nexit 0\n' > "$F/stubs/osascript"; chmod 755 "$F/stubs/osascript"
printf 'ex-4|com.apple.dock|largesize|int|64\n' >> "$F/$SESS/user-prefs.conf"; fl_tty APPLY; runi --mode apply --firstlogin; d7=$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "check=apply-gate" | head -1)
{ [ "$(pst apply-gate)" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && printf '%s' "$d7" | grep -q 'consent record is stale' && printf '%s' "$d7" | grep -qF 'notify=shown (none)' && [ "$(grep -c '^CALL' "$F/data/osa.log" 2>/dev/null)" = 1 ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ ! -e "$MK" ]; } && ok "D15 S5N7 (d16, S-11) a stale consent record at login (typed APPLY present): PENDING_CONSENT, the detail carries notify=shown (none), one notification, zero writes, no marker" || no "D15 S5N7: [$(pst apply-gate)] [$(printf '%s' "$d7" | grep -o 'detail=.*' | cut -c1-200)] calls=$(grep -c '^CALL' "$F/data/osa.log" 2>/dev/null) w=$(pnw)"
rm -rf "$F"
ups="$SUITE/UserSpace-preferences.txt"; lib="$SUITE/workstationadmin-lib.txt"
n5a=$(grep -v '^ *#' "$ups" | grep -c 'wsp_fl_notify'); n5b=$(grep -B1 'wsp_fl_notify' "$ups" | grep -c 'WSP_MODE=inspect$'); n5c=$(grep -l 'wsp_fl_notify\|osascript' "$SUITE"/workstationadmin-mod-*.txt "$SUITE/workstationadmin-provision.txt" | wc -l)
n5d=$(grep -v '^ *#' "$lib" | grep -c 'osascript'); n5e=$(grep -v '^ *#' "$lib" | grep 'osascript -e' | grep -c '\$')
{ [ "$n5a" -eq 2 ] && [ "$n5b" -eq 2 ] && [ "$n5c" -eq 0 ] && [ "$n5d" -eq 2 ] && [ "$n5e" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "D15 S5N6 text: the notice is called only in the two first-login PENDING_CONSENT branches, right after WSP_MODE=inspect; no root module or launcher calls it; the osascript line has no variable" || no "D15 S5N6: $n5a $n5b $n5c $n5d $n5e"
# Q3 (accepted): a marker the user pre-created (any content, mode 644) makes the first login a silent no-op: exit 0, nothing written, no prompt
fl_ready q3; mkdir -p "$MKR"; printf 'anything\n' > "$MK"; chmod 644 "$MK"; fl_tty APPLY; runi --mode apply --firstlogin
{ [ "$RC" -eq 0 ] && [ -z "$OUT" ] && [ "$(pnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(pbk "$PUBK")" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(cat "$MK")" = anything ] && ! grep -q 'Type APPLY' "$F/stderr"; } && ok "D15 Q3 (accepted by owner): a user-created done marker (any content, mode 644) skips the first-login apply: exit 0, no output, no prompt, no write, no backup, the marker is left as it is" || no "D15 Q3: rc=$RC out=[$OUT] w=$(pnw)"
rm -rf "$F"
# Q4a: a failure writes NO marker; the next login tries again and (once the fault is gone) applies and then writes the marker
fl_ready q4a; echo 1 > "$F/data/pdefs/write.rc"; fl_tty APPLY; runi --mode apply --firstlogin; r1=$RC; m1=0; [ -e "$MK" ] && m1=1
fl_tty APPLY; runi --mode apply --firstlogin; r2=$RC; m2=0; [ -e "$MK" ] && m2=1
rm -f "$F/data/pdefs/write.rc"; fl_tty APPLY; runi --mode apply --firstlogin; r3=$RC; a3=$(pall user)
{ [ "$r1" -eq 3 ] && [ "$m1" -eq 0 ] && [ "$r2" -eq 3 ] && [ "$m2" -eq 0 ] && [ "$a3" = "$(P3 CHANGED_VERIFIED)" ] && [ -f "$MK" ] && [ "$r3" -eq 2 ] && [ "$(pst firstlogin-marker)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ]; } && ok "D15 Q4a failed first logins (exit 3) write no marker and try again at the next login (twice); when the fault is gone the third login applies (3 CHANGED_VERIFIED) and only then writes the marker" || no "D15 Q4a: rc=$r1/$r2/$r3 marker=$m1/$m2 [$a3]"
# Q4b: and once the marker exists, a later login does nothing (the marker still wins)
runi --mode apply --firstlogin
{ [ "$RC" -eq 0 ] && [ -z "$OUT" ]; } && ok "D15 Q4b after the successful retry the marker is honoured again: next login exit 0, no output" || no "D15 Q4b: rc=$RC"
rm -rf "$F"
# Q4c: backups are bounded: 6 failed first logins keep ONE backup folder (firstlogin-original) that still holds the ORIGINAL pre-change plists even though the plists were changed in between; the later successful run adds no folder
fl_ready q4c; echo 1 > "$F/data/pdefs/write.rc"; q4bad=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5 6; do
  fl_tty APPLY; runi --mode apply --firstlogin
  [ "$RC" -eq 3 ] || q4bad="$q4bad run$i:rc=$RC"; [ ! -e "$MK" ] || q4bad="$q4bad run$i:marker"
  [ "$(pbk "$PUBK")" -eq 1 ] || q4bad="$q4bad run$i:folders=$(pbk "$PUBK")"
  printf 'MODIFIED-%s\n' "$i" > "$PD/com.apple.dock.plist"; printf 'MODIFIED-%s\n' "$i" > "$PD/.GlobalPreferences.plist"
done
bko="$PUBK/firstlogin-original"
{ [ -z "$q4bad" ] && [ -d "$bko" ] && [ ! -L "$bko" ] && [ "$(stat -c %a "$bko")" = 700 ] && [ "$(cat "$bko/com.apple.dock.plist")" = PLIST-DOCK-ORIG ] && [ "$(cat "$bko/.GlobalPreferences.plist")" = PLIST-GLOBAL-ORIG ] && [ "$(ls -A "$bko" | wc -l)" -eq 2 ]; } && ok "D15 Q4c 6 failed first logins keep exactly ONE backup folder (firstlogin-original, mode 700) holding the ORIGINAL plists (not overwritten although the plists changed in between)" || no "D15 Q4c:$q4bad folders=$(pbk "$PUBK") dock=[$(cat "$bko/com.apple.dock.plist" 2>/dev/null)]"
rm -f "$F/data/pdefs/write.rc"; fl_tty APPLY; runi --mode apply --firstlogin
{ [ "$(pall user)" = "$(P3 CHANGED_VERIFIED)" ] && [ -f "$MK" ] && [ "$(pbk "$PUBK")" -eq 1 ] && [ "$(cat "$bko/com.apple.dock.plist")" = PLIST-DOCK-ORIG ]; } && ok "D15 Q4d the retry that succeeds after the failures still uses the single backup folder (no new folder, original bytes intact), then writes the marker" || no "D15 Q4d: [$(pall user)] folders=$(pbk "$PUBK")"
rm -rf "$F"
# Q4e: an item that did not exist before (no plist) is recorded once as ABSENT and is not overwritten by a later try; the normal (non-first-login) path keeps its per-entry folders
fl_ready q4e; rm -f "$PD/com.apple.dock.plist"; echo 1 > "$F/data/pdefs/write.rc"; fl_tty APPLY; runi --mode apply --firstlogin; printf 'LATER\n' > "$PD/com.apple.dock.plist"; fl_tty APPLY; runi --mode apply --firstlogin
bko="$PUBK/firstlogin-original"
{ [ "$(pbk "$PUBK")" -eq 1 ] && [ -e "$bko/com.apple.dock.plist.ABSENT" ] && [ ! -e "$bko/com.apple.dock.plist" ]; } && ok "D15 Q4e a plist that did not exist at the first try is recorded as ABSENT in the single backup folder; a later try does not replace that record with the plist that appeared since" || no "D15 Q4e: $(ls -A "$bko" 2>/dev/null | tr '\n' ' ')"
rm -rf "$F"
# Q4f: the normal user path (UserSpace-preferences run by hand, not first login) is unchanged: one backup folder per plist per run
pf_fix q4f user; run $UPS --mode apply
{ [ "$(pall user)" = "$(P3 CHANGED_VERIFIED)" ] && [ "$(pbk "$PUBK")" -eq 2 ] && [ ! -e "$PUBK/firstlogin-original" ]; } && ok "D15 Q4f the hand-run user path is unchanged: one backup folder per plist (2), no firstlogin-original folder" || no "D15 Q4f: folders=$(pbk "$PUBK")"
rm -rf "$F"
# Q2: a FIRSTLOGIN_LABEL line whose value the policy reader rejects (empty, semicolon, ampersand, pipe, command substitution) REFUSES (BLOCKED_POLICY fl-label, nothing written); an absent line still means the generic default label
q2bad=""
for lb in '' 'a.b.c;x' 'a.b.c&d' 'a.b.c|d' 'a.b.$(x)'; do
  fl_fix q2; printf 'FIRSTLOGIN_LABEL=%s\n' "$lb" >> "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/policy.conf"; t0=$(fltree); run $FLM --mode apply
  { [ "$(fls fl-label)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(fltree)" = "$t0" ] && [ "$(pbk "$FLBK")" -eq 0 ] && [ -z "$(fla | tr -d ' ')" ] && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ]; } || q2bad="$q2bad [${lb}]:[$(fls fl-label)]"
  rm -rf "$F"; done
fl_fix q2d; run $FLM --mode apply; { [ -f "$FLPL" ] && [ "$(fls fl-label)" != BLOCKED_POLICY ]; } || q2bad="$q2bad absent-line-refused"; rm -rf "$F"
[ -z "$q2bad" ] && ok "D15 Q2 FIRSTLOGIN_LABEL present but rejected by the policy reader (empty, ';', '&', '|', '\$(') -> BLOCKED_POLICY fl-label, no plist, no record, nothing classified; with no label line the default label is used and the plist is written" || no "D15 Q2:$q2bad"
mark "D15 module 4"
# ---- module 4 (d15): shared resources copied from an operator-chosen source. Fixtures and doubles only: the source tree lives under $F/srcvol,
# the destination is $F/Users/Shared, cp / mv / chown are pass-through doubles in $F/stubs that log every call and can inject a fault.
SHM=workstationadmin-mod-shared.txt
shstubs() { local c
  cat > "$F/stubs/cp" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; printf 'cp %s\n' "$*" >> "$r/shcalls.log"
[ -f "$r/data/cp.fail" ] && exit 1
/usr/bin/cp "$@" || exit $?
last=${!#}
if [ -f "$r/data/cp.corrupt" ]; then f=$(/usr/bin/find "$last" -type f | head -1); echo junk >> "$f"; fi
if [ -f "$r/data/cp.race" ]; then echo raced > "$r/Users/Shared/$(cat "$r/data/cp.race")"; fi
if [ -f "$r/data/cp.mutsrc" ]; then echo changed >> "$(cat "$r/data/cp.mutsrc")"; fi
if [ -f "$r/data/cp.unsafe2" ] && [ ! -f "$r/data/cp.unsafe2.done" ]; then : > "$r/data/cp.unsafe2.done"; /usr/bin/rm -f "$(cat "$r/data/cp.unsafe2")"; /usr/bin/ln -s /etc/passwd "$(cat "$r/data/cp.unsafe2")"; fi
exit 0
W
  cat > "$F/stubs/chmod" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; printf 'chmod %s\n' "$*" >> "$r/shcalls.log"
[ -f "$r/data/chmod.noop" ] && exit 0
exec /usr/bin/chmod "$@"
W
  cat > "$F/stubs/mv" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; printf 'mv %s\n' "$*" >> "$r/shcalls.log"
first=$1; last=${!#}; case "$first" in -?) first=$2;; esac
if [ -f "$r/data/race.at" ] && [ ! -e "$r/data/race.done" ]; then case "$last" in */Users/Shared/"$(cat "$r/data/race.at")") : > "$r/data/race.done"; /usr/bin/ln -s "$(cat "$r/data/race.to")" "$last";; esac; fi
if [ -f "$r/data/mv.failbk" ]; then case "$last" in */backup/*) exit 1;; esac; fi
if [ -f "$r/data/mv.failstage" ]; then case "$first" in *.shared-stage.*) exit 1;; esac; fi
/usr/bin/mv "$@" || exit $?
if [ -f "$r/data/mv.corrupt" ]; then case "$first" in *.shared-stage.*) echo junk >> "$last";; esac; fi
exit 0
W
  cat > "$F/stubs/link" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; printf 'link %s\n' "$*" >> "$r/shcalls.log"
first=$1; last=${!#}
if [ -f "$r/data/race.at" ] && [ ! -e "$r/data/race.done" ]; then case "$last" in */Users/Shared/"$(cat "$r/data/race.at")") : > "$r/data/race.done"; /usr/bin/ln -s "$(cat "$r/data/race.to")" "$last";; esac; fi
if [ -f "$r/data/mv.failstage" ]; then case "$first" in *.shared-stage.*) exit 1;; esac; fi
/usr/bin/link "$@" || exit $?
if [ -f "$r/data/mv.corrupt" ]; then case "$first" in *.shared-stage.*) echo junk >> "$last";; esac; fi
exit 0
W
  cat > "$F/stubs/mkdir" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; last=${!#}
if [ -f "$r/data/race.at" ] && [ ! -e "$r/data/race.done" ]; then case "$last" in */Users/Shared/"$(cat "$r/data/race.at")") : > "$r/data/race.done"; /usr/bin/ln -s "$(cat "$r/data/race.to")" "$last";; esac; fi
exec /usr/bin/mkdir "$@"
W
  cat > "$F/stubs/chown" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; printf 'chown %s\n' "$*" >> "$r/shcalls.log"
[ -f "$r/data/chown.fail" ] && exit 1
exit 0
W
  chmod 755 "$F/stubs/cp" "$F/stubs/mv" "$F/stubs/link" "$F/stubs/mkdir" "$F/stubs/chown" "$F/stubs/chmod"; : > "$F/shcalls.log"; }
shf() { # TAG [ITEMS]: operator source with a folder item and a file item, policy with the copy consent, doubles
  setup "h$1"; shstubs
  S="$F/srcvol/Resources"; mkdir -p "$S/Docs Pack/sub dir"
  printf 'one\n' > "$S/Docs Pack/a.txt"; printf 'two\n' > "$S/Docs Pack/sub dir/b.txt"; printf 'tool\n' > "$S/Tool.txt"
  chmod 755 "$S" "$S/Docs Pack" "$S/Docs Pack/sub dir"; chmod 644 "$S/Docs Pack/a.txt" "$S/Docs Pack/sub dir/b.txt" "$S/Tool.txt"
  SHP="$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/policy.conf"; SHBK="$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/backup"
  printf 'SHARED_SOURCE_DIR=/srcvol/Resources\nSHARED_ITEMS=%s\nSHARED_COPY_CONSENT=yes\n' "${2:-Docs Pack:Tool.txt}" >> "$SHP"; }
shpol() { printf '%s\n' "$1" >> "$SHP"; }
shst() { printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}module=shared${PTAB}.*${PTAB}check=$1${PTAB}" | head -1 | sed -n 's/.*status=\([A-Z_]*\).*/\1/p'; }
shsnap() { (cd "$F/Users/Shared" && find . -print | sort | cksum; find . -type f -exec cksum {} + | sort | cksum); }
shbk() { find "$SHBK" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type d 2>/dev/null | wc -l; }
shstage() { find "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning" -maxdepth 1 -name '.shared-stage.*' 2>/dev/null | wc -l; }
shnone() { [ "$(shnc)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(shsnap)" = "$SNAP0" ] && [ "$(shbk)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(shstage)" -eq 0 ]; }   # nothing written: no cp / mv / chown call, destination bytes and names unchanged, no backup, no staging folder
shnc() { grep -c '^\(cp\|mv\|link\|chown\) ' "$F/shcalls.log"; }
shrun() { SNAP0=$(shsnap); : > "$F/shcalls.log"; run $SHM "$@"; }
shmatch() { # DEST_ITEM SRC_ITEM: same content, folders 755, files 644
  [ -e "$F/Users/Shared/$1" ] && diff -r "$S/$2" "$F/Users/Shared/$1" >/dev/null && [ -z "$(find "$F/Users/Shared/$1" -type d ! -perm 755)" ] && [ -z "$(find "$F/Users/Shared/$1" -type f ! -perm 644)" ] && [ -z "$(find "$F/Users/Shared/$1" -type l)" ]; }
shcopy() { cp -R -P "$S/$1" "$F/Users/Shared/$1"; find "$F/Users/Shared/$1" -type d -exec chmod 755 {} +; find "$F/Users/Shared/$1" -type f -exec chmod 644 {} +; }
shpairs() { printf '%s %s %s' "$1" "$2" "$3"; }

# SH1 default and unconfigured: inspect reports, never writes; apply without any source is refused
shf 1; : > "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/policy.conf"; printf 'TARGET_OS_MAJOR=15\nALLOWED_BUILDS=24F74\n' >> "$SHP"; shrun --mode inspect; r1=$RC; s1=$(shst copy-resources)
shrun --mode apply; r2=$RC; s2=$(shst copy-resources); g2=$(shst apply-gate)
{ [ "$s1" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$s2" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$g2" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && shnone && [ "$r2" -ne 0 ]; } && ok "D15 SH1 no source or items in the root policy: BLOCKED_POLICY copy-resources in inspect and in apply (apply-gate BLOCKED_POLICY), nothing written" || no "D15 SH1: [$s1] [$s2] [$g2] rc=$r1/$r2 calls=$(shnc)"
rm -rf "$F"
# SH2 inspect with a valid source: PROPOSED per item, a matching destination is ALREADY_CORRECT, zero writes
shf 2; shcopy Tool.txt; shrun --mode inspect
{ [ "$(shst item1)" = PROPOSED ] && [ "$(shst item2)" = ALREADY_CORRECT ] && shnone && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ]; } && ok "D15 SH2 dry run: a missing destination item is PROPOSED, an identical one (content, 0755/0644) is ALREADY_CORRECT, exit 2, zero writes (no cp, mv or chown call, no staging, no backup)" || no "D15 SH2: [$(shst item1)] [$(shst item2)] rc=$RC calls=$(shnc)"
rm -rf "$F"
# SH3 apply happy path: copy verified, explicit owner root:wheel called per item, modes 0755/0644, nothing left in staging, no backup, source untouched
shf 3; chmod 640 "$S/Tool.txt"; chmod 750 "$S/Docs Pack/sub dir"; chmod 600 "$S/Docs Pack/a.txt"; sbefore=$(cd "$S" && find . -print | sort | cksum; cd "$S" && find . -type f -exec cksum {} + | sort | cksum); shrun --mode apply; a3=$RC
safter=$(cd "$S" && find . -print | sort | cksum; cd "$S" && find . -type f -exec cksum {} + | sort | cksum)
{ [ "$(shst item1)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ "$(shst item2)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && shmatch "Docs Pack" "Docs Pack" && shmatch Tool.txt Tool.txt && [ "$(shstage)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(shbk)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$sbefore" = "$safter" ] \
  && [ "$(grep -c '^chown -R -h root:wheel ' "$F/shcalls.log")" -eq 2 ] && [ "$(grep -c '^cp -R -P ' "$F/shcalls.log")" -eq 2 ] && [ "$a3" -eq 2 ]; } && ok "D15 SH3 apply (source modes 640, 750, 600 on purpose): both items CHANGED_VERIFIED (content equal, folders 0755, files 0644 whatever the source modes were, no link), 'chown -R -h root:wheel' and 'cp -R -P' once per item, staging folder removed, no backup, source tree unchanged, exit 2 (manual Finder items remain)" || no "D15 SH3 (source modes 640/750/600): [$(shst item1)] [$(shst item2)] rc=$a3 chown=$(grep -c '^chown' "$F/shcalls.log")"
# SH4 idempotent: a second apply changes nothing and calls nothing
shrun --mode apply
{ [ "$(shst item1)" = ALREADY_CORRECT ] && [ "$(shst item2)" = ALREADY_CORRECT ] && shnone; } && ok "D15 SH4 second apply: ALREADY_CORRECT x2, no cp, mv or chown call, no backup, no staging (idempotent)" || no "D15 SH4: [$(shst item1)] [$(shst item2)] calls=$(shnc)"
rm -rf "$F"
# SH5 consent key: absent, 'no', 'YES', empty -> inspect only (BLOCKED_POLICY apply-gate, PROPOSED items), nothing written
sh5bad=""
for cv in none no YES yes1 empty; do
  shf "5$cv"; grep -v '^SHARED_COPY_CONSENT' "$SHP" > "$SHP.n"; cat "$SHP.n" > "$SHP"; rm -f "$SHP.n"
  case "$cv" in none) ;; empty) shpol 'SHARED_COPY_CONSENT=';; *) shpol "SHARED_COPY_CONSENT=$cv";; esac
  shrun --mode apply
  { [ "$(shst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(shst item1)" = PROPOSED ] && [ "$(shst item2)" = PROPOSED ] && shnone; } || sh5bad="$sh5bad $cv:$(shst apply-gate)/$(shst item1)/$(shnc)"
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$sh5bad" ] && ok "D15 SH5 apply without SHARED_COPY_CONSENT=yes (absent, 'no', 'YES', 'yes1', empty): BLOCKED_POLICY apply-gate, items only PROPOSED, nothing written" || no "D15 SH5:$sh5bad"
# SH6 the root apply gate still applies: a caller that is not the operator, a wrong OS major, an unknown build -> refused, nothing written
sh6bad=""
for gk in user major build; do
  shf "6$gk"
  case "$gk" in user) touch "$F/data/ctx.lock"; printf 'someoneelse\n' > "$F/data/id_ctx_-un_.out"; printf 'someoneelse\n' > "$F/data/stat_ctx_-f__Su__dev_console_.out";;
    major) shpol 'TARGET_OS_MAJOR=14';; build) shpol 'ALLOWED_BUILDS=99Z99';; esac
  shrun --mode apply
  { [ "$(shst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && shnone; } || sh6bad="$sh6bad $gk:$(shst apply-gate)/$(shnc)"
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$sh6bad" ] && ok "D15 SH6 apply gate (wsp_apply_gate): a caller that is not the operator, a wrong OS major or a build outside ALLOWED_BUILDS: BLOCKED_POLICY apply-gate, nothing written" || no "D15 SH6:$sh6bad"
# SH7 operator-chosen source path: unsafe forms are refused by name, in inspect and apply, nothing written
sh7bad=""
for sp in 'srcvol/Resources' '/srcvol/../srcvol/Resources' '/srcvol/Resources/' '/srcvol//Resources' '/srcvol/./Resources' '/srcvol/Res*' '/Users/Shared' '/Users/Shared/x' '/Users' '/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning' '/System/x' '/' '/srcvol/Res$x' "/srcvol/R'x" '/srcvol/Resources/.' '/srcvol/Resources/..'; do
  shf 7; grep -v '^SHARED_SOURCE_DIR' "$SHP" > "$SHP.n"; cat "$SHP.n" > "$SHP"; rm -f "$SHP.n"; shpol "SHARED_SOURCE_DIR=$sp"
  for m in inspect apply; do shrun --mode $m; { [ "$(shst copy-resources)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && shnone; } || sh7bad="$sh7bad [$sp:$m:$(shst copy-resources)/$(shnc)]"; done
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$sh7bad" ] && ok "D15 SH7 source path refused (relative, '..', trailing '/', '//', '/./', glob, '/Users/Shared' and below, '/Users', policy folder, '/System', '/', '\$', quote, trailing '.' or '..'): BLOCKED_POLICY copy-resources in inspect and apply, nothing written" || no "D15 SH7:$sh7bad"
# SH8 item list: names that are not plain single names are refused (path, dot-leading, empty member, duplicate, glob, trailing space, 51 items), nothing written
sh8bad=""; many=$(for i in $(seq 1 51); do printf 'i%s:' "$i"; done); many=${many%:}
for it in '../x' 'a/b' '.hidden' 'a::b' ':a' 'a:' 'Tool.txt:Tool.txt' 'Too*' 'Tool.txt ' '-x' 'a$b' "$many"; do
  shf 8 "$it"; for m in inspect apply; do shrun --mode $m; { [ "$(shst copy-resources)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && shnone; } || sh8bad="$sh8bad [${it:0:12}:$m:$(shst copy-resources)/$(shnc)]"; done
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$sh8bad" ] && ok "D15 SH8 item list refused (../x, a/b, .hidden, empty member, duplicate, glob, trailing space, leading dash, \$, 51 items): BLOCKED_POLICY copy-resources, nothing written" || no "D15 SH8:$sh8bad"
# SH9 source not attached: PENDING_CONSENT, apply refused, nothing written
shf 9; rm -rf "$F/srcvol"; shrun --mode apply
{ [ "$(shst source-present)" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && [ "$(shst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && shnone; } && ok "D15 SH9 source folder absent (volume not attached): PENDING_CONSENT source-present, apply refused, nothing written" || no "D15 SH9: [$(shst source-present)] [$(shst apply-gate)]"
rm -rf "$F"
# SH10 symlinks on the SOURCE side: the chosen folder, an ancestor, an item, an entry inside an item -> refused, whole apply, nothing written (no item copied)
sh10bad=""
for sk in root ancestor item inner dirlink; do
  shf "10$sk"
  case "$sk" in
    root) mv "$F/srcvol/Resources" "$F/srcvol/Real"; ln -s Real "$F/srcvol/Resources";;
    ancestor) mkdir "$F/real"; mv "$F/srcvol" "$F/real/srcvol"; ln -s real/srcvol "$F/srcvol";;
    item) rm "$S/Tool.txt"; ln -s /etc/passwd "$S/Tool.txt";;
    inner) ln -s /etc/passwd "$S/Docs Pack/sub dir/link.txt";;
    dirlink) mkdir "$F/elsewhere"; printf 'x\n' > "$F/elsewhere/leak.txt"; ln -s ../../elsewhere "$S/Docs Pack/out";;
  esac
  shrun --mode apply; ok1=0; case "$sk" in root|ancestor) [ "$(shst source-trust)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] || [ "$(shst source-present)" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && ok1=1;; *) [ "$(shst item1)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] || [ "$(shst item2)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && ok1=1;; esac
  { [ "$ok1" -eq 1 ] && [ "$(shst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && shnone; } || sh10bad="$sh10bad $sk:$(shst source-trust)/$(shst item1)/$(shst item2)/$(shst apply-gate)/$(shnc)"
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$sh10bad" ] && ok "D15 SH10 a symlink on the source side (the chosen folder, an ancestor, an item, a file or a folder link inside an item) is refused: BLOCKED_POLICY, apply-gate refuses the WHOLE apply, nothing written, no item copied" || no "D15 SH10:$sh10bad"
# SH11 source entry modes and kinds: group/other writable, setuid, setgid, sticky, a FIFO -> refused, nothing written
sh11bad=""
for mk in gw ow suid sgid sticky fifo; do
  shf "11$mk"
  case "$mk" in gw) chmod 664 "$S/Docs Pack/a.txt";; ow) chmod 757 "$S/Docs Pack/sub dir";; suid) chmod 4755 "$S/Tool.txt";; sgid) chmod 2755 "$S/Docs Pack";; sticky) chmod 1755 "$S/Docs Pack/sub dir";; fifo) mkfifo "$S/Docs Pack/pipe";; esac
  shrun --mode apply
  { [ "$(shst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && { [ "$(shst item1)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] || [ "$(shst item2)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ]; } && shnone; } || sh11bad="$sh11bad $mk:$(shst item1)/$(shst item2)/$(shnc)"
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$sh11bad" ] && ok "D15 SH11 source entries that are group/other writable, setuid, setgid, sticky or not regular (FIFO) are refused: BLOCKED_POLICY, whole apply refused, nothing written" || no "D15 SH11:$sh11bad"
# SH12 a missing source item refuses the whole apply (the other item is not copied); a name outside the allowed characters inside an item is refused
shf 12 "Docs Pack:Missing.txt"; shrun --mode apply
{ [ "$(shst item2)" = FAILED ] && [ "$(shst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && shnone; } && ok "D15 SH12 an item missing in the source: FAILED for it, apply-gate refuses the whole apply, the present item is NOT copied" || no "D15 SH12: [$(shst item2)] [$(shst apply-gate)] calls=$(shnc)"
rm -rf "$F"; shf 12b; printf 'x\n' > "$S/Docs Pack/bad;name.txt"; chmod 644 "$S/Docs Pack/bad;name.txt"; printf 'x\n' > "$S/Docs Pack/tab"$'\t'"x.txt"; shrun --mode apply
{ [ "$(shst item1)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && shnone; } && ok "D15 SH12b an entry name with a character outside A-Za-z0-9 space . _ + - (semicolon, tab) is refused, nothing written" || no "D15 SH12b: [$(shst item1)]"
rm -rf "$F"
# SH13 caps (the constants are lowered in a fixture COPY of the module; SH13z pins the shipped values): entries, depth, per-file size, item total
sh13bad=""
for ck in entries depth file total; do
  shf "13$ck"; cp "$SUITE/$SHM" "$F/bin/$SHM"
  case "$ck" in entries) sed -i 's/^SH_MAX_ENTRIES=500;/SH_MAX_ENTRIES=3;/;s/SH_MAX_ENTRIES=500;/SH_MAX_ENTRIES=3;/' "$F/bin/$SHM";;
    depth) sed -i 's/SH_MAX_DEPTH=8;/SH_MAX_DEPTH=1;/' "$F/bin/$SHM";;
    file) sed -i 's/SH_MAX_FILE=67108864;/SH_MAX_FILE=3;/' "$F/bin/$SHM";;
    total) sed -i 's/SH_MAX_TOTAL=268435456$/SH_MAX_TOTAL=5/;s/SH_MAX_TOTAL=268435456;/SH_MAX_TOTAL=5;/' "$F/bin/$SHM";; esac
  cmp -s "$SUITE/$SHM" "$F/bin/$SHM" && sh13bad="$sh13bad $ck:sed-no-change"
  shrun --mode apply
  { [ "$(shst item1)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(shst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && shnone; } || sh13bad="$sh13bad $ck:$(shst item1)/$(shst apply-gate)/$(shnc)"
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$sh13bad" ] && ok "D15 SH13 size and count caps (entries, folder depth, per-file bytes, item total bytes, lowered in a fixture copy of the module): the item is BLOCKED_POLICY, the whole apply is refused, nothing written" || no "D15 SH13:$sh13bad"
grep -q '^SH_MAX_ITEMS=50; SH_MAX_ENTRIES=500; SH_MAX_DEPTH=8; SH_MAX_FILE=67108864; SH_MAX_TOTAL=268435456$' "$SUITE/$SHM" && ok "D15 SH13z the shipped caps are 50 items, 500 entries, depth 8, 64 MiB per file, 256 MiB per item" || no "D15 SH13z caps changed"
# SH14 conflict policy skip (default): a destination that differs is never touched, the other item is still copied, no backup
shf 14; mkdir "$F/Users/Shared/Docs Pack"; printf 'mine\n' > "$F/Users/Shared/Docs Pack/a.txt"; dsum=$(cksum < "$F/Users/Shared/Docs Pack/a.txt"); shrun --mode apply
{ [ "$(shst item1)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(shst item2)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ "$(cksum < "$F/Users/Shared/Docs Pack/a.txt")" = "$dsum" ] && [ "$(find "$F/Users/Shared/Docs Pack" | wc -l)" -eq 2 ] && [ "$(shbk)" -eq 0 ] && shmatch Tool.txt Tool.txt; } && ok "D15 SH14 default SHARED_CONFLICT=skip: a differing destination folder is BLOCKED_POLICY and untouched (bytes and names), no backup; the other item is copied" || no "D15 SH14: [$(shst item1)] [$(shst item2)] bk=$(shbk)"
rm -rf "$F"
# SH15 SHARED_CONFLICT=backup-replace: the existing item is MOVED into a unique 0700 backup folder, the new copy is verified in place, BACKUPDIR is reported
shf 15; shpol 'SHARED_CONFLICT=backup-replace'; mkdir "$F/Users/Shared/Docs Pack"; printf 'mine\n' > "$F/Users/Shared/Docs Pack/a.txt"; printf 'oldtool\n' > "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt"; shrun --mode apply
bkd=$(find "$SHBK" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type d | head -1)
{ [ "$(shst item1)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ "$(shst item2)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ "$(shbk)" -eq 2 ] && shmatch "Docs Pack" "Docs Pack" && shmatch Tool.txt Tool.txt \
  && grep -rl '^mine$' "$SHBK" | grep -q 'Docs Pack/a.txt$' && grep -rl '^oldtool$' "$SHBK" | grep -q 'Tool.txt$' && [ -z "$(find "$SHBK" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type d ! -perm 700)" ] && [ "$(stat -c %a "$SHBK")" = 700 ] \
  && printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q "${PTAB}backup=/" ; } && ok "D15 SH15 SHARED_CONFLICT=backup-replace: both existing items were moved whole into their own mode-0700 backup folders (old bytes intact), the new copies are verified in place, backup= (the folder) is reported" || no "D15 SH15: [$(shst item1)] [$(shst item2)] bk=$(shbk)"
# SH16 and a second run changes nothing (the destination now matches): no new backup
shrun --mode apply
{ [ "$(shst item1)" = ALREADY_CORRECT ] && [ "$(shst item2)" = ALREADY_CORRECT ] && [ "$(shbk)" -eq 2 ] && [ "$(shnc)" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "D15 SH16 after a replace, the next apply is ALREADY_CORRECT with no new backup and no cp, mv or chown call" || no "D15 SH16: bk=$(shbk) calls=$(shnc)"
rm -rf "$F"
# SH17 SHARED_CONFLICT with a bad value (replace, BACKUP-REPLACE, empty, overwrite) is refused for apply; an absent key means skip
sh17bad=""
for cv in replace BACKUP-REPLACE empty overwrite; do
  shf "17$cv"; case "$cv" in empty) shpol 'SHARED_CONFLICT=';; *) shpol "SHARED_CONFLICT=$cv";; esac; mkdir "$F/Users/Shared/Docs Pack"; shrun --mode apply
  { [ "$(shst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(shnc)" -eq 0 ] && [ -z "$(ls -A "$F/Users/Shared/Docs Pack")" ] && [ ! -e "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt" ]; } || sh17bad="$sh17bad $cv:$(shst apply-gate)/$(shnc)"
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$sh17bad" ] && ok "D15 SH17 a SHARED_CONFLICT value that is not skip or backup-replace (replace, BACKUP-REPLACE, empty, overwrite) refuses apply: nothing copied, nothing replaced" || no "D15 SH17:$sh17bad"
# SH18 destination objects: a symlink at the item name (to a file elsewhere) is never followed or replaced; a link or special object inside a destination folder is bad; backup-replace does not override this
sh18bad=""
for dk in link dirlink inner; do
  shf "18$dk"; shpol 'SHARED_CONFLICT=backup-replace'; mkdir "$F/elsewhere"; printf 'victim\n' > "$F/elsewhere/v.txt"; vsum=$(cksum < "$F/elsewhere/v.txt")
  case "$dk" in link) ln -s ../../elsewhere/v.txt "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt";; dirlink) ln -s ../../elsewhere "$F/Users/Shared/Docs Pack";;
    inner) shcopy "Docs Pack"; ln -s ../../../elsewhere/v.txt "$F/Users/Shared/Docs Pack/evil";; esac
  shrun --mode apply
  c=$(printf '%s %s' "$(shst item1)" "$(shst item2)")
  case "$dk" in link) want="CHANGED_VERIFIED BLOCKED_POLICY";; dirlink) want="BLOCKED_POLICY CHANGED_VERIFIED";; inner) want="BLOCKED_POLICY CHANGED_VERIFIED";; esac
  { [ "$c" = "$want" ] && [ "$(cksum < "$F/elsewhere/v.txt")" = "$vsum" ] && [ "$(shbk)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(find "$F/elsewhere" -type f | wc -l)" -eq 1 ]; } || sh18bad="$sh18bad $dk:$c"
  case "$dk" in link) [ -L "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt" ] || sh18bad="$sh18bad $dk:link-gone";; dirlink) [ -L "$F/Users/Shared/Docs Pack" ] || sh18bad="$sh18bad $dk:link-gone";; inner) [ -L "$F/Users/Shared/Docs Pack/evil" ] || sh18bad="$sh18bad $dk:link-gone";; esac
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$sh18bad" ] && ok "D15 SH18 destination symlinks (at the item name to a file or a folder, or inside a destination folder) are BLOCKED_POLICY: never followed, never replaced, never backed up, the link target is byte-for-byte unchanged (also under backup-replace)" || no "D15 SH18:$sh18bad"
# SH19 failure injection: every step that can fail leaves the destination as it was and reports FAILED (named), later items "not attempted"
sh19bad=""
for fk in cp.fail cp.corrupt chown.fail mv.failstage cp.mutsrc cp.race cp.race2 chmod.noop; do
  case "$fk" in cp.race2) shf "19$fk" Tool.txt;; *) shf "19$fk";; esac; [ "$fk" != chmod.noop ] || chmod 640 "$S/Tool.txt" "$S/Docs Pack/a.txt"; case "$fk" in cp.race2) printf 'Tool.txt\n' > "$F/data/cp.race";; esac; case "$fk" in cp.mutsrc) printf '%s\n' "$S/Docs Pack/a.txt" > "$F/data/cp.mutsrc";; cp.race) printf 'Docs Pack\n' > "$F/data/cp.race";; cp.race2) ;; *) : > "$F/data/$fk";; esac
  shrun --mode apply
  c=$(printf '%s %s' "$(shst item1)" "$(shst item2)")
  case "$fk" in
    cp.race) want="FAILED FAILED"; { [ -e "$F/Users/Shared/Docs Pack" ] && [ "$(cat "$F/Users/Shared/Docs Pack")" = raced ]; } || sh19bad="$sh19bad $fk:raced-item-changed";;
    cp.race2) want="FAILED "; { [ -f "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt" ] && [ "$(cat "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt")" = raced ]; } || sh19bad="$sh19bad $fk:raced-file-overwritten";;
    cp.mutsrc) want="FAILED FAILED"; [ ! -e "$F/Users/Shared/Docs Pack" ] || sh19bad="$sh19bad $fk:dst-written";;
    *) want="FAILED FAILED"; [ -z "$(ls -A "$F/Users/Shared")" ] || sh19bad="$sh19bad $fk:dst-written";;
  esac
  [ "$c" = "$want" ] || sh19bad="$sh19bad $fk:$c"
  case "$fk" in cp.corrupt|cp.mutsrc) wt='staged copy does not match the source';; chmod.noop) wt='staged copy has the wrong owner or mode';; cp.race|cp.race2) wt='something appeared at the destination';; cp.fail) wt='copy into the staging folder failed';; chown.fail) wt='explicit owner could not be set';; mv.failstage) wt='rename into the destination failed';; esac
  printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q "$wt" || sh19bad="$sh19bad $fk:text-[$wt]-missing"
  [ "$(shstage)" -eq 0 ] || sh19bad="$sh19bad $fk:stage-left"
  [ "$(shbk)" -eq 0 ] || sh19bad="$sh19bad $fk:backup"
  [ "$RC" -eq 3 ] || sh19bad="$sh19bad $fk:rc=$RC"
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$sh19bad" ] && ok "D15 SH19 a failing cp, a staged copy that differs, a failing chown, a failing rename, a chmod that silently does nothing (the staged modes are checked BEFORE the rename), a source that changes during the copy, or a destination item (a folder, or a plain file that a rename would silently replace) that appears right before the rename: FAILED (exit 3), the first item FAILED and the next 'not attempted', nothing placed or overwritten, staging folder removed" || no "D15 SH19:$sh19bad"
# SH19b the source is re-checked right before each copy: item 2 becomes a symlink after item 1 was copied -> item 1 CHANGED_VERIFIED, item 2 FAILED "no longer safe", nothing of item 2 in the destination
shf 19b; printf '%s\n' "$S/Tool.txt" > "$F/data/cp.unsafe2"; shrun --mode apply
{ [ "$(shst item1)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ "$(shst item2)" = FAILED ] && printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'no longer safe' && [ ! -e "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt" ] && [ ! -L "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt" ] && [ "$RC" -eq 3 ]; } && ok "D15 SH19b the source is checked again right before each copy: an item that turned into a symlink after the plan is FAILED 'no longer safe' and nothing of it reaches the destination" || no "D15 SH19b: [$(shst item1)] [$(shst item2)] rc=$RC"
rm -rf "$F"
# SH19c (B-1, d15 draft4): a local user plants the item name in /Users/Shared AFTER the last check (the doubles plant it at the first mv, link or
# mkdir aimed at that name): a symlink to a folder, a symlink to a file, a dangling symlink; for a file item and a folder item; and once under
# backup-replace (the name is planted right after the verified backup freed it). Nothing may land behind the link, the link and its target
# stay byte-for-byte as they were, the item is FAILED (exit 3), no staging is left; under backup-replace the old item stays in the backup.
sh19cbad=""; n19c=0
for it in Tool.txt "Docs Pack" bk; do for k in dirlink filelink dangling; do
  [ "$it" != bk ] || [ "$k" = dirlink ] || continue
  n19c=$((n19c+1)); case "$it" in bk) shf "19c$k$n19c" Tool.txt; shpol 'SHARED_CONFLICT=backup-replace'; printf 'oldtool\n' > "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt"; nm=Tool.txt;; *) shf "19c$k$n19c" "$it"; nm=$it;; esac
  mkdir -p "$F/elsewhere"; printf 'v\n' > "$F/elsewhere/v.txt"; vsum=$(cksum < "$F/elsewhere/v.txt")
  case "$k" in dirlink) to="$F/elsewhere";; filelink) to="$F/elsewhere/v.txt";; dangling) to="$F/elsewhere/nothing";; esac
  printf '%s\n' "$nm" > "$F/data/race.at"; printf '%s\n' "$to" > "$F/data/race.to"
  shrun --mode apply; tg="$it/$k"
  [ -f "$F/data/race.done" ] || sh19cbad="$sh19cbad $tg:race-not-fired"
  [ "$(shst item1)" = FAILED ] || sh19cbad="$sh19cbad $tg:status=$(shst item1)"
  [ "$RC" -eq 3 ] || sh19cbad="$sh19cbad $tg:rc=$RC"
  { [ -L "$F/Users/Shared/$nm" ] && [ "$(readlink "$F/Users/Shared/$nm")" = "$to" ]; } || sh19cbad="$sh19cbad $tg:link-changed"
  { [ "$(cd "$F/elsewhere" && find . | sort | tr '\n' ' ')" = ". ./v.txt " ] && [ "$(cksum < "$F/elsewhere/v.txt")" = "$vsum" ]; } || sh19cbad="$sh19cbad $tg:written-behind-link"
  printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'something appeared at the destination right before the rename' || sh19cbad="$sh19cbad $tg:text"
  [ "$(shstage)" -eq 0 ] || sh19cbad="$sh19cbad $tg:stage-left"
  case "$it" in bk) { [ "$(shbk)" -eq 1 ] && grep -rl '^oldtool$' "$SHBK" | grep -q 'Tool.txt$'; } || sh19cbad="$sh19cbad $tg:backup-lost";; *) [ "$(shbk)" -eq 0 ] || sh19cbad="$sh19cbad $tg:backup";; esac
  rm -rf "$F"; done; done
[ -z "$sh19cbad" ] && [ "$n19c" -eq 7 ] && ok "D15 SH19c B-1 race after the last check ($n19c cases: a symlink to a folder, to a file, a dangling symlink, for a file and a folder item, and under backup-replace): the planted link is never followed or replaced, nothing lands behind it, its target is unchanged, the item is FAILED (exit 3), no staging left, a replaced item stays in the backup" || no "D15 SH19c:$sh19cbad n=$n19c"
# SH20 stop on failure: item 1 fails -> item 2 is reported 'not attempted', and no cp ran for it
shf 20; : > "$F/data/chown.fail"; shrun --mode apply
{ [ "$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -c 'not attempted')" -eq 1 ] && [ "$(grep -c '^cp ' "$F/shcalls.log")" -eq 1 ]; } && ok "D15 SH20 stop on the first failed item: the next item is FAILED 'not attempted' and its cp never ran" || no "D15 SH20: cp=$(grep -c '^cp ' "$F/shcalls.log")"
rm -rf "$F"
# SH21 backup-replace with a failing backup: the existing item is untouched and the new copy is NOT placed; with a failing final rename the old item is put back
shf 21a; shpol 'SHARED_CONFLICT=backup-replace'; printf 'oldtool\n' > "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt"; : > "$F/data/mv.failbk"; shrun --mode apply
{ [ "$(shst item2)" = FAILED ] && [ "$(cat "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt")" = oldtool ] && [ "$(shbk)" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "D15 SH21 backup-replace whose backup move fails: FAILED, the existing item keeps its bytes and place, the new copy is not placed, no backup folder is left" || no "D15 SH21: [$(shst item2)] [$(cat "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt")] bk=$(shbk)"
rm -rf "$F"; shf 21b Tool.txt; shpol 'SHARED_CONFLICT=backup-replace'; printf 'oldtool\n' > "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt"; : > "$F/data/mv.failstage"; shrun --mode apply
{ [ "$(shst item1)" = FAILED ] && [ "$(cat "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt")" = oldtool ] && [ -d "$SHBK" ]; } && ok "D15 SH21b backup-replace whose final rename fails: FAILED, the old item is put back (old bytes at the old place)" || no "D15 SH21b: [$(shst item1)] [$(cat "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt" 2>/dev/null)]"
rm -rf "$F"
# SH22 a result that does not match the source after the rename: FAILED, nothing rolled back automatically, backup kept
shf 22 "Tool.txt"; shpol 'SHARED_CONFLICT=backup-replace'; printf 'oldtool\n' > "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt"; : > "$F/data/mv.corrupt"; shrun --mode apply
{ [ "$(shst item1)" = FAILED ] && [ "$(shbk)" -eq 1 ] && grep -rl '^oldtool$' "$SHBK" | grep -q 'Tool.txt$'; } && ok "D15 SH22 a destination that does not match the source after the rename: FAILED (result mismatch), the replaced item stays in the verified backup" || no "D15 SH22: [$(shst item1)] bk=$(shbk)"
rm -rf "$F"
# SH23 /Users/Shared and the policy folder: a symlinked destination, a group/other-writable destination without the sticky bit -> refused; the sticky default 1777 works
sh23bad=""
for dk in link open sticky; do
  shf "23$dk"
  case "$dk" in link) rm -rf "$F/Users/Shared"; mkdir "$F/Users/Real"; ln -s Real "$F/Users/Shared";; open) chmod 777 "$F/Users/Shared";; sticky) chmod 1777 "$F/Users/Shared";; esac
  shrun --mode apply
  if [ "$dk" = sticky ]; then { [ "$(shst item1)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ "$(shst item2)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ]; } || sh23bad="$sh23bad $dk:$(shst item1)"
  else { [ "$(shst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(shnc)" -eq 0 ]; } || sh23bad="$sh23bad $dk:$(shst apply-gate)/$(shnc)"; fi
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$sh23bad" ] && ok "D15 SH23 destination folder: a symlinked /Users/Shared and a 0777 one without the sticky bit refuse apply (nothing written); the macOS default 1777 works" || no "D15 SH23:$sh23bad"
# SH24 reports: the records follow wsp-report/1, name no source path, no fixture path, and carry only normalized text; BACKUPDIR is a path-class value
shf 24; shpol 'SHARED_CONFLICT=backup-replace'; printf 'oldtool\n' > "$F/Users/Shared/Tool.txt"; shrun --mode apply
nrec=$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -c "^WSPREC${PTAB}\|${PTAB}module=shared${PTAB}") || nrec=0
{ [ "$nrec" -ge 4 ] && ! printf '%s\n%s\n' "$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | sed 's/'"$PTAB"'backup=[^'"$PTAB"']*//')" "$(cat "$F/stderr")" | grep -q "srcvol\|$F" && ! printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q '\[unrecognized' ; } && ok "D15 SH24 reports: wsp-report/1 records for module shared, no source path and no fixture path in stdout or stderr, no un-normalized detail" || no "D15 SH24: records=$nrec"
rm -rf "$F"
# SH25 units on the source walker with doubles for the library readers: owner, ACL, mode bits, size and count caps, kinds (the ACL and owner readers cannot be faked in a fixture folder)
sed -n '/^sh_owner_ok() /,/^# ---- policy ----$/p' "$SUITE/$SHM" | sed '$d' > "$T/sh-unit-fn.sh"
cat > "$T/sh-unit.sh" <<'W'
set -u
WSP_OPERATOR=workstationadmin; WSP_FIXTURE=1
SH_MAX_ITEMS=50; SH_MAX_ENTRIES=3; SH_MAX_DEPTH=8; SH_MAX_FILE=100; SH_MAX_TOTAL=150
U_OWNER=root; U_MODE=644; U_SIZE=10; U_ACL=0
wsp_fl_owner_ok() { [ "$1" = root ]; }
wsp_stat_owner() { printf '%s' "$U_OWNER"; }
wsp_stat_mode() { printf '%s' "$U_MODE"; }
wsp_stat_size() { printf '%s' "$U_SIZE"; }
wsp_stat_group() { printf wheel; }
wsp_no_acl() { [ "$U_ACL" = 0 ]; }
wsp_mode_not_gw() { case "$1" in *[2367]?|*[2367]) return 1;; esac; return 0; }
. "$T/sh-unit-fn.sh"
bad=""; n=0
chk() { n=$((n+1)); [ "$2" = "$3" ] || bad="$bad $1:got=$3,want=$2"; }
tc() { SH_N=0; SH_BYTES=0; SH_WHY=""; sh_check_one "$1"; printf '%s' "$?"; }
mkdir -p "$T/shu"; : > "$T/shu/f"; mkdir -p "$T/shu/d"; ln -sf f "$T/shu/l"; mkfifo "$T/shu/p" 2>/dev/null
chk file-ok 0 "$(tc "$T/shu/f")"; chk dir-ok 0 "$(tc "$T/shu/d")"
chk symlink 1 "$(tc "$T/shu/l")"; chk fifo 1 "$(tc "$T/shu/p")"; chk missing 1 "$(tc "$T/shu/none")"
U_OWNER=stranger; chk owner-stranger 1 "$(tc "$T/shu/f")"; U_OWNER=workstationadmin; chk owner-operator-ok 0 "$(tc "$T/shu/f")"; U_OWNER=root
U_ACL=1; chk acl 1 "$(tc "$T/shu/f")"; U_ACL=0
for m in 664 646 666 4755 2755 1755 4644; do U_MODE=$m; chk "mode-$m" 1 "$(tc "$T/shu/f")"; done; U_MODE=644
U_SIZE=101; chk size-file 1 "$(tc "$T/shu/f")"; U_SIZE=100; chk size-file-edge 0 "$(tc "$T/shu/f")"; U_SIZE=10
SH_N=0; SH_BYTES=140; SH_WHY=""; U_SIZE=20; sh_check_one "$T/shu/f"; chk size-total 1 "$?"; U_SIZE=10
SH_N=3; SH_BYTES=0; sh_check_one "$T/shu/f"; chk entries-cap 1 "$?"
SH_N=2; SH_BYTES=0; sh_check_one "$T/shu/f"; chk entries-edge 0 "$?"
chk srcok-abs 0 "$(sh_src_ok /srcvol/Some_Folder-1.x+y; printf '%s' $?)"
chk srcok-space 0 "$(sh_src_ok '/Volumes/My Disk/Pack'; printf '%s' $?)"
for p in rel /a/../b /a//b /a/ '/a/b*' /Users/Shared /Users/Shared/a /Library/Management/x /System/Library /Users 'a b' ''; do chk "src-$p" 1 "$(sh_src_ok "$p"; printf '%s' $?)"; done
chk items-ok 0 "$(sh_items_ok 'A b:c.d:e_f+g'; printf '%s' $?)"
for i in '' ':' 'a:' ':a' 'a::b' 'a:a' '.a' '-a' 'a/b' 'a*' 'a;b' ' ' 'a '; do chk "item-$i" 1 "$(sh_items_ok "$i"; printf '%s' $?)"; done
chk item-leading-space-kept 0 "$(sh_items_ok ' Pinned:b'; printf '%s' $?)"
# d15 draft6 (reviewer FINAL MUTATION, sh-dst-dir-link): sh_dst_dir_ok with the readers doubled (owner root, mode 1777, no ACL, /Users
# trusted), so only its [ ! -L ] check can refuse: a real folder passes (control), a symlink to it is refused. On GNU a link reads 777 (the
