# mode check refuses it first in SH23); on macOS a root-made link reads 0755 and only -L refuses it.
wsp_trusted_dir() { return 0; }; WSP_ROOT=$T   # sh_dst_dir_ok passes "$WSP_ROOT/Users" to the (doubled) trusted-dir check; set -u needs it set
mkdir -p "$T/shu/dreal"; ln -s dreal "$T/shu/dlink"; U_MODE=1777
dst="$T/shu/dreal"; chk dstdir-real 0 "$(sh_dst_dir_ok; printf '%s' $?)"
dst="$T/shu/dlink"; chk dstdir-link 1 "$(sh_dst_dir_ok; printf '%s' $?)"; U_MODE=644
if [ -z "$bad" ]; then echo "UNIT-OK $n"; else echo "UNIT-BAD$bad"; fi
W
ub=$(cd "$T" && T="$T" bash "$T/sh-unit.sh" 2>&1); case "$ub" in UNIT-OK*) ok "D15 SH25 source-walker units (library readers doubled): a symlink, FIFO or missing path, a foreign owner, an ACL, group/other-writable, setuid, setgid and sticky modes, a file over the cap, an item over the total cap and an entry over the count cap are refused; the edge values are accepted; the path and item-name grammars accept plain names and refuse the 24 unsafe forms; sh_dst_dir_ok accepts a real folder and refuses a symlink to it with every other check passing (${ub#UNIT-OK })";; *) no "D15 SH25 units: $ub";; esac
rm -rf "$T/shu" "$T/sh-unit.sh" "$T/sh-unit-fn.sh"
# SH26 text assertions for module 4: the copy is a real copy (cp -R -P, never a dereference), every cp has -P, owner and mode are explicit, no recursive delete, no link creation, no sudo, no eval; the order is source re-check, copy, compare, modes, owner, compare, backup, rename, final compare
shc="$T/sh-code.txt"; grep -v '^ *#' "$SUITE/$SHM" > "$shc"
nbad=$(grep -c -E 'ln -s|rsync|ditto|sudo|[e]val |rm -r|rm -f|chmod -R|chmod [0-7]*[0-7][0-7][2367] |cp -[a-zA-Z]*[LH]|cp [^-]|chown -R [^-]|chown [^-]' "$shc") || nbad=0
ncp=$(grep -c -E '(^|[^a-z_])cp ' "$shc"); ncpp=$(grep -c -E '(^|[^a-z_])cp -R -P ' "$shc")
nchown=$(grep -c 'chown -R -h root:wheel "\$_st"' "$shc"); nrmx=$(grep -c 'wsp_backup_discard' "$shc"); nrmd=$(grep -c 'wsp_backup_discard "\$STAGE"' "$shc")
l1=$(grep -n 'sh_src_item "\$_s" ||' "$shc" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1); l2=$(grep -n 'cp -R -P ' "$shc" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1); l3=$(grep -n 'sh_cmp "\$_s" "\$_st" 0' "$shc" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1); l4=$(grep -n 'sh_fix "\$_st"' "$shc" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
l5=$(grep -n 'chown -R -h' "$shc" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1); l6=$(grep -n 'sh_cmp "\$_s" "\$_st" 1' "$shc" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1); l7=$(grep -n 'wsp_backup_move' "$shc" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1); l8=$(grep -n 'wsp_rename_nofollow "\$_st" "\$dst/\$_it"' "$shc" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1); l9=$(grep -n 'if sh_cmp "\$_s" "\$dst/\$_it" 1' "$shc" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
{ [ "$nbad" -eq 0 ] && [ "$ncp" -eq 1 ] && [ "$ncpp" -eq 1 ] && [ "$nchown" -eq 1 ] && [ "$nrmx" -eq 1 ] && [ "$nrmd" -eq 1 ] && [ -n "$l1" ] && [ -n "$l9" ] && [ "$l1" -lt "$l2" ] && [ "$l2" -lt "$l3" ] && [ "$l3" -lt "$l4" ] && [ "$l4" -lt "$l5" ] && [ "$l5" -lt "$l6" ] && [ "$l6" -lt "$l7" ] && [ "$l7" -lt "$l8" ] && [ "$l8" -lt "$l9" ]; } && ok "D15 SH26 module 4 text: one cp (cp -R -P), one explicit chown -R -h root:wheel, no link creation, rsync, ditto, sudo, eval, rm, recursive chmod or world-writable chmod; the only delete helper is the staging folder; order: source re-check, copy, compare, explicit modes, explicit owner, compare, backup, rename, final compare" || no "D15 SH26: bad=$nbad cp=$ncp/$ncpp chown=$nchown rm=$nrmx/$nrmd order=$l1,$l2,$l3,$l4,$l5,$l6,$l7,$l8,$l9"
rm -f "$shc"
# SH27 the disabled-module list no longer has shared (d16: nor apps, module 5); admin-session stays disabled
{ ! grep -q 'wsp_disable_apply' "$SUITE/$SHM" && ! grep -q 'wsp_disable_apply' "$SUITE/workstationadmin-mod-apps.txt" && grep -q '^wsp_disable_apply ' "$SUITE/workstationadmin-mod-admin-session.txt" && ! grep -q 'defaults write\|launchctl\|dscl\|softwareupdate' "$SUITE/$SHM"; } && ok "D15 SH27 module 4 has no wsp_disable_apply and no defaults write, launchctl, dscl or softwareupdate; d16: apps (module 5) no longer calls wsp_disable_apply; admin-session still does" || no "D15 SH27"
# SH28 the apply gate runs inside wsp_parse_mode before any copy, and the module asks no question of its own
{ grep -q '^wsp_parse_mode "\$@"' "$SUITE/$SHM" && ! grep -q 'read -r\|wsp_confirm\|/dev/tty' "$SUITE/$SHM" && grep -q '^WSP_GATE_NEEDS=root' "$SUITE/$SHM"; } && ok "D15 SH28 module 4 declares WSP_GATE_NEEDS=root and calls wsp_parse_mode (the apply gate) before any code that copies; it reads nothing from the terminal (the typed APPLY is the launcher's, the consent key is the policy's)" || no "D15 SH28"
# ---- d15 harness change: pause-aware limits (stall_total, ptimeout). Synthetic ticker files only; the real ticker is not touched. ----
# SH29 the launcher's typed APPLY: with no terminal (the sandbox has no controlling terminal) the launcher refuses apply (exit 4) before any module runs; nothing is copied. The positive path needs a real terminal (Mac item).
shf 29; shrun --mode apply; : > "$F/shcalls.log"; SNAP0=$(shsnap); run workstationadmin-provision.txt --mode apply
{ [ "$RC" -eq 4 ] && shnone && printf '%s\n' "$(cat "$F/stderr")" | grep -q 'not confirmed'; } && ok "D15 SH29 launcher --mode apply without a terminal: refused with exit 4 ('apply not confirmed'), no module ran, nothing copied (the typed APPLY is the launcher's own /dev/tty prompt)" || no "D15 SH29: rc=$RC calls=$(shnc) err=[$(head -c 200 "$F/stderr")]"
rm -rf "$F"
mark "D15 harness pause-aware"
for pttry in 1 2 3; do pa0=$(date +%s)
  pt0=$SECONDS; : > "$T/pt-one.log"; printf '%s\n' "$(date +%s)" > "$T/pt-one.log"
  PT_TICKER="$T/pt-one.log" ptimeout 2 bash -c 'sleep 30' >/dev/null 2>&1; ptr=$?; ptd=$((SECONDS-pt0))
  [ "$pttry" -lt 3 ] && pt_stalled "$pa0" && { echo "# DIAG H1 try $pttry: the test host paused during the test, running it again"; continue; }; break
done
{ [ "$ptr" -eq 124 ] && [ "$ptd" -le 12 ]; } && ok "D15 H1 ptimeout: a hung command (no stall in the ticker) is stopped after the 2 s limit with exit 124 (took ${ptd}s)" || no "D15 H1: rc=$ptr took=${ptd}s"
pto=$(printf 'x\n' | PT_TICKER="$T/pt-one.log" ptimeout 5 bash -c 'cat; echo hi >&2; exit 7' 2>&1); ptr=$?
{ [ "$ptr" -eq 7 ] && [ "$pto" = "$(printf 'x\nhi')" ]; } && ok "D15 H2 ptimeout passes stdin, stdout, stderr and the exit status of the command through" || no "D15 H2: rc=$ptr out=[$pto]"
for pttry in 1 2 3; do pa0=$(date +%s)
  e0=$(date +%s); printf '%s\n%s\n%s\n' "$((e0-1))" "$((e0+99))" "$((e0+100))" > "$T/pt-gap.log"; pt0=$SECONDS
  PT_TICKER="$T/pt-gap.log" ptimeout 2 bash -c 'sleep 4' >/dev/null 2>&1; ptr=$?; ptd=$((SECONDS-pt0))
  [ "$pttry" -lt 3 ] && pt_stalled "$pa0" && { echo "# DIAG H3 try $pttry: the test host paused during the test, running it again"; continue; }; break
done
{ [ "$ptr" -eq 0 ] && [ "$ptd" -ge 3 ] && [ "$ptd" -le 12 ]; } && ok "D15 H3 ptimeout credits a ticker gap inside the run window: a 4 s command with a 2 s limit and a 98 s stall in the ticker is NOT killed (the limit counts active time)" || no "D15 H3: rc=$ptr took=${ptd}s"
for pttry in 1 2 3; do pa0=$(date +%s)
  e0=$(date +%s); printf '%s\n' "$((e0-10))" > "$T/pt-late.log"; pt0=$SECONDS
  ( sleep 3; printf '%s\n' "$((e0+60))" >> "$T/pt-late.log" ) & ptw=$!
  PT_TICKER="$T/pt-late.log" ptimeout 2 bash -c 'sleep 6' >/dev/null 2>&1; ptr=$?; ptd=$((SECONDS-pt0)); wait "$ptw" 2>/dev/null
  [ "$pttry" -lt 3 ] && pt_stalled "$pa0" && { echo "# DIAG H6 try $pttry: the test host paused during the test, running it again"; continue; }; break
done
{ [ "$ptr" -eq 0 ] && [ "$ptd" -ge 5 ] && [ "$ptd" -le 14 ]; } && ok "D15 H6 (triage) ptimeout waits for the tick that closes a gap: the limit is reached while the last tick is 12 s old, the next tick (70 s gap) arrives 1 s later and is credited, the 6 s command is NOT killed" || no "D15 H6: rc=$ptr took=${ptd}s"
for pttry in 1 2 3; do pa0=$(date +%s)
  e0=$(date +%s); printf '%s\n' "$((e0-10))" > "$T/pt-dead.log"; pt0=$SECONDS
  PT_TICKER="$T/pt-dead.log" ptimeout 2 bash -c 'sleep 30' >/dev/null 2>&1; ptr=$?; ptd=$((SECONDS-pt0))
  [ "$pttry" -lt 3 ] && pt_stalled "$pa0" && { echo "# DIAG H6b try $pttry: the test host paused during the test, running it again"; continue; }; break
done
{ [ "$ptr" -eq 124 ] && [ "$ptd" -le 12 ]; } && ok "D15 H6b (triage) a dead ticker (last tick 12 s old, no new tick) earns no credit: the hung command is still stopped with exit 124 (took ${ptd}s)" || no "D15 H6b: rc=$ptr took=${ptd}s"
printf '1\n2\n3\n4\n20\n21\n' > "$T/pt-u1.log"; printf '1\n2\n7\n' > "$T/pt-u2.log"; printf '1\n2\n' > "$T/pt-u3.log"; printf '1\n8\n9\n30\n' > "$T/pt-u4.log"
u1=$(PT_TICKER="$T/pt-u1.log" stall_total); u2=$(PT_TICKER="$T/pt-u2.log" stall_total); u3=$(PT_TICKER="$T/pt-u3.log" stall_total); u4=$(PT_TICKER="$T/pt-u4.log" stall_total)
{ [ "$u1" = 15 ] && [ "$u2" = 0 ] && [ "$u3" = 0 ] && [ "$u4" = 26 ]; } && ok "D15 H4 stall_total: a 16 s gap earns 15 s of credit, a 5 s gap none, a missing trailing tick none (a dead ticker earns no credit), two gaps add up (6 + 20 = 26)" || no "D15 H4: $u1 $u2 $u3 $u4"
rm -f "$T"/pt-*.log
hb=$(sed -n '/^mark() {/,/^mkdir -p "\$T\/fx0"/p' "$SUITE/workstationadmin-test-harness.txt")
printf '%s\n' "$hb" | grep -q 'SECONDS-_st' && printf '%s\n' "$hb" | grep -q '_act" -gt "\$HARNESS_BUDGET"' && ! printf '%s\n' "$hb" | grep -q '"\$SECONDS" -gt "\$HARNESS_BUDGET"' && [ "$(grep -c 'timeout -k 5 "\$RUN_LIMIT"' "$SUITE/workstationadmin-test-harness.txt")" -eq 0 ] && ok "D15 H5 text: the budget compares ACTIVE time (SECONDS minus stall_total) and no per-run limit uses the plain timeout any more (every site goes through ptimeout)" || no "D15 H5"
mark "D15 end"
mark "D16 module 5"
# ---- module 5 (d16): app install from a LOCAL signed .pkg or .dmg, Team ID allowlist (S-2). Fixtures and doubles only: pkgutil, spctl,
# codesign, installer, hdiutil, ditto, cp and chown are doubles in $F/stubs, driven by key=value lines INSIDE the fixture package files
# (sig= team= notary= chain= spctl= app= appteam= appsig= img=) and by data/ flags; each call is logged (name and basename) to apcalls.log.
# An app bundle double is a folder with Contents/fx holding team= and sig=. No real package, image or signature is ever touched.
APM=workstationadmin-mod-apps.txt
apstubs() {
  rm -f "$F/stubs/pkgutil" "$F/stubs/spctl" "$F/stubs/codesign" "$F/stubs/installer" "$F/stubs/hdiutil" "$F/stubs/ditto" "$F/stubs/cp" "$F/stubs/chown"   # several are links to the generic _stub: never write through them
  cat > "$F/stubs/pkgutil" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; p=${!#}; printf 'pkgutil %s %s\n' "$1" "${p##*/}" >> "$r/apcalls.log"
[ "$1" = --check-signature ] && [ -f "$p" ] || exit 1
kv() { /usr/bin/sed -n "s/^$1=//p" "$p" | /usr/bin/head -1; }
s=$(kv sig); t=$(kv team); n=$(kv notary); c=$(kv chain)
case "$s" in
  apple) printf 'Package "%s":\n   Status: signed by a developer certificate issued by Apple for distribution\n' "${p##*/}";;
  other) printf 'Package "%s":\n   Status: signed by a certificate trusted by Mac OS X\n' "${p##*/}";;
  *) printf 'Package "%s":\n   Status: no signature\n' "${p##*/}"; exit 1;;
esac
[ "$n" = no ] || printf '   Notarization: trusted by the Apple notary service\n'
printf '   Signed with a trusted timestamp on: 2026-01-01 00:00:00 +0000\n   Certificate Chain:\n'
printf '    1. Developer ID Installer: Example Vendor LLC (%s)\n       Expires: 2030-01-01 00:00:00 +0000\n' "$t"
[ "$c" != twoleaf ] || printf '    1. Developer ID Installer: Other Example Vendor (%s)\n' "$t"
printf '    2. Developer ID Certification Authority\n'
[ "$c" = noroot ] || printf '    3. Apple Root CA\n'
x=$(kv rc); exit "${x:-0}"
W
  cat > "$F/stubs/spctl" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; p=${!#}; printf 'spctl %s %s %s\n' "$2" "$3" "${p##*/}" >> "$r/apcalls.log"
if [ -d "$p" ]; then f="$p/Contents/fx"; else f="$p"; fi
[ -f "$f" ] || exit 3
/usr/bin/grep -qx 'spctl=no' "$f" && exit 3
exit 0
W
  cat > "$F/stubs/codesign" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; p=${!#}; req=""; for a in "$@"; do case "$a" in -R=*) req=${a#-R=};; esac; done
if [ -n "$req" ]; then k=req; else k=noreq; fi; printf 'codesign %s %s\n' "$k" "${p##*/}" >> "$r/apcalls.log"
[ ! -L "$p" ] || exit 1
if [ -d "$p" ]; then f="$p/Contents/fx"; else f="$p"; fi
[ -f "$f" ] || exit 1
sig=$(/usr/bin/sed -n 's/^sig=//p' "$f" | /usr/bin/head -1); team=$(/usr/bin/sed -n 's/^team=//p' "$f" | /usr/bin/head -1)
[ "$sig" = good ] || exit 3
if [ -n "$req" ]; then [ "$req" = "anchor apple generic and certificate 1[field.1.2.840.113635.100.6.2.6] exists and certificate leaf[field.1.2.840.113635.100.6.1.13] exists and certificate leaf[subject.OU] = \"$team\"" ] || exit 3; fi   # d16 draft2: S-1 OIDs
case "$p" in */mnt.*/*.app) if [ -f "$r/data/codesign.term" ]; then q=$PPID; t=""; while [ -n "$q" ] && [ "$q" -gt 1 ]; do case "$(/usr/bin/tr '\0' ' ' < "/proc/$q/cmdline" 2>/dev/null)" in "/bin/bash "*/workstationadmin-mod-apps.txt*) t=$q;; esac; q=$(/usr/bin/awk '/^PPid:/{print $2}' "/proc/$q/status" 2>/dev/null); done; [ -z "$t" ] || { kill -TERM "$t" && : > "$r/data/term.sent"; }; fi;; esac   # d17 SUG-2: TERM while the image is attached
exit 0
W
  cat > "$F/stubs/installer" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; p=""; t=""
while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do case "$1" in -pkg) p=$2; shift;; -target) t=$2; shift;; esac; shift; done
printf 'installer -pkg %s -target %s\n' "${p##*/}" "$t" >> "$r/apcalls.log"
[ ! -f "$r/data/installer.fail" ] || exit 1
a=$(/usr/bin/sed -n 's/^app=//p' "$p" | /usr/bin/head -1); [ -n "$a" ] || exit 1
/usr/bin/mkdir -p "$r/Applications/$a.app/Contents/MacOS"
printf 'team=%s\nsig=%s\n' "$(/usr/bin/sed -n 's/^appteam=//p' "$p")" "$(/usr/bin/sed -n 's/^appsig=//p' "$p")" > "$r/Applications/$a.app/Contents/fx"
echo 'installer: The install was successful.'
W
  cat > "$F/stubs/hdiutil" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; v=$1; shift
case "$v" in
  attach) m=""; ro=0; nb=0; img=${!#}
    while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do case "$1" in -readonly) ro=1;; -nobrowse) nb=1;; -mountpoint) m=$2; shift;; esac; shift; done
    printf 'hdiutil attach ro=%s nobrowse=%s\n' "$ro" "$nb" >> "$r/apcalls.log"
    [ ! -f "$r/data/hdiutil.attachfail" ] && [ -d "$m" ] || exit 1
    d=$(/usr/bin/sed -n 's/^img=//p' "$img" | /usr/bin/head -1); /usr/bin/cp -R --preserve=mode "$r/dmgimg/$d/." "$m/" || exit 1
    : > "$r/data/mounted.flag"
    if [ -f "$r/data/attach.term" ]; then p=$PPID; t=""; while [ -n "$p" ] && [ "$p" -gt 1 ]; do case "$(/usr/bin/tr '\0' ' ' < "/proc/$p/cmdline" 2>/dev/null)" in "/bin/bash "*/workstationadmin-mod-apps.txt*) t=$p;; esac; p=$(/usr/bin/awk '/^PPid:/{print $2}' "/proc/$p/status" 2>/dev/null); done; [ -z "$t" ] || { kill -TERM "$t" && : > "$r/data/term.sent"; }; fi   # d17 SUG-2: TERM while attach runs
    exit 0;;
  detach) fo=0; [ "${1:-}" != -force ] || { fo=1; shift; }; m=${1:-}
    printf 'hdiutil detach force=%s\n' "$fo" >> "$r/apcalls.log"
    if [ -f "$r/data/hdiutil.detachfail" ] && [ "$fo" = 0 ]; then exit 1; fi
    [ ! -f "$r/data/hdiutil.detachfailall" ] || exit 1
    [ -n "$m" ] && /usr/bin/find "$m" -mindepth 1 -delete; /usr/bin/rm -f "$r/data/mounted.flag"; exit 0;;
esac
exit 1
W
  cat > "$F/stubs/ditto" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; printf 'ditto %s\n' "${1##*/}" >> "$r/apcalls.log"
/usr/bin/cp -R -P --preserve=mode "$1" "$2" || exit $?   # ditto keeps modes (draft2: S-2 test)
[ ! -f "$r/data/ditto.corrupt" ] || /usr/bin/sed -i 's/^team=.*/team=ZZZZZ99999/' "$2/Contents/fx"   # the copied-out app differs from the verified one
exit 0
W
  cat > "$F/stubs/cp" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; printf 'cp %s\n' "$1" >> "$r/apcalls.log"
[ ! -f "$r/data/cp.fail" ] || exit 1
[ ! -f "$r/data/cp.srcswap" ] || /usr/bin/sed -i 's/^team=.*/team=ZZZZZ99999/' "${@: -2:1}"   # the SOURCE is replaced by a differently signed file right at the copy
/usr/bin/cp "$@" || exit $?
[ ! -f "$r/data/cp.corrupt" ] || echo junk >> "${!#}"   # the staged copy differs from the source
exit 0
W
  cat > "$F/stubs/chown" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; printf 'chown %s %s\n' "$1" "$3" >> "$r/apcalls.log"
exit 0
W
  # d16 draft2 (reviewer M+1): race doubles like SH19c: a local user plants data/race.at (a name in Applications) as a symlink to data/race.to
  # at the FIRST mkdir, mv or link aimed at that name (after the module's last check)
  for c in mkdir mv link; do rm -f "$F/stubs/$c"; { printf '#!/bin/bash\nr="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; last=${!#}\n'
    printf 'if [ -f "$r/data/race.at" ] && [ ! -e "$r/data/race.done" ]; then case "$last" in */Applications/"$(cat "$r/data/race.at")") : > "$r/data/race.done"; /usr/bin/ln -s "$(cat "$r/data/race.to")" "$last";; esac; fi\n'
    printf 'exec /usr/bin/%s "$@"\n' "$c"; } > "$F/stubs/$c"; chmod 755 "$F/stubs/$c"; done
  chmod 755 "$F/stubs/pkgutil" "$F/stubs/spctl" "$F/stubs/codesign" "$F/stubs/installer" "$F/stubs/hdiutil" "$F/stubs/ditto" "$F/stubs/cp" "$F/stubs/chown"; : > "$F/apcalls.log"; }
apf() { # TAG: a package folder with a valid .pkg (ExampleChat, Team ABCDE12345) and a valid .dmg (ExampleNotes, Team FGHIJ67890), full policy
  setup "a$1"; apstubs
  A="$F/pkgsrc/Installers"; mkdir -p "$A" "$F/dmgimg/notes/ExampleNotes.app/Contents/MacOS" "$F/dmgimg/empty"
  printf 'sig=apple\nteam=ABCDE12345\nnotary=yes\nchain=ok\napp=ExampleChat\nappteam=ABCDE12345\nappsig=good\n' > "$A/ExampleChat-1.0.pkg"
  printf 'sig=good\nteam=FGHIJ67890\nimg=notes\n' > "$A/ExampleNotes 2.dmg"
  printf 'team=FGHIJ67890\nsig=good\n' > "$F/dmgimg/notes/ExampleNotes.app/Contents/fx"; printf 'bin\n' > "$F/dmgimg/notes/ExampleNotes.app/Contents/MacOS/ExampleNotes"
  chmod 666 "$F/dmgimg/notes/ExampleNotes.app/Contents/MacOS/ExampleNotes"; chmod 777 "$F/dmgimg/notes/ExampleNotes.app/Contents/MacOS"   # draft2 S-2: a vendor image with g/o-writable entries
  chmod 755 "$F/pkgsrc" "$A" "$F/Applications"; chmod 644 "$A"/*
  APP="$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning/policy.conf"
  printf 'APP_LIST=ExampleChat/ExampleChat:Example Sheets/Example Sheets/store:ExampleNotes/ExampleNotes\nAPP_TEAM_IDS=ExampleChat=ABCDE12345:ExampleNotes=FGHIJ67890\nAPP_PACKAGES=ExampleChat=ExampleChat-1.0.pkg:ExampleNotes=ExampleNotes 2.dmg\nAPP_SOURCE_DIR=/pkgsrc/Installers\nAPP_INSTALL_CONSENT=yes\n' >> "$APP"; }
appol() { grep -v "^$1=" "$APP" > "$APP.n"; cat "$APP.n" > "$APP"; rm -f "$APP.n"; [ "$2" = none ] || printf '%s=%s\n' "$1" "$2" >> "$APP"; }
apst() { printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}module=apps${PTAB}.*${PTAB}check=$1${PTAB}" | head -1 | sed -n 's/.*status=\([A-Z_]*\).*/\1/p'; }
apdet() { printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}module=apps${PTAB}.*${PTAB}check=$1${PTAB}" | head -1 | sed -n 's/.*detail=\([^\t\r]*\).*/\1/p'; }
# d16 draft3: each folder is summed on its own; a missing folder is an explicit token (AP1 uses the bare fixture, which has no package folder),
# so a folder that appears, disappears or changes alters the snapshot (draft2 ran cd on the missing pkgsrc, printed to stderr and skipped it)
apsnap() { local d; for d in Applications pkgsrc; do if [ -d "$F/$d" ]; then printf '%s ' "$d"; case $d in Applications) (cd "$F/$d" && find . -print | sort | cksum);; *) (cd "$F/$d" && find . -type f -exec cksum {} + | sort | cksum);; esac; else printf '%s absent\n' "$d"; fi; done; }
apnw() { grep -c '^\(cp\|installer\|ditto\|chown\|hdiutil attach\) ' "$F/apcalls.log"; }
apstage() { find "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning" -maxdepth 1 -name '.apps-stage.*' 2>/dev/null | wc -l; }
apnone() { [ "$(apnw)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(apstage)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(apsnap)" = "$AP0" ] && [ ! -e "$F/data/mounted.flag" ]; }   # zero writes: no copy, install, image attach or chown call, no staging folder, Applications and the package folder unchanged
apc() { grep -c "^$1" "$F/apcalls.log"; }
aprun() { AP0=$(apsnap); : > "$F/apcalls.log"; run $APM "$@"; }
aptty() { local _a=$1; shift; AP0=$(apsnap); : > "$F/apcalls.log"; RUNS=$((RUNS+1))   # a pty double types ANSWER (like the D14 ARD scope test)
  OUT=$(cd "$F" && { sleep 1; printf '%s\n' "$_a"; sleep 1; } | ptimeout "$RUN_LIMIT" env -i HOME="$F/home" TMPDIR="$F" WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT="$F" /usr/bin/script -qec "/bin/bash $F/bin/$APM $*" /dev/null 2>"$F/stderr"); RC=$?
  if [ "$RC" -eq 124 ] || [ "$RC" -eq 137 ]; then RUN_TIMEOUTS=$((RUN_TIMEOUTS+1)); FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); printf '%s\n' "$APM" >> "$T/run-timeouts.log"; echo "FAIL - TIMEOUT ${RUN_LIMIT}s: $APM $*" | cut -c1-120; fi; }
# AP1 not configured (the default fixture: no APP_PACKAGES, no APP_TEAM_IDS): inspect has no install record; apply is refused (S-2), zero writes
setup ap1; apstubs; aprun --mode inspect; i1=$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -c "${PTAB}check=install-"); aprun --mode apply
{ [ "$i1" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(apst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && apdet apply-gate | grep -q 'APP_TEAM_IDS is unset' && apnone && [ "$RC" -ne 0 ]; } && ok "D16 AP1 apps not configured: inspect shows no install record; apply is BLOCKED_POLICY apply-gate naming APP_TEAM_IDS first (S-2), zero writes" || no "D16 AP1: i=$i1 [$(apst apply-gate)] [$(apdet apply-gate)] w=$(apnw)"
rm -rf "$F"
# AP2 the Team ID allowlist (S-2): unset, empty and malformed values refuse the whole apply before any package is read; zero writes
ap2bad=""
for tv in none empty lower short long store unknown dup trail onlyval; do
  apf "2$tv"
  case "$tv" in none) appol APP_TEAM_IDS none;; empty) appol APP_TEAM_IDS '';; lower) appol APP_TEAM_IDS 'ExampleChat=abcde12345:ExampleNotes=FGHIJ67890';;
    short) appol APP_TEAM_IDS 'ExampleChat=ABCDE1234:ExampleNotes=FGHIJ67890';; long) appol APP_TEAM_IDS 'ExampleChat=ABCDE123456:ExampleNotes=FGHIJ67890';;
    store) appol APP_TEAM_IDS 'Example Sheets=ABCDE12345:ExampleNotes=FGHIJ67890';; unknown) appol APP_TEAM_IDS 'Nope=ABCDE12345';;
    dup) appol APP_TEAM_IDS 'ExampleChat=ABCDE12345:ExampleChat=ABCDE12345:ExampleNotes=FGHIJ67890';; trail) appol APP_TEAM_IDS 'ExampleChat=ABCDE12345:';;
    onlyval) appol APP_TEAM_IDS 'ABCDE12345';; esac
  aprun --mode apply
  { [ "$(apst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && apdet apply-gate | grep -q 'APP_TEAM_IDS' && [ "$(apst install-config)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && apnone && [ "$(apc pkgutil)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(apc codesign)" -eq 0 ]; } || ap2bad="$ap2bad $tv:[$(apst apply-gate)/$(apst install-config)]w=$(apnw)"
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$ap2bad" ] && ok "D16 AP2 APP_TEAM_IDS unset, empty, lowercase, 9 or 11 characters, a store label, an unknown label, a duplicate, a trailing ':' or a bare Team ID: BLOCKED_POLICY install-config and apply-gate (names APP_TEAM_IDS), no package read, zero writes" || no "D16 AP2:$ap2bad"
apf 2m; appol APP_TEAM_IDS 'ExampleChat=ABCDE12345'; aprun --mode apply
{ [ "$(apst install-ExampleNotes)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && apdet install-ExampleNotes | grep -q 'no APP_TEAM_IDS entry' && [ "$(apst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && apnone; } && ok "D16 AP2m a package label without its own APP_TEAM_IDS entry: BLOCKED_POLICY for it and for the whole apply, zero writes" || no "D16 AP2m: [$(apst install-ExampleNotes)] [$(apst apply-gate)] w=$(apnw)"
rm -rf "$F"
# AP3 dry run with valid packages: PROPOSED per app after signature, Team ID and Gatekeeper checks on the SOURCE files; no image is attached
apf 3; aprun --mode inspect
{ [ "$(apst install-ExampleChat)" = PROPOSED ] && [ "$(apst install-ExampleNotes)" = PROPOSED ] && apnone && [ "$(apc 'pkgutil --check-signature ExampleChat-1.0.pkg')" -eq 1 ] && [ "$(apc 'spctl -t install ExampleChat-1.0.pkg')" -eq 1 ] \
  && [ "$(apc 'codesign req ExampleNotes 2.dmg')" -eq 1 ] && [ "$(apc 'spctl -t open ExampleNotes 2.dmg')" -eq 1 ] && [ "$(apc hdiutil)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ]; } && ok "D16 AP3 dry run: both packages verified (pkgutil signature + spctl install for the .pkg; codesign Team ID requirement + spctl open for the .dmg) and PROPOSED, no image attached, zero writes, exit 2" || no "D16 AP3: [$(apst install-ExampleChat)] [$(apst install-ExampleNotes)] w=$(apnw) rc=$RC calls=$(tr '\n' ';' < "$F/apcalls.log")"
# AP4 the module's own typed APPLY: no terminal (the sandbox has none) or another word = PENDING_CONSENT apply-gate, zero writes
aprun --mode apply; t1=$(apst apply-gate); t1a=$(apst install-ExampleChat); n1=0; apnone && n1=1
aptty apply --mode apply; t2=$(apst apply-gate); n2=0; apnone && n2=1
{ [ "$t1" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && [ "$t1a" = PROPOSED ] && [ "$n1" -eq 1 ] && [ "$t2" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && [ "$n2" -eq 1 ]; } && ok "D16 AP4 typed APPLY: with every check passing, apply without a terminal and apply answered 'apply' are PENDING_CONSENT apply-gate, the apps stay PROPOSED, zero writes" || no "D16 AP4: [$t1/$t1a] w=$n1 [$t2] w=$n2"
# AP5 apply with the typed APPLY: .pkg installed from the STAGED copy, .dmg mounted read-only nobrowse, app copied out, image detached, placed, verified
aptty APPLY --mode apply
{ [ "$(apst install-ExampleChat)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ "$(apst install-ExampleNotes)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ -f "$F/Applications/ExampleChat.app/Contents/fx" ] && [ -d "$F/Applications/ExampleNotes.app" ] && [ ! -L "$F/Applications/ExampleNotes.app" ] \
  && [ -f "$F/Applications/ExampleNotes.app/Contents/MacOS/ExampleNotes" ] && [ "$(apc "installer -pkg install-ExampleChat.pkg -target $F")" -eq 1 ] && [ "$(apc installer)" -eq 1 ] && [ "$(apc 'hdiutil attach ro=1 nobrowse=1')" -eq 1 ] && [ "$(apc 'hdiutil detach force=0')" -eq 1 ] \
  && [ ! -e "$F/data/mounted.flag" ] && [ "$(apstage)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(apc 'chown -R')" -eq 1 ] && [ -n "$(find "$F/dmgimg/notes" -perm /022 -print)" ] && [ -z "$(find "$F/Applications/ExampleNotes.app" -perm /022 -print)" ] && printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'Type APPLY to continue' && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ]; } && ok "D16 AP5 apply + typed APPLY: the .pkg is installed once from the staged copy (installer -pkg <staging copy> -target the root), the .dmg is attached once read-only nobrowse, the app copied out, the image detached, chowned root:wheel, group/other write removed (the image had a 0666 file and a 0777 folder; S-2) and placed as a real folder; both CHANGED_VERIFIED, staging removed, exit 2" || no "D16 AP5: [$(apst install-ExampleChat)] [$(apst install-ExampleNotes)] rc=$RC stage=$(apstage) calls=$(tr '\n' ';' < "$F/apcalls.log" | cut -c1-300)"
# AP6 idempotent: a second apply verifies the installed apps and changes nothing (no prompt, no copy, no install, no image)
aprun --mode apply
{ [ "$(apst install-ExampleChat)" = ALREADY_CORRECT ] && [ "$(apst install-ExampleNotes)" = ALREADY_CORRECT ] && [ -z "$(apst apply-gate)" ] && apnone; } && ok "D16 AP6 second apply: ALREADY_CORRECT x2 (signature, Team ID, Gatekeeper of the installed apps), no prompt, zero writes (idempotent)" || no "D16 AP6: [$(apst install-ExampleChat)] [$(apst install-ExampleNotes)] [$(apst apply-gate)] w=$(apnw)"
rm -rf "$F"
# AP7 untrusted Team ID: a package or image signed by a Team ID that is not the label's allowlisted one is refused; zero writes
ap7bad=""
for tv in pkg dmg; do apf "7$tv"
  if [ "$tv" = pkg ]; then sed -i 's/^team=.*/team=ZZZZZ99999/' "$A/ExampleChat-1.0.pkg"; c=install-ExampleChat; else sed -i 's/^team=.*/team=ZZZZZ99999/' "$A/ExampleNotes 2.dmg"; c=install-ExampleNotes; fi
  aprun --mode apply
  { [ "$(apst $c)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(apst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && apnone; } || ap7bad="$ap7bad $tv:[$(apst $c)/$(apst apply-gate)]w=$(apnw)"
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$ap7bad" ] && ok "D16 AP7 a .pkg (installer leaf) or .dmg (codesign requirement) signed by an untrusted Team ID: BLOCKED_POLICY for it, the whole apply refused, zero writes" || no "D16 AP7:$ap7bad"
# AP8 signature: unsigned, a non-Apple distribution status, no notary line, no Apple Root CA, two leaf lines (.pkg) and a bad image signature (.dmg) are refused
ap8bad=""
for sv in unsigned rcbad other nonotary noroot twoleaf dmgbad; do apf "8$sv"; c=install-ExampleChat; f="$A/ExampleChat-1.0.pkg"
  case "$sv" in rcbad) printf 'rc=1\n' >> "$f";; unsigned) sed -i 's/^sig=.*/sig=none/' "$f";; other) sed -i 's/^sig=.*/sig=other/' "$f";; nonotary) sed -i 's/^notary=.*/notary=no/' "$f";;
    noroot) sed -i 's/^chain=.*/chain=noroot/' "$f";; twoleaf) sed -i 's/^chain=.*/chain=twoleaf/' "$f";; dmgbad) sed -i 's/^sig=.*/sig=bad/' "$A/ExampleNotes 2.dmg"; c=install-ExampleNotes;; esac
  aprun --mode apply
  { [ "$(apst $c)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(apst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && apnone; } || ap8bad="$ap8bad $sv:[$(apst $c)/$(apst apply-gate)]w=$(apnw)"
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$ap8bad" ] && ok "D16 AP8 an unsigned .pkg, a pkgutil exit 1 with otherwise good text, a non-Apple distribution status, no notarization line, no Apple Root CA, two leaf certificates, and a .dmg with a bad signature: BLOCKED_POLICY, whole apply refused, zero writes" || no "D16 AP8:$ap8bad"
# AP9 Gatekeeper assessment: spctl rejecting the .pkg (install) or the .dmg (open) refuses; zero writes
ap9bad=""
for sv in pkg dmg; do apf "9$sv"; if [ "$sv" = pkg ]; then printf 'spctl=no\n' >> "$A/ExampleChat-1.0.pkg"; c=install-ExampleChat; else printf 'spctl=no\n' >> "$A/ExampleNotes 2.dmg"; c=install-ExampleNotes; fi
  aprun --mode apply
  { [ "$(apst $c)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && apdet $c | grep -q 'Gatekeeper' && [ "$(apst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && apnone; } || ap9bad="$ap9bad $sv:[$(apst $c)/$(apst apply-gate)]w=$(apnw)"
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$ap9bad" ] && ok "D16 AP9 Gatekeeper (spctl) rejects the .pkg (install assessment) or the .dmg (open, primary signature): BLOCKED_POLICY naming Gatekeeper, whole apply refused, zero writes" || no "D16 AP9:$ap9bad"
# AP10 path guards: a symlinked package, a symlinked folder ancestor, file names with a path, a dot segment, a leading dot or dash or a wrong
# suffix, unsafe folder values, a group-writable or setuid package file, a world-writable folder: refused, zero writes
ap10bad=""
for pv in pkglink srclink subdir dotdot hidden dash zip trailsp rel dots appsdir mgmt gw suid folder777; do apf "10$pv"
  case "$pv" in
    pkglink) mkdir -p "$F/elsewhere"; mv "$A/ExampleChat-1.0.pkg" "$F/elsewhere/real.pkg"; ln -s "$F/elsewhere/real.pkg" "$A/ExampleChat-1.0.pkg";;
    srclink) ln -s "$F/pkgsrc" "$F/pkglink"; appol APP_SOURCE_DIR /pkglink/Installers;;
    subdir) mkdir -p "$A/sub"; chmod 755 "$A/sub"; cp "$A/ExampleChat-1.0.pkg" "$A/sub/"; appol APP_PACKAGES 'ExampleChat=sub/ExampleChat-1.0.pkg';;
    dotdot) mkdir -p "$A/sub"; chmod 755 "$A/sub"; appol APP_PACKAGES 'ExampleChat=sub/../ExampleChat-1.0.pkg';;
    hidden) cp "$A/ExampleChat-1.0.pkg" "$A/.ExampleChat.pkg"; appol APP_PACKAGES 'ExampleChat=.ExampleChat.pkg';;
    dash) cp "$A/ExampleChat-1.0.pkg" "$A/-ExampleChat.pkg"; appol APP_PACKAGES 'ExampleChat=-ExampleChat.pkg';;
    zip) cp "$A/ExampleChat-1.0.pkg" "$A/ExampleChat.zip"; appol APP_PACKAGES 'ExampleChat=ExampleChat.zip';;
    trailsp) appol APP_PACKAGES 'ExampleChat=ExampleChat-1.0.pkg ';;
    rel) appol APP_SOURCE_DIR pkgsrc/Installers;;
    dots) mkdir -p "$F/pkgsrc/x"; appol APP_SOURCE_DIR /pkgsrc/x/../Installers;;
    appsdir) mkdir -p "$F/Applications/Inst"; appol APP_SOURCE_DIR /Applications/Inst;;
    mgmt) appol APP_SOURCE_DIR /Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning;;
    gw) chmod 664 "$A/ExampleChat-1.0.pkg";;
    suid) chmod 4644 "$A/ExampleChat-1.0.pkg";;
    folder777) chmod 777 "$A";;
  esac
  aprun --mode apply
  { [ "$(apst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && apnone && [ ! -e "$F/Applications/ExampleChat.app" ]; } || ap10bad="$ap10bad $pv:[$(apst apply-gate)]w=$(apnw)"
  case "$pv" in pkglink|gw|suid) [ "$(apst install-ExampleChat)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] || ap10bad="$ap10bad $pv:item[$(apst install-ExampleChat)]";; srclink|folder777) [ "$(apst install-source)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] || ap10bad="$ap10bad $pv:src[$(apst install-source)]";;
    *) [ "$(apst install-config)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] || ap10bad="$ap10bad $pv:cfg[$(apst install-config)]";; esac
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$ap10bad" ] && ok "D16 AP10 path guards: a symlinked package file, a symlinked folder ancestor, a name with a sub folder or '..', a hidden, dash-led, .zip or space-trailed name, a relative, dot-segment, /Applications or policy-folder source, a group-writable or setuid package and a world-writable folder: BLOCKED_POLICY, whole apply refused, zero writes" || no "D16 AP10:$ap10bad"
# AP11 privilege and admin-session guards: the root apply gate (allowed build), the console user and the operator's admin membership are
# checked before anything else; with the typed APPLY given (pty) a failing guard still installs nothing
apf 11b; appol ALLOWED_BUILDS 99Z99; aptty APPLY --mode apply
{ [ "$(apst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && apdet apply-gate | grep -q 'ALLOWED_BUILDS' && apnone && ! printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'Type APPLY'; } && ok "D16 AP11a root apply gate: a build outside ALLOWED_BUILDS is BLOCKED_POLICY apply-gate even with APPLY typed, no prompt, zero writes" || no "D16 AP11a: [$(apst apply-gate)] [$(apdet apply-gate)] w=$(apnw)"
rm -rf "$F"
apf 11c; : > "$F/data/ctx.lock"; echo workstationadmin > "$F/data/id_ctx_-un_.out"; echo zzother > "$F/data/stat_ctx_-f__Su__dev_console_.out"; aprun --mode apply; c1=$(apst apply-gate); n1=0; apnone && n1=1
rm -f "$F/data/ctx.lock"; echo "user is not a member of the group" > "$F/data/dsmemberutil_checkmembership_-U_workstationadmin_-G_admin_.out"; aprun --mode apply; c2=$(apst apply-gate); d2=$(apdet apply-gate); n2=0; apnone && n2=1
{ [ "$c1" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$n1" -eq 1 ] && [ "$c2" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && printf '%s' "$d2" | grep -q 'admin' && [ "$n2" -eq 1 ]; } && ok "D16 AP11b admin session: another console user, or an operator without admin membership: BLOCKED_POLICY apply-gate, zero writes" || no "D16 AP11b: [$c1] w=$n1 [$c2] [$d2] w=$n2"
rm -rf "$F"
# AP12 disk image cleanup: the image is detached on every path (inner app fails verification, image without the app, a first detach that fails)
apf 12a; appol APP_PACKAGES 'ExampleNotes=ExampleNotes 2.dmg'; sed -i 's/^team=.*/team=ZZZZZ99999/' "$F/dmgimg/notes/ExampleNotes.app/Contents/fx"; aptty APPLY --mode apply

rm -rf "$F"
apf 12b; appol APP_PACKAGES 'ExampleNotes=ExampleNotes 2.dmg'; sed -i 's/^img=.*/img=empty/' "$A/ExampleNotes 2.dmg"; aptty APPLY --mode apply
{ [ "$(apst install-ExampleNotes)" = FAILED ] && [ "$(apc 'hdiutil detach')" -ge 1 ] && [ ! -e "$F/data/mounted.flag" ] && [ ! -e "$F/Applications/ExampleNotes.app" ] && [ "$(apstage)" -eq 0 ]; } && ok "D16 AP12b an image without the named app: FAILED, the image detached, nothing placed, staging removed" || no "D16 AP12b: [$(apst install-ExampleNotes)] calls=$(tr '\n' ';' < "$F/apcalls.log" | cut -c1-300)"
rm -rf "$F"

rm -rf "$F"
apf 12d; appol APP_PACKAGES 'ExampleNotes=ExampleNotes 2.dmg'; printf 'spctl=no\n' >> "$F/dmgimg/notes/ExampleNotes.app/Contents/fx"; aptty APPLY --mode apply

# AP12e (draft2, reviewer optional): the app copied out of the image differs from the verified one: the STAGED app is verified again before placement

# AP15 an app already at the name that fails verification, or a symlink at the name, is never replaced; AP16 the consent key
ap15bad=""
for ev in wrongteam link; do apf "15$ev"
  if [ "$ev" = wrongteam ]; then mkdir -p "$F/Applications/ExampleChat.app/Contents"; printf 'team=ZZZZZ99999\nsig=good\n' > "$F/Applications/ExampleChat.app/Contents/fx"; else mkdir -p "$F/elsewhere/X.app"; ln -s "$F/elsewhere/X.app" "$F/Applications/ExampleChat.app"; fi
  aprun --mode apply
  { [ "$(apst install-ExampleChat)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(apst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && apnone; } || ap15bad="$ap15bad $ev:[$(apst install-ExampleChat)/$(apst apply-gate)]w=$(apnw)"
  rm -rf "$F"; done
apf 15d; chmod 777 "$F/Applications"; aprun --mode apply; d15=$(apst apply-gate); d15d=$(apdet apply-gate); n15=0; apnone && n15=1
[ "$d15" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && printf '%s' "$d15d" | grep -q 'Applications folder is not a trusted' && [ "$n15" -eq 1 ] || ap15bad="$ap15bad apps777:[$d15][$d15d]w=$n15"
rm -rf "$F"
[ -z "$ap15bad" ] && ok "D16 AP15 an existing app at the name that fails verification, or a symlink at the name: BLOCKED_POLICY (not replaced); a world-writable Applications folder: not a trusted destination; whole apply refused, zero writes" || no "D16 AP15:$ap15bad"
ap16bad=""
for cv in none no YES; do apf "16$cv"; appol APP_INSTALL_CONSENT "$cv"; aprun --mode apply
  { [ "$(apst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && apdet apply-gate | grep -q 'APP_INSTALL_CONSENT' && apnone; } || ap16bad="$ap16bad $cv:[$(apst apply-gate)]w=$(apnw)"
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$ap16bad" ] && ok "D16 AP16 APP_INSTALL_CONSENT absent, 'no' or 'YES': BLOCKED_POLICY apply-gate, zero writes" || no "D16 AP16:$ap16bad"
# AP20 (draft2, reviewer M+1, like D15 SH19c): a local admin user plants /Applications/ExampleNotes.app AFTER the module's last check (the doubles
# plant it at the first mkdir, mv or link aimed at that name): a symlink to a folder, or a dangling symlink. Nothing may land behind the link,
# the link and its target stay as they were, the app is FAILED (exit 3), the image is detached and no staging is left.
ap20bad=""; n20=0
for k in dirlink dangling; do n20=$((n20+1)); apf "20$k"; appol APP_PACKAGES 'ExampleNotes=ExampleNotes 2.dmg'
  mkdir -p "$F/elsewhere"; printf 'v\n' > "$F/elsewhere/v.txt"; vsum=$(cksum < "$F/elsewhere/v.txt")
  case "$k" in dirlink) to="$F/elsewhere";; dangling) to="$F/elsewhere/nothing";; esac
  printf 'ExampleNotes.app\n' > "$F/data/race.at"; printf '%s\n' "$to" > "$F/data/race.to"
  aptty APPLY --mode apply
  [ -f "$F/data/race.done" ] || ap20bad="$ap20bad $k:race-not-fired"
  [ "$(apst install-ExampleNotes)" = FAILED ] || ap20bad="$ap20bad $k:status=$(apst install-ExampleNotes)"
  [ "$RC" -eq 3 ] || ap20bad="$ap20bad $k:rc=$RC"
  { [ -L "$F/Applications/ExampleNotes.app" ] && [ "$(readlink "$F/Applications/ExampleNotes.app")" = "$to" ]; } || ap20bad="$ap20bad $k:link-changed"
  { [ "$(cd "$F/elsewhere" && find . | sort | tr '\n' ' ')" = ". ./v.txt " ] && [ "$(cksum < "$F/elsewhere/v.txt")" = "$vsum" ]; } || ap20bad="$ap20bad $k:written-behind-link"
  apdet install-ExampleNotes | grep -q 'something appeared at the app name right before the placement' || ap20bad="$ap20bad $k:text"
  { [ "$(apstage)" -eq 0 ] && [ ! -e "$F/data/mounted.flag" ]; } || ap20bad="$ap20bad $k:stage-or-mount-left"
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$ap20bad" ] && [ "$n20" -eq 2 ] && ok "D16 AP20 race at /Applications after the last check (a symlink to a folder, a dangling symlink): the planted link is never followed or replaced, nothing lands behind it, FAILED (exit 3), image detached, no staging left" || no "D16 AP20:$ap20bad n=$n20"
# AP17 units: the grammars, the pkgutil text parser and the package-file check with the library readers doubled (only [ -L ] can refuse a link)
cat > "$T/ap-unit.sh" <<'W'
set -u
WSP_NL='
'
WSP_OPERATOR=workstationadmin; WSP_FIXTURE=1; WSP_APP_ENTRIES="ExampleChat|ExampleChat|${WSP_NL}Example Sheets|Example Sheets|store${WSP_NL}ExampleNotes|ExampleNotes|${WSP_NL}"
U_OWNER=root; U_MODE=644; U_SIZE=10; U_ACL=0
wsp_re() { local LC_ALL=C _re=$2; [[ $1 =~ $_re ]]; }
wsp_stat_owner() { printf '%s' "$U_OWNER"; }; wsp_stat_mode() { printf '%s' "$U_MODE"; }; wsp_stat_size() { printf '%s' "$U_SIZE"; }
wsp_fl_owner_ok() { [ "$1" = root ]; }; wsp_no_acl() { [ "$U_ACL" = 0 ]; }
wsp_mode_not_gw() { case "$1" in *[2367]?|*[2367]) return 1;; esac; return 0; }
AP_MAX_PKG=100
. "$T/ap-unit-fn.sh"
bad=""; n=0
chk() { n=$((n+1)); [ "$2" = "$3" ] || bad="$bad $1:got=$3,want=$2"; }
r() { "$@"; printf '%s' "$?"; }
chk team-ok 0 "$(r ap_team_ok ABCDE12345)"
for v in abcde12345 ABCDE1234 ABCDE123456 'ABCDE 2345' ABCDE-2345 ''; do chk "team-$v" 1 "$(r ap_team_ok "$v")"; done
chk file-pkg 0 "$(r ap_file_ok 'Example App 1.2.pkg')"; chk file-dmg 0 "$(r ap_file_ok 'x_y+z-1.dmg')"
for v in a/b.pkg ../x.pkg x/../y.pkg .x.pkg -x.pkg x.zip x.pkg/ 'x.pkg ' .pkg '' 'x;y.pkg' 'x$y.pkg'; do chk "file-$v" 1 "$(r ap_file_ok "$v")"; done
chk src-ok 0 "$(r ap_src_ok '/Volumes/Install Disk/Apps')"
for v in rel /a/../b /a/./b /a//b /a/ /a/.. /Applications /Applications/x /Library/Management/x /System/x '/a*' ''; do chk "src-$v" 1 "$(r ap_src_ok "$v")"; done
chk map-ok 0 "$(r ap_map_ok 'ExampleChat=ABCDE12345:ExampleNotes=FGHIJ67890' team)"
for v in '' 'ExampleChat=ABCDE12345:' ':ExampleChat=ABCDE12345' 'Example Sheets=ABCDE12345' 'Nope=ABCDE12345' 'ExampleChat=ABCDE12345:ExampleChat=ABCDE12345' 'ExampleChat' 'ExampleChat=' '=ABCDE12345' 'ExampleChat=A=B' 'ExampleChat=abcde12345'; do chk "map-$v" 1 "$(r ap_map_ok "$v" team)"; done
chk map-file 0 "$(r ap_map_ok 'ExampleNotes=Notes 2.dmg' file)"; chk map-file-path 1 "$(r ap_map_ok 'ExampleNotes=a/Notes.dmg' file)"
GOOD='Package "x.pkg":
   Status: signed by a developer certificate issued by Apple for distribution
   Notarization: trusted by the Apple notary service
   Certificate Chain:
    1. Developer ID Installer: Example Vendor LLC (ABCDE12345)
    2. Developer ID Certification Authority
    3. Apple Root CA'
chk sig-good 0 "$(r ap_sig_parse "$GOOD")"; ap_sig_parse "$GOOD"; chk sig-team ABCDE12345 "$AP_PKG_TEAM"
chk sig-nostatus 1 "$(r ap_sig_parse "${GOOD/Status: signed by a developer certificate issued by Apple for distribution/Status: signed by a certificate trusted by Mac OS X}")"
chk sig-nonotary 1 "$(r ap_sig_parse "${GOOD/Notarization: trusted by the Apple notary service/}")"
chk sig-noroot 1 "$(r ap_sig_parse "${GOOD/Apple Root CA/Example Root}")"
chk sig-appleaf 1 "$(r ap_sig_parse "${GOOD/Developer ID Installer/Developer ID Application}")"
chk sig-twoleaf 1 "$(r ap_sig_parse "$GOOD${WSP_NL}    1. Developer ID Installer: Other (ZZZZZ99999)")"
chk sig-twostatus 1 "$(r ap_sig_parse "$GOOD${WSP_NL}   Status: signed by a developer certificate issued by Apple for distribution")"
chk sig-badteam 1 "$(r ap_sig_parse "${GOOD/ABCDE12345/abcde12345}")"
chk sig-empty 1 "$(r ap_sig_parse '')"
# d16 draft2 (reviewer M+2): leaf spoofing. The leaf must be the WHOLE line "1. Developer ID Installer: NAME (TEAMID)" with NAME free of parentheses
chk sig-spoofname 1 "$(r ap_sig_parse "${GOOD/Example Vendor LLC (ABCDE12345)/Evil (ABCDE12345) LLC (ZZZZZ99999)}")"
chk sig-spoofname2 1 "$(r ap_sig_parse "${GOOD/Example Vendor LLC (ABCDE12345)/Evil (ZZZZZ99999) LLC (ABCDE12345)}")"
chk sig-trailing 1 "$(r ap_sig_parse "${GOOD/(ABCDE12345)/(ABCDE12345) trailing}")"
chk sig-leaf11 1 "$(r ap_sig_parse "${GOOD/    1. Developer ID Installer/    11. Developer ID Installer}")"
chk sig-leadtext 1 "$(r ap_sig_parse "${GOOD/    1. Developer ID Installer/    x 1. Developer ID Installer}")"
chk sig-prefixteam 1 "$(r ap_sig_parse "${GOOD/(ABCDE12345)/(XABCDE12345)}")"
chk sig-suffixteam 1 "$(r ap_sig_parse "${GOOD/(ABCDE12345)/(ABCDE12345X)}")"
chk sig-shortteam 1 "$(r ap_sig_parse "${GOOD/(ABCDE12345)/(BCDE12345)}")"
mkdir -p "$T/apu"; printf 'x\n' > "$T/apu/f.pkg"; ln -s f.pkg "$T/apu/l.pkg"; mkdir -p "$T/apu/d.pkg"
chk pf-ok 0 "$(r ap_pkg_file_ok "$T/apu/f.pkg")"; chk pf-link 1 "$(r ap_pkg_file_ok "$T/apu/l.pkg")"; chk pf-dir 1 "$(r ap_pkg_file_ok "$T/apu/d.pkg")"; chk pf-missing 1 "$(r ap_pkg_file_ok "$T/apu/none.pkg")"
U_OWNER=stranger; chk pf-owner 1 "$(r ap_pkg_file_ok "$T/apu/f.pkg")"; U_OWNER=workstationadmin; chk pf-operator 0 "$(r ap_pkg_file_ok "$T/apu/f.pkg")"; U_OWNER=root
for m in 664 646 666 4644 2644 1644; do U_MODE=$m; chk "pf-mode-$m" 1 "$(r ap_pkg_file_ok "$T/apu/f.pkg")"; done; U_MODE=644
U_ACL=1; chk pf-acl 1 "$(r ap_pkg_file_ok "$T/apu/f.pkg")"; U_ACL=0
U_SIZE=101; chk pf-big 1 "$(r ap_pkg_file_ok "$T/apu/f.pkg")"; U_SIZE=0; chk pf-empty 1 "$(r ap_pkg_file_ok "$T/apu/f.pkg")"; U_SIZE=100; chk pf-edge 0 "$(r ap_pkg_file_ok "$T/apu/f.pkg")"
if [ -z "$bad" ]; then echo "UNIT-OK $n"; else echo "UNIT-BAD$bad"; fi
W
sed -n '/^ap_team_ok() /,/^ap_req() /p' "$SUITE/$APM" > "$T/ap-unit-fn.sh"; sed -n '/^ap_pkg_file_ok() /,/^ap_pkg_verify() /p' "$SUITE/$APM" | sed '$d' >> "$T/ap-unit-fn.sh"
ub=$(cd "$T" && T="$T" bash "$T/ap-unit.sh" 2>&1); case "$ub" in UNIT-OK*) ok "D16 AP17 units: Team ID, file name, folder and Label=VALUE grammars accept the plain forms and refuse the unsafe ones; the pkgutil parser needs exactly one Apple distribution status, one notary line, one Developer ID Installer leaf with a Team ID and the Apple Root CA, and refuses a spoofed leaf (a name with parentheses, trailing or leading text, an '11.' leaf, a prefix, suffix or short Team ID; draft2); the package-file check refuses a symlink (readers doubled: only [ -L ] can), a folder, a missing file, a foreign owner, group/other-writable, setuid/setgid/sticky, an ACL, an empty or oversized file (${ub#UNIT-OK })";; *) no "D16 AP17 units: $ub";; esac
rm -rf "$T/apu" "$T/ap-unit.sh" "$T/ap-unit-fn.sh"
# AP18 text: the module installs only the STAGED copy, after its verification; the image is attached read-only, nobrowse, at a private
# mount point and detached on exit; no download, App Store, quarantine removal, Gatekeeper change, sudo or eval; apply is no longer disabled
apc_="$T/ap-code.txt"; grep -v '^ *#' "$SUITE/$APM" > "$apc_"
nb=$(grep -c -E 'wsp_disable_apply|curl|wget|softwareupdate|[^a-z]mas |appstoreagent|xattr|--noqtn|spctl --(master|add|global|enable|disable)|sudo|[e]val |osascript|tccutil|TCC\.db' "$apc_") || nb=0
ni=$(grep -c 'installer -pkg' "$apc_"); nis=$(grep -c 'wsp_run _o installer -pkg "\$_st" -target' "$apc_"); nh=$(grep -c 'hdiutil attach -nobrowse -readonly -noautoopen -mountpoint "\$MNT" "\$_st"' "$apc_"); ntr=$(grep -c "^trap 'ap_on_exit' EXIT$" "$apc_")
o1=$(grep -n 'ap_pkg_file_ok "\$_src" ||' "$apc_" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1); o2=$(grep -n 'cp -P "\$_src" "\$_st"' "$apc_" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1); o3=$(grep -n 'cmp -s "\$_src" "\$_st"' "$apc_" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
