o10=$(grep -n 'chown -R -h root:wheel "\$STAGE/\$_id.app"' "$apc_" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1); o11=$(grep -n 'wsp_rename_nofollow "\$STAGE/\$_id.app" "\$_app"' "$apc_" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
g1=$(grep -n 'apply refused, running inspect only' "$apc_" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1); g2=$(grep -n 'wsp_confirm "Install' "$apc_" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1); g3=$(grep -n 'ap_install "\$id"' "$apc_" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
{ [ "$nb" -eq 0 ] && [ "$ni" -eq 1 ] && [ "$nis" -eq 1 ] && [ "$nh" -eq 1 ] && [ "$ntr" -eq 1 ] && [ -n "$o1" ] && [ -n "$o11" ] && [ "$o1" -lt "$o2" ] && [ "$o2" -lt "$o3" ] && [ "$o3" -lt "$o4" ] && [ "$o4" -lt "$o5" ] && [ "$o5" -lt "$o6" ] && [ "$o6" -lt "$o7" ] && [ "$o7" -lt "$o8" ] && [ "$o8" -lt "$o9" ] && [ "$o9" -lt "$o10" ] && [ "$o10" -lt "$o11" ] \
  && [ -n "$g1" ] && [ -n "$g3" ] && [ "$g1" -lt "$g2" ] && [ "$g2" -lt "$g3" ] && grep -q '^WSP_GATE_NEEDS=root$' "$apc_" && grep -q '^wsp_parse_mode "\$@"$' "$apc_"; } && ok "D16 AP18 module 5 text: no wsp_disable_apply, download, App Store, quarantine/xattr removal, Gatekeeper change, sudo, shell evaluation or TCC; one installer call, on the staged copy; one read-only nobrowse attach at the private mount point; an EXIT trap detaches; order: source re-check, copy, compare, verify the staged copy, install / attach, verify the app in the image, copy out, owner, place; the refusal precedes the typed APPLY, which precedes the first install; root gate declared" || no "D16 AP18: bad=$nb inst=$ni/$nis attach=$nh trap=$ntr order=$o1,$o2,$o3,$o4,$o5,$o6,$o7,$o8,$o9,$o10,$o11 gate=$g1,$g2,$g3"
rm -f "$apc_"
# AP19 (reviewer S-8, S-9; d16 lib change): wsp_rename_nofollow with doubles. S-8: a file whose link(2) succeeded is placed (0) even if
# removing the staging name fails. S-9: a folder whose undo fails returns 3 (a root-only claim remains), not 1; the plain failure is still 1.
cat > "$T/rn-unit.sh" <<'W'
set -u
WSP_FIXTURE=1; MVN=0; MVFAIL=""; RMFAIL=0
wsp_stat_owner() { printf 'root'; }; wsp_stat_group() { printf 'wheel'; }; wsp_stat_mode() { printf '755'; }
chown() { return 0; }
rm() { if [ "$RMFAIL" = 1 ]; then return 1; fi; command rm "$@"; }
mv() { MVN=$((MVN+1)); case " $MVFAIL " in *" $MVN "*) return 1;; esac; command mv "$@"; }
. "$T/rn-unit-fn.sh"
bad=""
chk() { [ "$2" = "$3" ] || bad="$bad $1:got=$3,want=$2"; }
R="$T/rnu"; command mkdir -p "$R/s" "$R/s2/a" "$R/s3/a"; printf 'x\n' > "$R/s/f"; printf 'b\n' > "$R/s2/b"; printf 'b\n' > "$R/s3/b"
RMFAIL=1; wsp_rename_nofollow "$R/s/f" "$R/d-f"; chk s8-file-placed 0 "$?"; RMFAIL=0; [ -f "$R/d-f" ] || bad="$bad s8-not-placed"
MVN=0; MVFAIL="2"; wsp_rename_nofollow "$R/s2" "$R/d-2"; chk s9-plain-1 1 "$?"; [ ! -e "$R/d-2" ] || bad="$bad s9-plain-left"
MVN=0; MVFAIL="2 3"; wsp_rename_nofollow "$R/s3" "$R/d-3"; chk s9-undo-3 3 "$?"; [ -d "$R/d-3" ] || bad="$bad s9-claim-gone"
mkdir -p "$R/d-4"; wsp_rename_nofollow "$R/s/f2" "$R/d-4"; chk exists-2 2 "$?"
if [ -z "$bad" ]; then echo UNIT-OK; else echo "UNIT-BAD$bad"; fi
W
sed -n '/^wsp_rename_nofollow() {/,/^}/p' "$SUITE/workstationadmin-lib.txt" > "$T/rn-unit-fn.sh"
ub=$(cd "$T" && T="$T" bash "$T/rn-unit.sh" 2>&1)
{ [ "$ub" = UNIT-OK ] && grep -q 'a partial root-only folder may remain at the name' "$SUITE/workstationadmin-mod-shared.txt" && grep -q 'if \[ "\$_rr" -eq 3 \]' "$SUITE/workstationadmin-mod-shared.txt" && grep -q '^        3) _rb="; putting the replaced item back failed and its undo failed' "$SUITE/workstationadmin-mod-shared.txt"; } && ok "D16 AP19 (S-8, S-9) wsp_rename_nofollow: a linked file is placed (0) even when the staging name cannot be removed; a failed folder placement whose undo works is 1 (nothing left), whose undo fails is 3 (the claim remains) and module 4 reports that with its own text" || no "D16 AP19: $ub"
rm -rf "$T/rnu" "$T/rn-unit.sh" "$T/rn-unit-fn.sh"
# AP21 (d17, reviewer SUG-2): signals during the disk-image install. (a) TERM while the image is attached (the codesign double sends it
# to the module shell while the app INSIDE the image is checked); (b) TERM while hdiutil attach runs (the attach double sends it right after
# it mounted, before the module recorded the mount). Both: the INT/TERM/HUP trap exits 3, the EXIT trap detaches the image and removes the
# staging folder, nothing is copied out or placed.
ap21bad=""; n21=0
for k in attached attaching; do n21=$((n21+1)); apf "21$k"; appol APP_PACKAGES 'ExampleNotes=ExampleNotes 2.dmg'
  case "$k" in attached) : > "$F/data/codesign.term";; attaching) : > "$F/data/attach.term";; esac
  aptty APPLY --mode apply
  [ -f "$F/data/term.sent" ] || ap21bad="$ap21bad $k:term-not-sent"
  [ "$RC" -eq 3 ] || ap21bad="$ap21bad $k:rc=$RC"
  [ "$(apc 'hdiutil attach')" -eq 1 ] || ap21bad="$ap21bad $k:attach=$(apc 'hdiutil attach')"
  { [ "$(apc 'hdiutil detach')" -ge 1 ] && [ ! -e "$F/data/mounted.flag" ]; } || ap21bad="$ap21bad $k:still-mounted"
  { [ ! -e "$F/Applications/ExampleNotes.app" ] && [ "$(apc ditto)" -eq 0 ]; } || ap21bad="$ap21bad $k:placed"
  [ "$(apstage)" -eq 0 ] || ap21bad="$ap21bad $k:stage-left"
  rm -rf "$F"; done
[ -z "$ap21bad" ] && [ "$n21" -eq 2 ] && ok "D16 AP21 (SUG-2) TERM while the disk image is attached, and TERM while hdiutil attach runs: exit 3, the image ends detached, the staging folder is removed, nothing copied out or placed" || no "D16 AP21:$ap21bad n=$n21"
mark "D16 end"
mark "D17 module 6"
# ---- module 6 (d17, the key-file decision): FileVault enable + NEW-key file ----------------------------------------------------------------
# Test doubles only, no real FileVault, disk or key: fdesetup is a small state machine (data/fv.state off|on|prog; it reads the
# synthetic key CANARY_KEY from the declared file data/fv.key and prints it ONLY inside the enable plist on its stdout), df (local
# unless data/df.nonlocal; data/df.race plants a file at the key name AFTER the module's last name check) and chown (logs, records
# the key file's mode at that moment; data/chown.widen = a filesystem that ignores the umask, data/chown.fail, data/chown.term).
# Every other tool the module can start is a logging pass-through: the argv and the environment of each child go to
# fvargv.log / fvenv.log, and the privacy checks read those (the key must never be in argv or env of any child).
FVM=workstationadmin-mod-filevault.txt
FVTERM='q=$PPID; t=""; while [ -n "$q" ] && [ "$q" -gt 1 ]; do case "$(/usr/bin/tr '"'"'\0'"'"' '"'"' '"'"' < "/proc/$q/cmdline" 2>/dev/null)" in "/bin/bash "*/workstationadmin-mod-filevault.txt*) t=$q;; esac; q=$(/usr/bin/awk '"'"'/^PPid:/{print $2}'"'"' "/proc/$q/status" 2>/dev/null); done; [ -z "$t" ] || { kill -TERM "$t" && : > "$r/data/term.sent"; }'
fvstubs() { local c
  rm -f "$F/stubs/fdesetup" "$F/stubs/df" "$F/stubs/chown"   # fdesetup is a link to the generic _stub: never write through it
  { printf '#!/bin/bash\nr="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; d="$r/data"\nfvterm() { %s; }\n' "$FVTERM"; cat <<'W'
st=$(/usr/bin/cat "$d/fv.state" 2>/dev/null); tin=pipe; [ -t 0 ] && tin=tty
printf 'fdesetup %s stdin=%s\n' "$*" "$tin" >> "$r/fvcalls.log"
case "${1:-}" in
  status) n=$(( $(/usr/bin/cat "$d/fv.nstatus" 2>/dev/null || echo 0) + 1 )); echo "$n" > "$d/fv.nstatus"
    if [ -f "$d/fv.flip" ] && [ "$n" -ge 2 ] && [ "$st" = off ]; then st=on; echo on > "$d/fv.state"; fi   # another admin turned it on after the first read
    [ ! -f "$d/fv.statusfail" ] || [ "$st" = off ] || exit 1
    case "$st" in on) echo "FileVault is On.";; prog) printf 'FileVault is On.\nEncryption in progress: Percent completed = 1.0\n';; *) echo "FileVault is Off.";; esac; exit 0;;
  haspersonalrecoverykey) if [ "$st" = off ]; then echo false; else echo true; fi; exit 0;;
  list) [ "$st" = off ] || echo 'workstationadmin,ABCDEF01-2345-6789-ABCD-EF0123456789'; exit 0;;
  enable) [ "$*" = "enable -user workstationadmin -outputplist" ] || exit 64
    [ ! -f "$d/fv.enable.term" ] || fvterm
    [ ! -f "$d/fv.enablefail" ] || exit 1
    echo on > "$d/fv.state"
    [ ! -f "$d/fv.enablefail.on" ] || exit 1
    if [ -f "$d/fv.corrupt" ]; then for f in "$r"/keys/*-FileVault-recovery-key.txt; do [ ! -f "$f" ] || printf '%040d\n' 0 >> "$f"; done; fi   # the file changes under the module (another writer): 41 bytes, longer than the key line
    k=$(/usr/bin/cat "$d/fv.key"); [ ! -f "$d/fv.badplist" ] || k=${k%?????}
    printf '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n<plist version="1.0">\n<dict>\n\t<key>EnabledDate</key>\n\t<string>2026-11-18 10:00:00 -0600</string>\n\t<key>EnabledUser</key>\n\t<string>workstationadmin</string>\n\t<key>RecoveryKey</key>\n\t<string>%s</string>\n</dict>\n</plist>\n' "$k"
    exit 0;;
esac
exit 1
W
  } > "$F/stubs/fdesetup"
  cat > "$F/stubs/df" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
r="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"; printf 'df %s\n' "${1:-}" >> "$r/fvcalls.log"
n=$(( $(/usr/bin/cat "$r/data/df.n" 2>/dev/null || echo 0) + 1 )); echo "$n" > "$r/data/df.n"
if [ -f "$r/data/df.race" ] && [ "$n" -eq 2 ]; then printf 'planted\n' > "$(/usr/bin/cat "$r/data/df.race")"; fi   # the 2nd df = the re-check right before the create
printf 'Filesystem 512-blocks Used Available Capacity Mounted on\n'
[ -f "$r/data/df.nonlocal" ] || printf '/dev/disk3s5 489620264 30000000 400000000 7%% /\n'
exit 0
W
  { printf '#!/bin/bash\nr="$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT"\nfvterm() { %s; }\n' "$FVTERM"; cat <<'W'
p=${!#}; printf 'chown %s %s\n' "${1:-}" "${2:-}" >> "$r/fvcalls.log"
/usr/bin/stat -c %a "$p" >> "$r/fvmode.log"
[ ! -f "$r/data/chown.term" ] || fvterm
[ ! -f "$r/data/chown.widen" ] || /usr/bin/chmod 644 "$p"
[ ! -f "$r/data/chown.fail" ] || exit 1
exit 0
W
  } > "$F/stubs/chown"
  chmod 755 "$F/stubs/fdesetup" "$F/stubs/df" "$F/stubs/chown"
  for c in stat ls rm id date od head tee wc awk tr cut sed grep cat dirname basename mktemp chmod mkdir rmdir sort shasum printf; do
    [ -x "/usr/bin/$c" ] || continue
    printf '#!/bin/bash\nprintf '"'"'%%s %%s\\n'"'"' %s "$*" >> "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/fvargv.log"\n/usr/bin/env >> "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/fvenv.log"\nexec /usr/bin/%s "$@"\n' "$c" "$c" > "$F/stubs/$c"
    chmod 755 "$F/stubs/$c"; done; }
fvf() { # TAG: FileVault off, consent yes, key folder /keys (700), asset tag recorded; harness-side shell umask 022 (an operator's usual umask)
  setup "v$1"; fvstubs
  FVB="$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning"; FVK="$F/keys/$CANARY_TAG-FileVault-recovery-key.txt"
  mkdir -p "$F/keys"; chmod 700 "$F/keys"
  printf '%s\n' "$CANARY_TAG" > "$FVB/asset-tag"; chmod 600 "$FVB/asset-tag"; declare_input "$FVB/asset-tag"
  printf '%s\n' "$CANARY_KEY" > "$F/data/fv.key"; declare_input "$F/data/fv.key"
  echo off > "$F/data/fv.state"
  printf 'FILEVAULT_KEY_DIR=/keys\nFILEVAULT_CONSENT=yes\n' >> "$FVB/policy.conf"
  : > "$F/fvcalls.log"; : > "$F/fvargv.log"; : > "$F/fvenv.log"; : > "$F/fvmode.log"; }
fvpol() { grep -v "^$1=" "$FVB/policy.conf" > "$FVB/policy.n"; cat "$FVB/policy.n" > "$FVB/policy.conf"; rm -f "$FVB/policy.n"; [ "$2" = none ] || printf '%s=%s\n' "$1" "$2" >> "$FVB/policy.conf"; }
fvst() { printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}module=filevault${PTAB}.*${PTAB}check=$1${PTAB}" | head -1 | sed -n 's/.*status=\([A-Z_]*\).*/\1/p'; }
fvdet() { printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep "${PTAB}module=filevault${PTAB}.*${PTAB}check=$1${PTAB}" | head -1 | sed -n 's/.*detail=\([^\t\r]*\).*/\1/p'; }
fvnen() { grep -c '^fdesetup enable' "$F/fvcalls.log"; }
fvnk() { find "$F/keys" -mindepth 1 2>/dev/null | wc -l; }
fvrun() { umask 022; run "$FVM" "$@"; umask 077; }
fvtty() { local _a=$1; shift; RUNS=$((RUNS+1)); umask 022   # a pty double types ANSWER (as aptty)
  OUT=$(cd "$F" && { sleep 1; printf '%s\n' "$_a"; sleep 1; } | ptimeout "$RUN_LIMIT" env -i HOME="$F/home" TMPDIR="$F" WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT="$F" /usr/bin/script -qec "/bin/bash $F/bin/$FVM $*" /dev/null 2>"$F/stderr"); RC=$?
  umask 077
  if [ "$RC" -eq 124 ] || [ "$RC" -eq 137 ]; then RUN_TIMEOUTS=$((RUN_TIMEOUTS+1)); FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); printf '%s\n' "$FVM" >> "$T/run-timeouts.log"; echo "FAIL - TIMEOUT ${RUN_LIMIT}s: $FVM $*" | cut -c1-120; fi; }
fvleak() { # PATTERN (default the key) -> FVL: every place it may NOT be: terminal/report output, stderr, child argv, child env, any fixture file except
  # the key file. Call it in the MAIN shell (it declares the key file; a declaration inside $(...) would be lost and read as a tampered manifest)
  local c=${1:-$CANARY_KEY} w="" s
  printf '%s' "$OUT" | grep -qF -- "$c" && w="$w out"
  grep -qF -- "$c" "$F/stderr" 2>/dev/null && w="$w stderr"
  grep -qF -- "$c" "$F/fvargv.log" && w="$w argv"
  grep -qF -- "$c" "$F/fvenv.log" && w="$w env"
  if [ -f "$FVK" ] && [ ! -L "$FVK" ] && ! grep -q "	$FVK\$" "$(decl_file)"; then declare_input "$FVK"; fi
  s=$(scan_fixture "$c"); [ -z "$s" ] || w="$w files:$(printf '%s' "$s" | tr '\n' ' ' | sed "s#$F##g" | cut -c1-160)"
  FVL=$w; }
fvclean() { chmod -R u+rwx "$F" 2>/dev/null; rm -rf "$F"; }
# FV1 inspect (FileVault off, configured): PROPOSED, nothing enabled, nothing written
fvf 1; fvrun --mode inspect
{ [ "$(fvst key-export)" = PROPOSED ] && [ "$(fvnen)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(fvnk)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(grep -c '^chown' "$F/fvcalls.log")" -eq 0 ] && fvleak && [ -z "$FVL" ]; } && ok "D17 FV1 FileVault off, configured, inspect: key-export PROPOSED, no enable, no file" || no "D17 FV1: [$(fvst key-export)] [$(fvdet key-export)] en=$(fvnen) n=$(fvnk)"
fvclean
# FV2 a NEW enable with the typed APPLY (operator shell umask 022): the key file is mode 600 from its first observation (FV2u), holds exactly
# the key (FV2), and the key is nowhere else (FV2p: not in stdout, stderr, report, any child's argv or env, any other file)
fvf 2; fvtty APPLY --mode apply
fv2m=$(tr '\n' ' ' < "$F/fvmode.log")
[ "$fv2m" = "600 " ] && ok "D17 FV2u (umask) the key file was mode 600 at its first observation (right after the exclusive create), with the operator shell at umask 022" || no "D17 FV2u: modes at chown=[$fv2m]"
fv2k=0; [ -f "$FVK" ] && [ ! -L "$FVK" ] && [ "$(stat -c %a "$FVK")" = 600 ] && [ "$(stat -c %s "$FVK")" = 30 ] && [ "$(cat "$FVK")" = "$CANARY_KEY" ] && fv2k=1
{ [ "$(fvst key-export)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ "$(fvst fv-enable)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ "$fv2k" -eq 1 ] && [ "$(fvnen)" -eq 1 ] && grep -qx 'fdesetup enable -user workstationadmin -outputplist stdin=tty' "$F/fvcalls.log" \
  && [ "$(grep -c '^chown -h workstationadmin' "$F/fvcalls.log")" -eq 1 ] && [ "$(fvnk)" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'Type APPLY to continue' && [ "$RC" -eq 2 ]; } \
  && ok "D17 FV2 new enable after the typed APPLY: one fdesetup enable -user OPERATOR -outputplist (terminal stdin), key file <tag>-FileVault-recovery-key.txt mode 600, exactly the key, CHANGED_VERIFIED key-export and fv-enable, rc 2" \
  || no "D17 FV2: [$(fvst key-export)] [$(fvdet key-export)] [$(fvst fv-enable)] file=$fv2k en=$(fvnen) n=$(fvnk) rc=$RC"
fvleak; fv2l=$FVL
{ [ -z "$fv2l" ] && ! printf '%s' "$OUT" | grep -qF "$CANARY_TAG" && [ -s "$F/fvargv.log" ] && [ -s "$F/fvenv.log" ]; } && ok "D17 FV2p the key is in no output, stderr, report record, child argv, child environment or fixture file except the key file; the tag is not printed" || no "D17 FV2p: leak:$fv2l tag=$(printf '%s' "$OUT" | grep -cF "$CANARY_TAG")"
# FV3 idempotent: a second apply on the same Mac (now on) exports nothing, enables nothing, leaves the key file as it is
fv3s=$(cksum < "$FVK" 2>/dev/null); : > "$F/fvcalls.log"; fvtty APPLY --mode apply
{ [ "$(fvst key-export)" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && fvdet key-export | grep -q 'no key exported' && [ "$(fvnen)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(cksum < "$FVK")" = "$fv3s" ] && [ "$(fvnk)" -eq 1 ] && ! printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'Type APPLY' && fvleak && [ -z "$FVL" ]; } \
  && ok "D17 FV3 second apply (now on): no key exported, no enable, no prompt, key file unchanged" || no "D17 FV3: [$(fvst key-export)] [$(fvdet key-export)] en=$(fvnen) n=$(fvnk) same=$([ "$(cksum < "$FVK")" = "$fv3s" ] && echo y) prompt=$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -c 'Type APPLY') leak:$FVL"
fvclean
# FV4 already on or encrypting: no rotation, no re-enable, "no key exported", zero writes, no prompt (inspect and apply)
fv4bad=""
for k in on prog; do fvf "4$k"; echo "$k" > "$F/data/fv.state"
  for md in inspect apply; do fvtty APPLY --mode "$md"
    { [ "$(fvst key-export)" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && fvdet key-export | grep -q 'no key exported' && [ "$(grep -c '^fdesetup \(enable\|changerecovery\|disable\)' "$F/fvcalls.log")" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(fvnk)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(grep -c '^chown' "$F/fvcalls.log")" -eq 0 ] && ! printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'Type APPLY'; } || fv4bad="$fv4bad $k/$md:[$(fvst key-export)]"; done
  fvclean; done
[ -z "$fv4bad" ] && ok "D17 FV4 FileVault already on / encrypting: no key exported, no enable/changerecovery/disable, no file, no prompt (inspect and apply)" || no "D17 FV4:$fv4bad"
# FV5 the typed APPLY: no terminal, or any other answer: PENDING_CONSENT apply-gate, nothing enabled, nothing written
fv5bad=""
fvf 5; fvrun --mode apply; { [ "$(fvst apply-gate)" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && [ "$(fvnen)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(fvnk)" -eq 0 ]; } || fv5bad="$fv5bad notty:[$(fvst apply-gate)]"
for a in apply YES 'APPLY ' ''; do fvtty "$a" --mode apply; { [ "$(fvst apply-gate)" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && [ "$(fvnen)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(fvnk)" -eq 0 ]; } || fv5bad="$fv5bad [$a]:[$(fvst apply-gate)]"; done
fvclean
[ -z "$fv5bad" ] && ok "D17 FV5 apply without a terminal or with another answer than APPLY: PENDING_CONSENT apply-gate, no enable, no file" || no "D17 FV5:$fv5bad"
# FV6 something at the key file name (never overwritten, never followed): a file, a dangling symlink, a symlink to /dev/null, a symlink to a
# folder. Inspect and apply are BLOCKED_POLICY naming it; nothing enabled; the object is unchanged
fv6bad=""
for k in file dangling devnull dirlink; do fvf "6$k"; mkdir -p "$F/elsewhere"
  case "$k" in file) printf 'old\n' > "$FVK"; declare_input "$FVK";; dangling) ln -s "$F/elsewhere/none" "$FVK";; devnull) ln -s /dev/null "$FVK";; dirlink) ln -s "$F/elsewhere" "$FVK";; esac
  fv6s=$(ls -ld "$FVK" | awk '{print $1, $NF}'; cat "$FVK" 2>/dev/null | cksum)
  fvrun --mode inspect; i6=$(fvst key-export)
  fvtty APPLY --mode apply
  { [ "$i6" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(fvst key-export)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && fvdet key-export | grep -q 'already exists' && [ "$(fvnen)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(ls -ld "$FVK" | awk '{print $1, $NF}'; cat "$FVK" 2>/dev/null | cksum)" = "$fv6s" ] && [ ! -e "$F/elsewhere/none" ] && [ "$(fvnk)" -eq 1 ]; } \
    || fv6bad="$fv6bad $k:[$i6][$(fvst key-export)][$(fvdet key-export)]en=$(fvnen)"
  fvclean; done
[ -z "$fv6bad" ] && ok "D17 FV6 an existing file, dangling symlink, /dev/null symlink or folder symlink at the key name: BLOCKED_POLICY (inspect and apply), no enable, object unchanged, nothing written through a link" || no "D17 FV6:$fv6bad"
# FV7 the name race: an object appears at the key name AFTER the module's last name check (the df double plants it): the exclusive create
# (noclobber) refuses it; FAILED before the enable; the planted file is unchanged and not removed; nothing enabled; exit 3
fvf 7; printf '%s\n' "$FVK" > "$F/data/df.race"; fvtty APPLY --mode apply
{ [ "$(fvst key-export)" = FAILED ] && fvdet key-export | grep -q 'refused before the enable' && [ "$(cat "$FVK" 2>/dev/null)" = planted ] && [ "$(fvnen)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$RC" -eq 3 ]; } \
  && ok "D17 FV7 (noclobber) a file planted at the key name after the last check: the exclusive create refuses it, FAILED before the enable, planted file unchanged, no enable, rc 3" || no "D17 FV7: [$(fvst key-export)] [$(fvdet key-export)] planted=[$(cat "$FVK" 2>/dev/null | head -1)] en=$(fvnen) rc=$RC"
fvclean
# FV8 the key folder: relative, traversal and dot forms, a symlinked folder, a folder under a symlinked parent, system/policy/cloud
# folders, missing, writable by others (folder or ancestor), not local, unset: BLOCKED_POLICY, nothing enabled, no key file anywhere
fv8bad=""
for k in rel dotdot dot trail double policy system cloud dirlink parentlink missing ww gwanc nonlocal unset; do fvf "8$k"; v=""
  case "$k" in
    rel) v=keys;; dotdot) v=/keys/../Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning;; dot) v=/keys/.;; trail) v=/keys/;; double) v=//keys;;
    policy) v=/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning;; system) mkdir -p "$F/private/keys"; v=/private/keys;;
    cloud) mkdir -p "$F/Users/Shared/Library/Mobile Documents/k"; chmod 700 "$F/Users/Shared/Library/Mobile Documents/k"; v="/Users/Shared/Library/Mobile Documents/k";;
    dirlink) ln -s "$F/keys" "$F/klink"; declare_input "$F/klink"; v=/klink;;
    parentlink) mkdir -p "$F/real/keys"; chmod 700 "$F/real/keys"; ln -s "$F/real" "$F/plink"; declare_input "$F/plink"; v=/plink/keys;;
    missing) v=/nokeys;; ww) chmod 777 "$F/keys"; v=/keys;; gwanc) mkdir -p "$F/g/keys"; chmod 700 "$F/g/keys"; chmod 775 "$F/g"; v=/g/keys;;
    nonlocal) : > "$F/data/df.nonlocal"; v=/keys;; unset) v=none;; esac
  fvpol FILEVAULT_KEY_DIR "$v"; fvtty APPLY --mode apply
  fv8n=$(find "$F" -name "*FileVault-recovery-key.txt" | wc -l)
  { [ "$(fvst key-export)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(fvnen)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$fv8n" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(grep -c '^chown' "$F/fvcalls.log")" -eq 0 ]; } || fv8bad="$fv8bad $k:[$(fvst key-export)]en=$(fvnen)files=$fv8n"
  fvclean; done
[ -z "$fv8bad" ] && ok "D17 FV8 key folder relative, traversal (..), dot, trailing or double slash, policy/system/cloud folder, symlinked folder or parent, missing, writable by others, not local, unset: BLOCKED_POLICY, no enable, no key file anywhere" || no "D17 FV8:$fv8bad"
# FV9 the asset tag (the file name): traversal, slash, empty, missing, wrong mode: BLOCKED_POLICY, nothing enabled, no key file anywhere
fv9bad=""
for k in dotdot slash empty missing mode644 long; do fvf "9$k"
  case "$k" in dotdot) printf '../evil\n' > "$FVB/asset-tag";; slash) printf 'a/b\n' > "$FVB/asset-tag";; empty) : > "$FVB/asset-tag";;
    missing) rm -f "$FVB/asset-tag";; mode644) chmod 644 "$FVB/asset-tag";; long) printf 'AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA\n' > "$FVB/asset-tag";; esac
  [ ! -f "$FVB/asset-tag" ] || declare_input "$FVB/asset-tag"
  fvtty APPLY --mode apply
  fv9n=$(find "$F" -name "*FileVault-recovery-key.txt" | wc -l)
  { [ "$(fvst key-export)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && fvdet key-export | grep -q 'asset tag' && [ "$(fvnen)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$fv9n" -eq 0 ]; } || fv9bad="$fv9bad $k:[$(fvst key-export)]en=$(fvnen)files=$fv9n"
  fvclean; done
[ -z "$fv9bad" ] && ok "D17 FV9 asset tag with .., slash, empty, missing, mode 644 or too long: BLOCKED_POLICY naming the asset tag, no enable, no key file anywhere" || no "D17 FV9:$fv9bad"
# FV10 consent: FILEVAULT_CONSENT none/no/YES: BLOCKED_POLICY naming it; nothing configured at all: PENDING_CONSENT (manual); no enable
fv10bad=""
for k in none no YES; do fvf "10$k"; fvpol FILEVAULT_CONSENT "$k"; fvtty APPLY --mode apply
  { [ "$(fvst key-export)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && fvdet key-export | grep -q 'FILEVAULT_CONSENT' && [ "$(fvnen)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(fvnk)" -eq 0 ]; } || fv10bad="$fv10bad $k:[$(fvst key-export)]"; fvclean; done
fvf 10n; fvpol FILEVAULT_CONSENT none; fvpol FILEVAULT_KEY_DIR none; fvtty APPLY --mode apply
{ [ "$(fvst key-export)" = PENDING_CONSENT ] && fvdet key-export | grep -q 'not configured' && [ "$(fvnen)" -eq 0 ]; } || fv10bad="$fv10bad unconfigured:[$(fvst key-export)]"; fvclean
[ -z "$fv10bad" ] && ok "D17 FV10 FILEVAULT_CONSENT unset, no or YES: BLOCKED_POLICY naming it; nothing configured: PENDING_CONSENT; never an enable" || no "D17 FV10:$fv10bad"
# FV11 the root gate: build not allowed, operator not admin, another console user: BLOCKED_POLICY apply-gate, no prompt, no enable
fv11bad=""
for k in build nonadmin console; do fvf "11$k"
  case "$k" in build) fvpol ALLOWED_BUILDS 99Z99;; nonadmin) echo "user is not a member of the group" > "$F/data/dsmemberutil_checkmembership_-U_workstationadmin_-G_admin_.out";;
    console) echo otheruser > "$F/data/stat_ctx_-f__Su__dev_console_.out"; : > "$F/data/ctx.lock";; esac
  fvtty APPLY --mode apply
  { [ "$(fvst apply-gate)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && [ "$(fvnen)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(fvnk)" -eq 0 ] && ! printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'Type APPLY'; } || fv11bad="$fv11bad $k:[$(fvst apply-gate)]en=$(fvnen)"; fvclean; done
[ -z "$fv11bad" ] && ok "D17 FV11 apply gate (build 99Z99 not allowed, operator not admin, another console user): BLOCKED_POLICY apply-gate, no prompt, no enable, no file" || no "D17 FV11:$fv11bad"
# FV12 the create step fails BEFORE the enable: folder not writable (0500), a filesystem that ignores the umask (mode 644 after create),
# chown failure, a signal at that moment: FAILED (or exit 3) before the enable, the empty file removed, nothing enabled
fv12bad=""
for k in ro widen chownfail term; do fvf "12$k"
  case "$k" in ro) chmod 500 "$F/keys";; widen) : > "$F/data/chown.widen";; chownfail) : > "$F/data/chown.fail";; term) : > "$F/data/chown.term";; esac
  fvtty APPLY --mode apply; chmod 700 "$F/keys"
  case "$k" in term) { [ -f "$F/data/term.sent" ] && [ "$RC" -eq 3 ]; } || fv12bad="$fv12bad term:rc=$RC";;
    *) { [ "$(fvst key-export)" = FAILED ] && fvdet key-export | grep -q 'refused before the enable' && [ "$RC" -eq 3 ]; } || fv12bad="$fv12bad $k:[$(fvst key-export)][$(fvdet key-export)]rc=$RC";; esac
  { [ "$(fvnen)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(fvnk)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(cat "$F/data/fv.state")" = off ]; } || fv12bad="$fv12bad $k:en=$(fvnen)n=$(fvnk)"
  fvclean; done
[ -z "$fv12bad" ] && ok "D17 FV12 (preflight before enable) folder read-only, mode not 600 after create, chown failure, TERM before the enable: refused before the enable (exit 3), file removed, FileVault still off" || no "D17 FV12:$fv12bad"
# FV13 the enable fails: still off (FAILED fv-enable, nothing lost); on but no exit 0 / a plist without one exact key (FAILED key-export with
# the operator guidance); the empty file is removed; no key or partial key anywhere; exit 3
fv13bad=""
for k in fail failon badplist; do fvf "13$k"
  case "$k" in fail) : > "$F/data/fv.enablefail";; failon) : > "$F/data/fv.enablefail.on";; badplist) : > "$F/data/fv.badplist";; esac
  fvtty APPLY --mode apply
  case "$k" in fail) { [ "$(fvst fv-enable)" = FAILED ] && [ "$(fvst key-export)" != CHANGED_VERIFIED ]; } || fv13bad="$fv13bad fail:[$(fvst fv-enable)]";;
    *) { [ "$(fvst key-export)" = FAILED ] && fvdet key-export | grep -q 'NOT saved' && fvdet key-export | grep -q 'changerecovery'; } || fv13bad="$fv13bad $k:[$(fvst key-export)][$(fvdet key-export)]";; esac
  fvleak "QQQQ-WWWW-EEEE-RRRR-TTTT"; l13=$FVL
  { [ "$(fvnen)" -eq 1 ] && [ "$(fvnk)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$RC" -eq 3 ] && [ -z "$l13" ]; } || fv13bad="$fv13bad $k:en=$(fvnen)n=$(fvnk)rc=$RC leak:$l13"
  fvclean; done
[ -z "$fv13bad" ] && ok "D17 FV13 enable failed (still off: FAILED fv-enable) / on without exit 0 / plist without one exact key: FAILED with operator guidance, file removed, no key or partial key anywhere, rc 3" || no "D17 FV13:$fv13bad"
# FV14 write failure after the enable (the key file changes under the module): FAILED key-export with the operator guidance, the file removed,
# the key never printed or kept anywhere, rc 3
fvf 14; : > "$F/data/fv.corrupt"; fvtty APPLY --mode apply; fvleak; l14=$FVL
{ [ "$(fvst key-export)" = FAILED ] && fvdet key-export | grep -q 'Do not restart' && [ "$(fvnen)" -eq 1 ] && [ "$(fvnk)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$RC" -eq 3 ] && [ -z "$l14" ]; } \
  && ok "D17 FV14 (write failure after enable) FAILED key-export, guidance (do not restart, changerecovery by hand), incomplete file removed, key nowhere, rc 3" || no "D17 FV14: [$(fvst key-export)] [$(fvdet key-export)] en=$(fvnen) n=$(fvnk) rc=$RC leak:$l14"
fvclean
# FV15 signals: TERM while fdesetup enable runs is held until the key is on disk (the key is written, CHANGED_VERIFIED)
fvf 15; : > "$F/data/fv.enable.term"; fvtty APPLY --mode apply
{ [ -f "$F/data/term.sent" ] && [ "$(fvst key-export)" = CHANGED_VERIFIED ] && [ "$(cat "$FVK" 2>/dev/null)" = "$CANARY_KEY" ] && fvleak && [ -z "$FVL" ]; } \
  && ok "D17 FV15 TERM during fdesetup enable: ignored until the key is on disk; key file written and verified" || no "D17 FV15: sent=$([ -f "$F/data/term.sent" ] && echo y) [$(fvst key-export)] rc=$RC n=$(fvnk)"
fvclean
# FV16 the state is read again right before the create: off at the first read, on at the second: BLOCKED_POLICY, nothing enabled or written
fvf 16; : > "$F/data/fv.flip"; fvtty APPLY --mode apply
{ [ "$(fvst key-export)" = BLOCKED_POLICY ] && fvdet key-export | grep -q 'no longer reported off' && [ "$(fvnen)" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(fvnk)" -eq 0 ]; } \
  && ok "D17 FV16 FileVault turned on between the first read and the apply: BLOCKED_POLICY, no enable, no file" || no "D17 FV16: [$(fvst key-export)] [$(fvdet key-export)] en=$(fvnen)"
fvclean
# FV17 redaction unit (the report filter): a key-shaped value is replaced, also inside a longer detail; a registered word is redacted
fvf 17; sed -n '/^WSP_RE_IP=/,/^}/p' "$F/bin/workstationadmin-lib.txt" > "$F/norm.sh"
fv17=$(bash -c '. "$1"; WSP_REDACT_WORDS=" ZZTAG42"; wsp_norm "$2"; echo; wsp_norm "x $2 y"; echo; wsp_norm "folder ZZTAG42 here"' _ "$F/norm.sh" "$CANARY_KEY" 2>&1)
{ ! printf '%s' "$fv17" | grep -qF "$CANARY_KEY" && ! printf '%s' "$fv17" | grep -qF "$CANARY_TAG" && [ "$(printf '%s\n' "$fv17" | grep -c 'secret-shape')" -eq 2 ] && printf '%s' "$fv17" | grep -q 'redacted'; } \
  && ok "D17 FV17 report filter: a 6x4 recovery-key shape becomes [secret-shape] (alone and inside a detail); a registered word becomes [redacted]" || no "D17 FV17: [$(printf '%s' "$fv17" | tr '\n' '|' | sed "s/$CANARY_KEY/KEY/g" | cut -c1-160)]"
fvclean
# FV18 text pins: one enable call (with -outputplist, terminal stdin), no rotation/disable/defer call, the key file is never read back
fv18bad=""
[ "$(grep -c 'wsp_run _p fdesetup enable -user "\$WSP_OPERATOR" -outputplist < /dev/tty$' "$SUITE/$FVM")" -eq 1 ] || fv18bad="$fv18bad enable-call"
grep -v '^ *#' "$SUITE/$FVM" | grep -n 'wsp_run[^"]*fdesetup *\(changerecovery\|disable\|authrestart\)\|-defer\|-inputplist' | grep -q . && fv18bad="$fv18bad rotation-call"
grep -v '^ *#' "$SUITE/$FVM" | grep -q 'cat "\$FV_PATH"\|< *"\$FV_PATH"' && fv18bad="$fv18bad readback"
[ -z "$fv18bad" ] && ok "D17 FV18 module text: exactly one fdesetup enable -outputplist call (terminal stdin), no changerecovery/disable/authrestart/defer call, the key file is never read back" || no "D17 FV18:$fv18bad"
mark "D17 end"
# --- D8-01: downstream transform faults. Every text tool a module could pipe a
# captured fact through is made to fail (rc1, no output) or to lie (first byte only,
# rc0). Premise being tested: "a safe capture makes later steps trustworthy". For
# each broken tool, every reported (check,status) must be UNKNOWN/BLOCKED_POLICY or
# identical to the clean run; any other status is a decision taken on a damaged
# transform. D9 (reviewer B1): the sweep runs twice - on the default fixtures and on
# MATCH-PATH fixtures where the grep/awk-shaped answer IS the clean result (a managed
# SoftwareUpdate profile, nested ssh groups, both pmset sections, a FileVault user line
# with a volume owner, a network-safe host name), so a tool that fails silently can no
# longer hide behind "no match is the clean answer".
mark "D8-01: downstream transform fault sweep"
DTOOLS="tr sed cut sort uniq paste wc awk grep"
MPTOOLS="grep awk sed tr cut"
dsw() { # TAG: sweep -> $T/dsw.TAG holds "check status" lines (sorted)
  sweep >/dev/null
  awk -F'\t' '{m="";c="";st=""; for(i=1;i<=NF;i++){ if($i ~ /^module=/) m=$i; if($i ~ /^check=/) c=$i; if($i ~ /^status=/) st=$i } if(m!="" && c!="" && st!="") print m " " c " " st}' "$T/sweep.log" | sort > "$T/dsw.$1"; }
sn() { printf '%s_' "$@" | tr -c 'A-Za-z0-9._-' '_' | cut -c1-120; }   # stub data-file stem, same rule as the generic stub
mpfx() { # match-path fixtures on the current fixture $F
  printf '_computerlevel[1] attribute: profileIdentifier: 3F2A1C9E-5B7D-4C1E-9A60-0D2E4B6F8A10\n_computerlevel[1] payload[1] type = com.apple.SoftwareUpdate\n' > "$F/data/$(sn profiles show -type configuration).out"
  for k in AutomaticCheckEnabled AutomaticDownload ConfigDataInstall CriticalUpdateInstall; do
    echo 0 > "$F/data/$(sn defaults read /Library/Preferences/com.apple.SoftwareUpdate "$k").out"; done
  printf 'NestedGroups: ABCDEF01-0000-0000-0000-000000000000\n' > "$F/data/$(sn dscl . -read /Groups/com.apple.access_ssh NestedGroups).out"
  printf 'Battery Power:\n displaysleep 20\nAC Power:\n displaysleep 30\n' > "$F/data/$(sn pmset -g custom).out"
  printf 'workstationadmin,ABCDEF01-2345-6789-ABCD-EF0123456789\n' > "$F/data/$(sn fdesetup list).out"
  printf 'Volume Owner: Yes\n' > "$F/data/$(sn diskutil apfs listUsers /).out"
  echo arm64 > "$F/data/uname_-m_.out"; }
d8sweep() { # LABEL TOOLS : sets d8_fail (0/1), prints one ok/no pair
  local lbl="$1" tools="$2" t md tmo0 tmo1 L
  [ "${WSP_HARNESS_SKIP_D8SWEEP:-0}" = 1 ] && { echo "# D8-01 $lbl sweep SKIPPED (mutation run only)"; return 0; }
  d8bad=0; d8diff=0; d8n=0
  dsw clean; dsw clean2
  if cmp -s "$T/dsw.clean" "$T/dsw.clean2" && [ -s "$T/dsw.clean" ]; then ok "D8-01 $lbl control: two clean sweeps agree ($(wc -l < "$T/dsw.clean") report lines)"; else no "D8-01 $lbl control: clean sweeps differ or are empty"; fi
  for t in $tools; do for md in rc1 trunc; do
    tmo0=0; [ -f "$T/run-timeouts.log" ] && tmo0=$(wc -l < "$T/run-timeouts.log")
    printf '%s\n' "$md" > "$F/data/$t.mode"; dsw "$t.$md"; rm -f "$F/data/$t.mode"
    tmo1=0; [ -f "$T/run-timeouts.log" ] && tmo1=$(wc -l < "$T/run-timeouts.log")
    [ "$tmo1" -gt "$tmo0" ] && echo "# D8-01 hang (60s timeout) with $t/$md"
    d8n=$((d8n+1))
    cmp -s "$T/dsw.clean" "$T/dsw.$t.$md" || d8diff=$((d8diff+1))
    while IFS= read -r L; do
      case "$L" in *" status=UNKNOWN"|*" status=BLOCKED_POLICY") continue;; esac
      grep -Fxq -- "$L" "$T/dsw.clean" || { d8bad=$((d8bad+1)); echo "# D8-01 $lbl flip with $t/$md: $L"; }
    done < "$T/dsw.$t.$md"
  done; done
  [ "$d8bad" -eq 0 ] && ok "D8-01 $lbl: $d8n broken-tool sweeps ($tools x rc1/trunc): no status other than the clean-run value or a fail-closed UNKNOWN/BLOCKED_POLICY" || no "D8-01 $lbl: $d8bad status flips under broken text tools"
  [ "$d8diff" -ge 1 ] && ok "D8-01 $lbl non-vacuous: $d8diff of $d8n broken-tool sweeps changed the report (wrappers are live)" || no "D8-01 $lbl vacuous: no broken tool changed anything"; }
setup 68; mkdir -p "$F/home/Library/Preferences"; chmod 700 "$F/home"
for t in $DTOOLS; do mkwrap "$t" "  rc1) exit 1;;
  trunc) /usr/bin/$t \"\$@\" | head -c 1; exit 0;;"; done
d8sweep default "$DTOOLS"
rm -rf "$T/fx68"
# match-path fixtures: the clean result must itself be the match path, asserted below
setup 72; mkdir -p "$F/home/Library/Preferences"; chmod 700 "$F/home"
printf 'USERS=%s\nTARGET_OS_MAJOR=15\nAPP_LIST=ExampleChat/ExampleChat\n' "$(id -un)" > "$F/Library/Management/WorkstationProvisioning-Session/session.conf"
mpfx
for t in $MPTOOLS; do mkwrap "$t" "  rc1) exit 1;;
  trunc) /usr/bin/$t \"\$@\" | head -c 1; exit 0;;"; done
dsw mpclean
mpmiss=""
mpx() { # MODULE CHECK STATUS: exactly one report line for that module+check, with exactly that status (D10 S2)
  [ "$(grep -c "^module=$1 check=$2 " "$T/dsw.mpclean")" -eq 1 ] && grep -Fxq "module=$1 check=$2 status=$3" "$T/dsw.mpclean" || mpmiss="$mpmiss $1/$2=$3"; }
mpx updates AutomaticCheckEnabled BLOCKED_POLICY
mpx updates AutomaticDownload BLOCKED_POLICY
mpx updates ConfigDataInstall BLOCKED_POLICY
mpx updates CriticalUpdateInstall BLOCKED_POLICY
mpx userspace-audit ssh-allowlist UNKNOWN
mpx power displaysleep-battery ALREADY_CORRECT
mpx power displaysleep-ac ALREADY_CORRECT
mpx filevault unlock-operator PENDING_LOGIN
mpx filevault volume-owner PENDING_CONSENT
mpx userspace-audit localhostname-shape PENDING_CONSENT
[ -z "$mpmiss" ] && ok "D8-01 match-path fixtures reach the match path in the clean run, each asserted as one exact module+check+status (updates profile, nested groups, pmset sections, FileVault user and volume owner, host-name shape)" || { no "D8-01 match-path fixtures missed:$mpmiss"; grep -a 'AutomaticCheck\|ssh-allow\|displaysleep\|unlock-op\|volume-owner\|localhost' "$T/dsw.mpclean" | head -16; }
d8sweep match-path "$MPTOOLS"
rm -rf "$T/fx72"
# --- D8-01b: account existence is a typed read (wsp_user_state), not a bare dscl status ---
mark "D8-01b: operator record existence is tri-state"
setup 69; mkdir -p "$F/home/Library/Preferences"; chmod 700 "$F/home"
UF="$F/data/dscl_._-read__Users_workstationadmin_UniqueID_"
run workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt --mode inspect
printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=operator-record' | grep -q 'status=ALREADY_CORRECT' && ok "operator record control: UniqueID 501, exit 0 -> ALREADY_CORRECT" || no "operator record control"
run UserSpace-audit.txt; printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=operator-admin' | grep -q 'status=' && ok "audit control: operator checks present" || no "audit operator control"
for cs in "1:" "2:" "255:" "56:"; do
  rcv=${cs%%:*}; : > "$UF.out"; echo "$rcv" > "$UF.rc"; rm -f "$UF.err"
  [ "$rcv" = 56 ] && echo "<dscl_cmd> DS Error: -14136 (eDSRecordNotFound)" > "$UF.err"
  for sc in workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt UserSpace-audit.txt; do
    if [ "$sc" = UserSpace-audit.txt ]; then run "$sc"; else run "$sc" --mode inspect; fi
    st=$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=operator-record' | sed -n 's/.*status=\([A-Z_]*\).*/\1/p' | head -1)
    if [ "$rcv" = 56 ]; then want=FAILED; else want=UNKNOWN; fi
    [ "$st" = "$want" ] && ok "dscl exit $rcv, no output -> operator-record $want ($sc)" || no "dscl exit $rcv -> operator-record '$st' (wanted $want) in $sc"
  done
done
# D9 (S6): "no" needs exit 56 AND empty stdout AND the not-found class
: > "$UF.out"; echo 56 > "$UF.rc"; rm -f "$UF.err"
for sc in workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt UserSpace-audit.txt; do
  if [ "$sc" = UserSpace-audit.txt ]; then run "$sc"; else run "$sc" --mode inspect; fi
  printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=operator-record' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' && ok "dscl exit 56 without the not-found error text -> UNKNOWN ($sc)" || no "exit 56 w/o error class accepted as missing ($sc)"
done
echo "<dscl_cmd> DS Error: -14136 (eDSRecordNotFound)" > "$UF.err"; echo "UniqueID: 501" > "$UF.out"; echo 56 > "$UF.rc"
run workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt --mode inspect
printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=operator-record' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' && ok "dscl exit 56 with stdout present -> UNKNOWN" || no "exit 56 with stdout accepted as missing"
echo "No such key: UniqueID" > "$UF.err"; : > "$UF.out"; echo 56 > "$UF.rc"
run workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt --mode inspect
printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=operator-record' | grep -q 'status=FAILED' && ok "exit 56, empty stdout, 'No such key' class -> FAILED (both not-found texts accepted)" || no "No such key variant"
rm -f "$UF.err"
printf 'UniqueID: 5\0\n' > "$UF.out"; echo 0 > "$UF.rc"
run workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt --mode inspect
printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=operator-record' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' && ok "UniqueID with NUL -> operator-record UNKNOWN" || no "operator record NUL"
printf 'UniqueID: 501\nUniqueID: 502\n' > "$UF.out"; echo 0 > "$UF.rc"
run workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt --mode inspect
printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=operator-record' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' && ok "two UniqueID lines -> operator-record UNKNOWN" || no "operator record two lines"
rm -rf "$T/fx69"
# --- D7-01 in-shell parsers replacing awk/sed/tr/wc (unit, extracted functions) ---
mark "in-shell parser units"
UO=$(SUITE="$SUITE" T="$T" timeout 20 /bin/bash -c '
  WSP_NL="
"
  sed -n "/^wsp_line_match() {/,/^wsp_words() {.*}\$/p" "$SUITE/workstationadmin-lib.txt" > "$T/unit-lib.sh" || exit 9
  sed -n "/^count_uid() {/,/^  IFS=\$_ifs; set +f/p" "$SUITE/workstationadmin-mod-accounts.txt" > "$T/unit-acct.sh" || exit 9
  . "$T/unit-lib.sh"; . "$T/unit-acct.sh"
  L="root 0
a 501
b 502
c 501
odd"
  printf "%s|" "$(count_uid "$L" 501)" "$(count_uid "$L" 502)" "$(count_uid "$L" 9)" "$(count_uid "$L" "*")"
  printf "%s|" "$(wsp_field "UniqueID: 501" UniqueID:)"; wsp_field "UniqueID: 501
UniqueID: 502" UniqueID: || printf "dupkey|"; wsp_field "UniqueID: 5 01" UniqueID: || printf "3fields|"
  wsp_field "NFSHomeDirectory: /Users/a*" NFSHomeDirectory: && printf "|"
  wsp_words "  a   b
c "; printf "%s:%s|" "$WSP_NWORDS" "$WSP_WORDS"; wsp_words "*"; printf "%s|" "$WSP_WORDS"
  wsp_line_match "x
MDM enrollment: No" "MDM enrollment: " && printf "%s|" "$WSP_MATCH"
  wsp_line_match "MDM enrollment: No
MDM enrollment: Yes" "MDM enrollment: " || printf "dupline|"' 2>&1)
[ "$UO" = "2|1|0|0|501|dupkey|3fields|/Users/a*|3:a b c|*|MDM enrollment: No|dupline|" ] && ok "in-shell parsers: uid count, unique key field, word normalise, single-line match (no glob expansion)" || no "parser units: $UO"
# --- D7-01: capture VALIDATOR (awk) failures fail closed ---
# --- d12 R3: unit tests for the library survivors of the d11 review (offset, length, any-line-re-rc2) ---
mark "d12 R3: capture units (offset, length) and ERE units"
mkdir -p "$T/cu-bin"
cat > "$T/cu-bin/od" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
# od wrapper for the capture unit: misbehaves only in the mode named by OD_MODE (a >=17-byte value has several data lines)
case "${OD_MODE:-}" in
  swap) /usr/bin/od "$@" | /usr/bin/awk 'NR==1{a=$0;next} NR==2{print $0; print a; next} {print}';;
  finaladdr) /usr/bin/od "$@" | /usr/bin/awk '{l[NR]=$0} END{for(i=1;i<NR;i++) print l[i]; print "0000077"}';;
  dupdrop) /usr/bin/od "$@" | /usr/bin/awk 'NR==1{print;print;next} NR==2{next} {print}';;
  *) exec /usr/bin/od "$@";;
esac
W
chmod 755 "$T/cu-bin/od"
sed -n '/^wsp_classify_stream() {/,/^wsp_script_hash() {/p' "$SUITE/workstationadmin-lib.txt" | sed '$d' > "$T/cu-lib.sh"
sed -n '/^wsp_any_line_re() {/,/^wsp_re() {/p' "$SUITE/workstationadmin-lib.txt" > "$T/cu-re.sh"
cat > "$T/cu-unit.sh" <<'W'
set -u
PATH="$T/cu-bin:$PATH"
. "$T/cu-lib.sh"; . "$T/cu-re.sh"
V='AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAABBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBCCCCCCCC'
wsp_run v printf '%s\n' "$V"; printf 'ctl:%s:%s:%s|' "$WSP_CAPTURE" "$WSP_RC" "${#v}"
for m in swap finaladdr dupdrop; do OD_MODE=$m wsp_run v printf '%s\n' "$V"; printf '%s:%s:%s:%s|' "$m" "$WSP_CAPTURE" "$WSP_RC" "${#v}"; done
printf() { if [ "${1:-}" = '%b' ]; then builtin printf '%s' short; else builtin printf "$@"; fi; }
wsp_run v printf '%s\n' "$V"; printf 'len:%s:%s:%s|' "$WSP_CAPTURE" "$WSP_RC" "${#v}"
unset -f printf
# d13 (d12 survivor limit+count-octal-length): an over-limit capture whose OTHER layers pass (head -c cap+1 keeps the bytes, wc and the
# octal length agree with n) must report capture=limit, rc 255, empty value; exactly at the cap it is ok. Without the awk limit check the
# octal-length layer says 'count'; without both, only the decode-length layer is left and says 'decode': both differ from 'limit'.
WSP_CAP_MAX=64
A64=$(printf '%064d' 0); A65=$(printf '%065d' 0); A900=$(printf '%0900d' 0)
wsp_run v printf '%s' "$A64"; printf 'cap64:%s:%s:%s|' "$WSP_CAPTURE" "$WSP_RC" "${#v}"
wsp_run v printf '%s' "$A65"; printf 'cap65:%s:%s:%s|' "$WSP_CAPTURE" "$WSP_RC" "${#v}"
wsp_run v printf '%s' "$A900"; printf 'cap900:%s:%s:%s|' "$WSP_CAPTURE" "$WSP_RC" "${#v}"
WSP_CAP_MAX=1048576
wsp_any_line_re a '('; printf 're1:%s|' "$?"
wsp_any_line_re "x${WSP_NL}abc" b; printf 're2:%s|' "$?"
wsp_any_line_re "x${WSP_NL}abc" q; printf 're3:%s|' "$?"
wsp_re a '('; printf 're4:%s|' "$?"
wsp_re abc '^abc$'; printf 're5:%s|' "$?"
wsp_re abc '^abd$'; printf 're6:%s|' "$?"
W
CU=$(T="$T" timeout 60 /bin/bash "$T/cu-unit.sh" 2>&1)
[ "$CU" = "ctl:ok:0:40|swap:offset:255:0|finaladdr:offset:255:0|dupdrop:offset:255:0|len:decode:255:0|cap64:ok:0:64|cap65:limit:255:0|cap900:limit:255:0|re1:2|re2:0|re3:1|re4:2|re5:0|re6:1|" ] \
  && ok "d12 R3 units (d13: + over-limit capture layers): a 64-byte value at a 64-byte cap is ok, 65 and 900 bytes give capture 'limit' (rc 255, empty value; asserts the status so that the double mutant limit+count-octal-length, which would give decode, cannot survive); a 41-byte value through od wrappers that SWAP data lines, change the FINAL address, or DUPLICATE+DROP a line -> capture 'offset', rc 255, value empty (control ok); a shadowed printf returning a short %b decode -> capture 'decode', rc 255; wsp_any_line_re and wsp_re return 2 for an invalid ERE (0 match, 1 none)" \
  || no "d12 R3 units: $CU"
rm -rf "$T/cu-bin" "$T/cu-lib.sh" "$T/cu-re.sh" "$T/cu-unit.sh"
mark "D7-01: capture validator (awk) faults"
# awk wrapper misbehaves only for the capture validator (args contain max=);
# every other awk use passes through. The stderr classifier is a builtin read
# loop (no external stage to inject); its pipeline status is covered by pipefail.
mkawk() { cat > "$F/stubs/awk" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash
m=$(cat "$WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT/data/awk.mode" 2>/dev/null)
case " $* " in *" max="*) ;; *) m="";; esac
case "$m" in
  rc1) /usr/bin/awk "$@"; exit 1;;
  trunc0) /usr/bin/awk "$@" | head -c 40;;
  trunc1) /usr/bin/awk "$@" | head -c 40; exit 1;;
  dropbyte) /usr/bin/awk "$@" | sed '1s/^.....//';;
  lien) /usr/bin/awk "$@" | /usr/bin/awk 'NR==2 && $1=="ok" { $3=$3-1; printf "%-80s\n", $0; next } { print }';;
  nomarker) /usr/bin/awk "$@" | sed '$d';;
  endmark) /usr/bin/awk "$@" | /usr/bin/sed 's/^WSPEND$/WSPENX/';;
  metanl) /usr/bin/awk "$@" | /usr/bin/sed '2s/ $/\n/';;
  *) exec /usr/bin/awk "$@";;
esac
W
chmod 755 "$F/stubs/awk"; }
d71() { setup 71
  printf '15.5\nUNEXPECTED-TRAILING-LINE-PADDING\n' > "$F/data/sw_vers_-productVersion_.out"
  mkawk; echo "$1" > "$F/data/awk.mode"
  run workstationadmin-mod-firewall.txt --mode apply
  if printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'check=apply-gate' && ! grep -q '^socketfilterfw --setglobalstate' "$F/calls.log" && [ "$(fld module-start os)" = unknown ]; then
    run workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt --mode inspect
    printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=target-os' | grep -q 'status=UNKNOWN' && echo "PASS awk/$1" || echo "FAIL preflight awk/$1"
  else echo "FAIL gate awk/$1"; fi
  rm -rf "$T/fx71"; }
: > "$T/d71.log"
for m in rc1 trunc0 trunc1 dropbyte lien nomarker; do d71 $m >> "$T/d71.log"; done
n=$(grep -c '^PASS' "$T/d71.log"); f=$(grep -c '^FAIL' "$T/d71.log")
[ "$n" -eq 6 ] && [ "$f" -eq 0 ] && ok "D7-01: 6 validator faults (rc1, truncated rc0/rc1, dropped byte, ok-meta with wrong length, missing end marker) fail closed" || { no "D7-01 pass=$n fail=$f"; grep '^FAIL' "$T/d71.log"; }
# D9 (S1): faults that MATTER. A valid ONE-line version, so output alone would be accepted:
# awk exiting 1 after emitting a valid frame, or lying about the byte count, must give UNKNOWN.
# Deleting the validator "|| wsp_run_fail" clause in wsp_run makes rc1 here be ACCEPTED (mutation run
# in the author evidence). Same wrapper at rc 0 / pass-through is accepted (control).
d71b() { setup 71; printf '15.5\n' > "$F/data/sw_vers_-productVersion_.out"; mkawk; echo "$1" > "$F/data/awk.mode"
  run workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt --mode inspect
  st=$(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=target-os' | sed -n 's/.*status=\([A-Z_]*\).*/\1/p' | head -1)
  if [ "$1" = ok ]; then [ "$st" = ALREADY_CORRECT ] && echo "PASS awk/$1" || echo "FAIL awk/$1 -> $st"
  else [ "$st" = UNKNOWN ] && [ "$(fld module-start os)" = unknown ] && echo "PASS awk/$1" || echo "FAIL awk/$1 -> $st"; fi
  rm -rf "$T/fx71"; }
: > "$T/d71b.log"; for m in ok rc1 trunc1 lien endmark metanl; do d71b $m >> "$T/d71b.log"; done
[ "$(grep -c '^PASS' "$T/d71b.log")" -eq 6 ] && ok "D7-01b: on a valid one-line version, validator exit 1 / truncated rc1 / wrong byte count / same-length wrong end marker / a newline inside the meta record -> UNKNOWN; same wrapper in pass-through -> accepted" || { no "D7-01b: $(grep '^FAIL' "$T/d71b.log" | tr '\n' ' ')"; }
# control: the awk wrapper in pass-through mode keeps a valid capture valid
setup 71; printf '15.5\n' > "$F/data/sw_vers_-productVersion_.out"; mkawk
run workstationadmin-mod-preflight.txt --mode inspect
printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=target-os' | grep -q 'status=ALREADY_CORRECT' && [ "$(fld module-start os)" = 15.5 ] && ok "awk wrapper pass-through control: valid capture accepted" || no "awk pass-through control: $(printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep 'check=target-os' | tr '\t' ' ' | cut -c1-160)"
rm -rf "$T/fx71"
# --- D7-02: identity/console guard faults at EVERY guard consumer ---
mark "D7-02: identity/console guard faults"
# CONSUMER|ROLE-READERS|good value. Each fault: tool exit 1 with plausible
# output, NUL, two lines, malformed, other user. Expect refusal and no writes.
d72() { # SCRIPT ARGS READER(id|con) GOODUSER FAULT -> PASS/FAIL line
  local sc="$1" ar="$2" rd="$3" gu="$4" ft="$5" k
  setup 72; mkdir -p "$F/home/Library/Preferences"; chmod 700 "$F/home"; touch "$F/data/ctx.lock"
  printf '%s\n' "$gu" > "$F/data/id_ctx_-un_.out"; printf '%s\n' "$gu" > "$F/data/stat_ctx_-f__Su__dev_console_.out"
  case "$rd" in id) k=id_ctx_-un_;; con) k=stat_ctx_-f__Su__dev_console_;; esac
  case "$ft" in
    rc1) printf '%s\n' "$gu" > "$F/data/$k.out"; echo 1 > "$F/data/$k.rc";;
    nul) printf '%s\0\n' "$gu" > "$F/data/$k.out";;
    twoline) printf '%s\n%s\n' "$gu" "$gu" > "$F/data/$k.out";;
    malformed) printf '%s x\n' "$gu" > "$F/data/$k.out";;
    other) printf 'zzother\n' > "$F/data/$k.out";;
    none) ;;
  esac
  # shellcheck disable=SC2086 # ar is a fixed literal word list
  run "$sc" $ar
  if [ "$ft" = none ]; then
    if [ "$sc" = workstationadmin-mod-firewall.txt ]; then
      printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'check=apply-gate' && echo "FAIL control-refused $sc" || echo "PASS control $sc"
    else [ "$RC" -ne 4 ] && ! grep -q REFUSED "$F/stderr" && echo "PASS control $sc" || echo "FAIL control-refused $sc rc=$RC"; fi
  elif [ "$sc" = workstationadmin-mod-firewall.txt ]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$OUT" | grep -q 'check=apply-gate' && ! grep -q '^socketfilterfw --setglobalstate' "$F/calls.log" && echo "PASS $sc $rd $ft" || echo "FAIL $sc $rd $ft"
  else
    [ "$RC" -eq 4 ] && grep -q REFUSED "$F/stderr" && ! grep -qE '^(defaults write|dscl \. -(create|append)|sysadminctl|fdesetup enable)' "$F/calls.log" && echo "PASS $sc $rd $ft" || echo "FAIL $sc $rd $ft rc=$RC"
  fi
  # the reader must actually have been consulted (non-vacuous)
  case "$rd" in id) grep -q '^id_ctx -un' "$F/calls.log";; con) grep -q '^stat_ctx -f %Su' "$F/calls.log";; esac || echo "FAIL not-consulted $sc $rd $ft"
  rm -rf "$T/fx72"; }
: > "$T/d72.log"; d72n=0
while IFS='|' read -r sc ar rds gu; do
  [ "$gu" = '$H' ] && gu=$HARNESS_USER
  # shellcheck disable=SC2086 # rds is a fixed literal word list
  for rd in $rds; do d72 "$sc" "$ar" "$rd" "$gu" none >> "$T/d72.log"; d72n=$((d72n+1))
    for ft in rc1 nul twoline malformed other; do d72 "$sc" "$ar" "$rd" "$gu" "$ft" >> "$T/d72.log"; d72n=$((d72n+1)); done; done
  mark "d72 $sc"
done <<'L'
workstationadmin-provision.txt|--mode inspect|id con|workstationadmin
workstationadmin-mod-firewall.txt|--mode apply|con|workstationadmin
workstationadmin-mod-admin-session.txt|--mode inspect|id con|workstationadmin
UserSpace-audit.txt||id con|$H
L
n=$(grep -c '^PASS' "$T/d72.log"); f=$(grep -c '^FAIL' "$T/d72.log")
[ "$n" -eq "$d72n" ] && [ "$f" -eq 0 ] && ok "D7-02: $d72n guard cases (5 consumers; rc1/NUL/two-line/malformed/other per reader + controls) refuse; readers consulted" || { no "D7-02 pass=$n/$d72n fail=$f"; grep '^FAIL' "$T/d72.log" | head -20; }
# --- D7-03: harness timeout propagation (self-test sub-harness) ---
mark "D7-03: harness timeout propagation self-test"
STO=$(WSP_HARNESS_SELFTEST=sweep-timeout WSP_HARNESS_RUN_LIMIT=2 timeout -k 5 120 /bin/bash "$SUITE/workstationadmin-test-harness.txt" "$SUITE" 2>&1); src=$?
[ "$src" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$STO" | grep -Eq '^SUMMARY pass=[0-9]+ fail=[1-9]' && printf '%s\n' "$STO" | grep -q '^# runs=[0-9]* timeouts=[1-9]' && printf '%s\n' "$STO" | grep -q 'TIMEOUT 2s' \
  && ok "injected timeout inside \$(sweep): FAIL, SUMMARY fail>=1, exit 1" || no "selftest sweep-timeout rc=$src: $(printf '%s\n' "$STO" | grep -E 'SUMMARY|timeouts=' | tr '\n' ' ')"
STO=$(WSP_HARNESS_SELFTEST=sweep-clean WSP_HARNESS_RUN_LIMIT=2 timeout -k 5 120 /bin/bash "$SUITE/workstationadmin-test-harness.txt" "$SUITE" 2>&1); src=$?
[ "$src" -eq 0 ] && printf '%s\n' "$STO" | grep -q '^SUMMARY pass=0 fail=0' && printf '%s\n' "$STO" | grep -q 'selftest sweep positives: .*sip' \
  && ok "selftest control (no hang): exit 0, fail=0, sweep has positives" || no "selftest sweep-clean rc=$src"
# --- DV01: inherited exported salt at every entry boundary (child env watched) ---
mark "DV01: inherited exported salt at every entry boundary (child"
for e in workstationadmin-provision.txt UserSpace-audit.txt workstationadmin-mod-gates.txt; do
  setup "51-${e%.txt}"; mkdir -p "$F/home/Library/Preferences"; chmod 700 "$F/home"
  OUT=$(cd "$F" && env -i HOME="$F/home" TMPDIR="$F" WSP_FIXTURE_ROOT="$F" WSP_RID_SALT=00ff SHELLOPTS=allexport /bin/bash "$F/bin/$e" </dev/null 2>/dev/null)
  grep -q . "$F/calls.log" && c=1 || c=0
  [ "$c" -eq 1 ] && [ ! -s "$F/envleak.log" ] && ok "entry $e: children ran, none saw WSP_RID_SALT" || no "entry $e c=$c leak=$(wc -l < "$F/envleak.log")"
done
# --- T12 static invariants ---
mark "T12 static invariants ---"
for f in "$SUITE"/workstationadmin-*.txt "$SUITE"/UserSpace-*.txt; do
  grep -nE '(^|[^_a-z])eval |declare -A|typeset -A|mapfile|readarray|\$\{[a-zA-Z_]+(,,|\^\^)\}|\|&|set -x|TCC\.db|sqlite3|csrutil disable|spctl --master-disable|curl |wget |[^a-z]nc ' "$f" | grep -v '^[0-9]*: *#' | grep -v 'grep -nE' >/dev/null && no "forbidden construct in $(basename "$f")" || ok "static clean $(basename "$f")"; done
awk '/^[^#]/ && /EUID/ {print; exit}' "$SUITE/workstationadmin-lib.txt" | grep -q 'case' && [ "$(awk '/^[^#]/ && /EUID/ {print NR; exit}' "$SUITE/workstationadmin-lib.txt")" -lt "$(awk '/\$\(/ && /^[^#]/ {print NR; exit}' "$SUITE/workstationadmin-lib.txt")" ] && ok "EUID guard before external commands in lib" || no "EUID order"
for m in admin-session; do   # d14: firstlogin is the module-3 installer (apply enabled, gated); its tests are in the D14 sections; d15: shared is module 4 (apply enabled, gated; SH27 pins it); d16: apps is module 5 (AP18 pins it)
  grep -q '^wsp_disable_apply ' "$SUITE/workstationadmin-mod-$m.txt" && ok "apply disabled: $m" || no "apply not disabled: $m"; done
SCO=$(bash "$SUITE/workstationadmin-static-check.txt" "$SUITE" --check-allow 2>&1) && ok "static check (premise invariants I-A/I-B): $(printf '%s\n' "$SCO" | tail -1)" || no "static check: $(printf '%s\n' "$SCO" | grep '^HIT' | head -5 | tr '\n' ' ')"
# --- static check: negative self-test (it must flag known bypass shapes) ---
mark "static-check negative self-test"
SCD="$T/sc-neg"; rm -rf "$SCD"; mkdir -p "$SCD"
cat > "$SCD/workstationadmin-mod-bad.txt" <<'B'
#!/bin/bash
. "$_wsp_lib"
a=$(id -un)
if dscl . -read /Users/x UniqueID >/dev/null 2>&1; then :; fi
f() { dscl . -list /Users; }
b=$(printf '%s' "$z" | awk '{print $1}')
if [ "$(printf '%s\n' "$z" | wc -l)" = 1 ]; then :; fi
c=$(printf '%s' "$z" | tr a b && echo ok)
d=$(wsp_do 1 2
   )
if printf '%s\n' "$z" | grep -q x; then :; fi
printf '%s\n' "$z" | awk '{print}' > "$TMP/o"
[ -n "$z" ] && printf '%s' "$z" | sed 's/a/b/' >/dev/null
B
cat > "$SCD/workstationadmin-mod-good.txt" <<'B'
#!/bin/bash
. "$_wsp_lib"
wsp_run a id -un
b=$(printf '%s' "$z" | awk '{print $1}') || b=""
if ! c=$(printf '%s' "$z" | tr a b); then c=""; fi
d=$(wsp_do 1 2
   ) || d=""
B
cat > "$SCD/workstationadmin-mod-good2.txt" <<'B'
#!/bin/bash
. "$_wsp_lib"
wsp_run a id -un
case "$z" in *x*) :;; esac
wsp_any_line "$z" x

B
SCN=$(bash "$SUITE/workstationadmin-static-check.txt" "$SCD" 2>&1); scrc=$?

[ "$scrc" -eq 1 ] && [ "$nN" -ge 3 ] && [ "$nP" -ge 2 ] && [ "$nQ" -ge 1 ] && [ "$nM" -ge 1 ] && [ "$nR" -ge 3 ] && [ "$nG" -eq 0 ] && [ "$nG2" -eq 0 ] \
  && ok "static check flags direct native read incl. condition and brace position (N=$nN), unchecked pipelines (P=$nP, '&&' is no check), pipeline in a decision (Q=$nQ), unchecked multi-line substitution (M=$nM), and (D9 rule R) a piped grep/awk/sed on captured data OUTSIDE \$(...) in if/&&/plain position (R=$nR); the checked-pipeline twin has 0 N/P/Q/M hits, and the pipe-free twin (case, in-shell helper, plain redirect) has 0 hits" \
  || no "static check negative self-test rc=$scrc N=$nN P=$nP Q=$nQ M=$nM R=$nR good=$nG good2=$nG2: $(printf '%s\n' "$SCN" | head -12 | tr '\n' '|')"

# D10 (reviewer S3): rule R also catches absolute-path tools, tools behind command/env/sudo (bare or by path),
# other interpreters, here-strings, process substitution and a pipe at the end of a line. Each bad line must be
# flagged BY LINE NUMBER; the pipe-free twin of every line must not be.
mark "static-check S3 bypass shapes"
SCD="$T/sc-s3"; rm -rf "$SCD"; mkdir -p "$SCD"
cat > "$SCD/workstationadmin-mod-bad3.txt" <<'B'
#!/bin/bash
. "$_wsp_lib"
printf '%s\n' "$z" | /usr/bin/grep -q x
printf '%s\n' "$z" | command awk '{print}' > "$TMP/o"
printf '%s\n' "$z" | env sed 's/a/b/' > "$TMP/o"
printf '%s\n' "$z" | LC_ALL=C grep -q x
printf '%s\n' "$z" | perl -ne 'print' > "$TMP/o"
grep -q x <<< "$z"
grep -q x <(printf '%s' "$z")
printf '%s\n' "$z" |
  sed 's/a/b/' > "$TMP/o"
printf '%s\n' "$z" | /usr/bin/env python3 -c 'pass' > "$TMP/o"
printf '%s\n' "$z" | sudo -n tr a b > "$TMP/o"
printf '%s\n' "$z" | python3.11 -c 'pass' > "$TMP/o"
printf '%s\n' "$z" | perl5 -ne 'print' > "$TMP/o"
printf '%s\n' "$z" | "$tool" -x > "$TMP/o"
printf '%s\n' "$z" | $tool -x > "$TMP/o"
B
cat > "$SCD/workstationadmin-mod-good3.txt" <<'B'
#!/bin/bash
. "$_wsp_lib"
grep -q x "$TMP/f"
awk '{print}' "$TMP/f" > "$TMP/o"
wsp_any_line "$z" x
printf '%s\n' "$z" > "$TMP/o"
c=$(printf '%s' "$z") || c=""
B
SCS=$(bash "$SUITE/workstationadmin-static-check.txt" "$SCD" 2>&1); scs=$?
s3miss=""; for ln in 3 4 5 6 7 8 9 10 12 13 14 15 16 17; do printf '%s\n' "$SCS" | grep -q "^HIT   R workstationadmin-mod-bad3.txt:$ln " || s3miss="$s3miss $ln"; done
s3good=$(printf '%s\n' "$SCS" | grep -c 'workstationadmin-mod-good3')
[ "$scs" -eq 1 ] && [ -z "$s3miss" ] && [ "$s3good" -eq 0 ] && ok "static check S3: R flags /usr/bin/grep, command awk, env sed, LC_ALL=C grep, perl, <<<, <( ), a pipe at end of line, /usr/bin/env python3, sudo tr, versioned interpreters (python3.11, perl5) and a pipe into a variable-named command (\$tool, \"\$tool\"), each by line; pipe-free twins are clean" || no "static check S3 rc=$scs missed lines:$s3miss good=$s3good: $(printf '%s\n' "$SCS" | head -4 | tr '\n' '|')"
rm -rf "$SCD"
# D9 (S3): a stale ALLOW entry is itself a hit; the real suite has none; an empty tree and a broken awk fail closed
sed 's/^lib~R~_r=\$(od -An -N4~/zzbogus-file~R~nothing-here~bogus\n&/' "$SUITE/workstationadmin-static-check.txt" > "$T/sc-stale.txt"
SCZ=$(bash "$T/sc-stale.txt" "$SUITE" --check-allow 2>&1); zc=$?

{ [ "$zc" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCZ" | grep -q 'STALE allow entry' && [ "$zr" -eq 0 ] && [ "$ze" -eq 3 ] && [ "$zb" -eq 3 ]; } && ok "static check: stale ALLOW entry -> hit (--check-allow); real suite has none; empty tree -> 3; broken awk -> 3" || no "static check stale/empty/broken rc stale=$zc real=$zr empty=$ze awk=$zb"
rm -rf "$T/sc-stale.txt" "$T/sc-empty" "$T/sc-brk"
# D9 (S8): wsp_new_salt failure ends the run in the main shell (exit 4), and wsp_rid never generates a salt
setup 73; cat > "$F/stubs/od" <<'W'
#!/bin/bash

chmod 755 "$F/stubs/od"; run workstationadmin-mod-gates.txt --mode inspect
{ [ "$RC" -eq 4 ] && grep -q 'REFUSED: random source' "$F/stderr"; } && ok "S8: salt generation failure (od -N16 fails) -> REFUSED, exit 4, no report" || no "S8 salt failure rc=$RC: $(head -c 200 "$F/stderr")"
rm -f "$F/stubs/od"; run workstationadmin-mod-gates.txt --mode inspect; [ "$RC" -ne 4 ] && ok "S8 control: same module with a working od starts normally (rc=$RC)" || no "S8 control refused"
rm -rf "$T/fx73"
nsc=$(sed -n '/^wsp_rid() {/,/^}/p' "$SUITE/workstationadmin-lib.txt" | grep -v '^ *#' | grep -c 'wsp_new_salt'); [ "$nsc" -eq 0 ] && ok "S8: wsp_rid contains no salt generation (it cannot hide a failing subshell exit)" || no "wsp_rid generates a salt ($nsc)"
# --- public-repo scrub check: synthetic self-test (real site patterns live outside the suite) ---
mark "scrub check self-test"
# D10 (test review d9 note): the synthetic bad samples are ASSEMBLED AT RUN TIME, so this file contains no foreign IP, e-mail, host,
# vendor word or line-exemption token as literal text and needs no exemption itself.
TOK="scrub-""allow"; IPA="8.8.8.$((2*4))"; IPB="8.8.$((2*2)).4"; IPC="8.8.4.$((10/2))"
ACME="acme""-corp"; MAILA="bob@$ACME.com"; HOSTA="https:/""/intranet.$ACME.com/page and www.$ACME.org"
VENDA="Chr""ome"; NOREPLYA="a+1@users"".noreply.github.com"
SCD="$T/scrub"; rm -rf "$SCD" "$T/scrub-pat"; mkdir -p "$SCD" "$T/scrub-pat"
printf '%s\n' '# synthetic' 'zzemployertok' > "$T/scrub-pat/p.txt"
printf '%s\n' 'name=Example Org' 'host=https://intranet.example.com/x' 'ip=10.0.0.5 192.0.2.7 198.51.100.9 203.0.113.1 127.0.0.1' 'mail=a@example.com b@x.test c@d.invalid' 'version 15.5.1 and 1.2.3' > "$SCD/clean.txt"
SCC=$(bash "$SUITE/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt" "$SCD" --site-file "$T/scrub-pat/p.txt" 2>&1); c1=$?
printf '%s\n' 'x ZZEmployerTok y' > "$SCD/site.txt"; printf '%s\n' "ip $IPA" > "$SCD/ip.txt"; printf '%s\n' "mail $MAILA" > "$SCD/mail.txt"
printf '%s\n' "see $HOSTA" > "$SCD/host.txt"; printf '%s\n' "ip $IPB $TOK" > "$SCD/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt"; : > "$SCD/zzemployertok-name.txt"
SCB=$(bash "$SUITE/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt" "$SCD" --site-file "$T/scrub-pat/p.txt" 2>&1); c2=$?
sh_=$(printf '%s\n' "$SCB" | grep -c 'HIT site.txt:1 site-pattern#1'); ip_=$(printf '%s\n' "$SCB" | grep -c 'HIT ip.txt:1 IP'); ml_=$(printf '%s\n' "$SCB" | grep -c 'HIT mail.txt:1 MAIL'); ho_=$(printf '%s\n' "$SCB" | grep -c 'HIT host.txt:1 HOST'); nm_=$(printf '%s\n' "$SCB" | grep -c 'name pattern#1'); al_=$(printf '%s\n' "$SCB" | grep -c 'workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt')
leak=$(printf '%s\n' "$SCB" | grep -ci 'zzemployertok y\|acme-corp\|8\.8\.8\.8')
bash "$SUITE/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt" "$SCD" --site-file "$T/nope.txt" >/dev/null 2>&1; c3=$?
SCG=$(bash "$SUITE/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt" "$SCD" --generic-only 2>&1); c4=$?
cp "$T/scrub-pat/p.txt" "$SCD/inside.txt"; bash "$SUITE/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt" "$SCD" --site-file "$SCD/inside.txt" >/dev/null 2>&1; c5=$?
rm -rf "$SCD/site.txt" "$SCD/ip.txt" "$SCD/mail.txt" "$SCD/host.txt" "$SCD/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt" "$SCD/zzemployertok-name.txt" "$SCD/inside.txt"
bash "$SUITE/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt" "$SCD" --site-file "$T/scrub-pat/p.txt" >/dev/null 2>&1; c6=$?
[ "$c1" -eq 0 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCC" | grep -q 'hits=0' && ok "scrub check: example.com/.test/.invalid, RFC 5737, 10.0.0.x and version numbers are clean" || no "scrub clean control rc=$c1: $SCC"
[ "$c2" -eq 1 ] && [ "$sh_" -eq 1 ] && [ "$ip_" -eq 1 ] && [ "$ml_" -eq 1 ] && [ "$ho_" -ge 1 ] && [ "$nm_" -eq 1 ] && [ "$al_" -eq 0 ] && [ "$leak" -eq 0 ] \
  && ok "scrub check flags site pattern (case-insensitive), file name, foreign IP, e-mail, hostnames; the exemption token works in the allowlisted file; matched text never printed" || no "scrub negative rc=$c2 site=$sh_ ip=$ip_ mail=$ml_ host=$ho_ name=$nm_ allow=$al_ leak=$leak"
[ "$c3" -eq 3 ] && [ "$c5" -eq 3 ] && ok "scrub check fails closed: missing site file -> 3; site file inside the suite dir -> 3" || no "scrub setup rc missing=$c3 inside=$c5"
[ "$c4" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCG" | grep -q 'GENERIC-ONLY' && ok "scrub --generic-only still flags built-in rules and labels itself" || no "scrub generic-only rc=$c4"
[ "$c6" -eq 0 ] && ok "scrub check clean after removal of injected leaks" || no "scrub clean-after rc=$c6"
rm -rf "$SCD" "$T/scrub-pat"
# --- D9 scrub hardening (reviewer B2, S4, S5): invalid ERE, CRLF, traversal, vendor words, noreply ---
mark "scrub check hardening"
SCX="bash $SUITE/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt"
SCD="$T/scrub2"; PD="$T/scrub-pat2"; rm -rf "$SCD" "$PD"; mkdir -p "$SCD" "$PD"
printf '%s\n' 'name=Example Org' > "$SCD/clean.txt"
sc_bad=""
for badre in 'zzbadpat(' 'zzbadpat[abc' 'zzbadpat{2,1}'; do
  printf '%s\n' '# c' 'zzemployertok' "$badre" > "$PD/bad.txt"
  SCI=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/bad.txt" 2>&1); ci=$?
  { [ "$ci" -eq 3 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'pattern #2 is not a valid ERE' && ! printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -qF -- zzbadpat; } || sc_bad="$sc_bad [$ci]"
done
[ -z "$sc_bad" ] && ok "scrub: an invalid ERE at pattern #2 (3 shapes) -> exit 3 'SCRUB-SETUP pattern #2', pattern text never shown (no false clean)" || no "scrub invalid-ERE handling:$sc_bad"
# CRLF pattern file: the "$" anchor must still work (CR stripped), never a silent miss
printf 'zzemployertok$\r\n' > "$PD/crlf.txt"; printf 'x zzemployertok\n' > "$SCD/hit.txt"
SCI=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/crlf.txt" 2>&1); ci=$?
[ "$ci" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'hit.txt:1 site-pattern#1' && ok "scrub: CRLF pattern file still detects an anchored pattern (CR stripped, not a false clean)" || no "scrub CRLF pattern file rc=$ci: $SCI"
# CRLF scanned file: anchored site pattern, IP and e-mail are still found
printf 'x zzemployertok\r\nip %s\r\nmail %s\r\n' "$IPA" "$MAILA" > "$SCD/hit.txt"
SCI=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/crlf.txt" 2>&1); ci=$?
[ "$ci" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'hit.txt:1 site-pattern#1' && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'hit.txt:2 IP' && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'hit.txt:3 MAIL' && ok "scrub: a CRLF-terminated scanned file is normalised (anchored site pattern, IP and e-mail all found)" || no "scrub CRLF scanned file rc=$ci: $SCI"
printf '%s\n' '# c' 'zzemployertok' > "$PD/ok.txt"; rm -f "$SCD/hit.txt"
# traversal: dotfile, nested dir, symlink, .git entry
mkdir -p "$SCD/sub/deep" "$SCD/.hid"; printf 'ip %s\n' "$IPA" > "$SCD/.dotfile"; printf 'mail %s\n' "$MAILA" > "$SCD/sub/deep/f.txt"; printf 'ip %s\n' "$IPB" > "$SCD/.hid/x.txt"
SCI=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/ok.txt" 2>&1); ci=$?
{ [ "$ci" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'HIT .dotfile:1 IP' && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'HIT sub/deep/f.txt:1 MAIL' && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'HIT .hid/x.txt:1 IP'; } && ok "scrub: dotfiles, hidden directories and nested sub-directories are scanned (3 planted leaks found)" || no "scrub traversal rc=$ci: $SCI"
rm -rf "$SCD/.dotfile" "$SCD/sub" "$SCD/.hid"
ln -s /etc/hostname "$SCD/lnk"; SCI=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/ok.txt" 2>&1); ci=$?
{ [ "$ci" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'HIT lnk symlink'; } && ok "scrub: a symlink is refused (HIT), not skipped" || no "scrub symlink rc=$ci"
rm -f "$SCD/lnk"; mkdir -p "$SCD/.git"; printf 'x\n' > "$SCD/.git/config"; SCI=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/ok.txt" 2>&1); ci=$?
{ [ "$ci" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'git-metadata'; } && ok "scrub: a .git entry is refused (scan an export, not a repository)" || no "scrub .git rc=$ci"
rm -rf "$SCD/.git"
# vendor words and personal/noreply addresses
printf 'we default to %s here\n' "$VENDA" > "$SCD/v.txt"; printf '%s\n' "$NOREPLYA" > "$SCD/n.txt"
SCI=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/ok.txt" 2>&1); ci=$?
{ [ "$ci" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'HIT v.txt:1 VEND' && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'HIT n.txt:1 MAIL'; } && ok "scrub: vendor words (VEND) and a noreply/personal address (MAIL) are hits" || no "scrub vendor/noreply rc=$ci: $SCI"
rm -f "$SCD/v.txt" "$SCD/n.txt"
# documentation-*: DOCHIT is reported with exit 0 by default, exit 1 with --strict-docs (release mode)
printf 'ip %s\n' "$IPA" > "$SCD/documentation-x.txt"
SCI=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/ok.txt" 2>&1); ci=$?; SCJ=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/ok.txt" --strict-docs 2>&1); cj=$?
{ [ "$ci" -eq 0 ] && [ "$cj" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'DOCHIT documentation-x.txt:1 IP'; } && ok "scrub: documentation hit -> DOCHIT exit 0; with --strict-docs (release mode) exit 1" || no "scrub strict-docs default=$ci strict=$cj"
rm -f "$SCD/documentation-x.txt"
# D10 (reviewer S1): the exemption token is honoured only in the allowlisted files; elsewhere it is a HIT and the line
# is scanned normally. The exempt=N count is printed and --expect-exempt N makes a different count a HIT.
printf 'ip %s %s\n' "$IPA" "$TOK" > "$SCD/misuse.txt"
SCI=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/ok.txt" 2>&1); ci=$?
{ [ "$ci" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'HIT misuse.txt:1 exempt-token-misuse' && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'HIT misuse.txt:1 IP'; } && ok "scrub S1: the exemption token in a file that is not allowlisted is a HIT (misuse) and the line is still scanned" || no "scrub misuse rc=$ci: $SCI"
rm -f "$SCD/misuse.txt"
printf 'ip %s %s\nip %s %s\n' "$IPB" "$TOK" "$IPC" "$TOK" > "$SCD/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt"
SCI=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/ok.txt" 2>&1); ci=$?
SCK=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/ok.txt" --expect-exempt 2 2>&1); ck=$?
SCL=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/ok.txt" --expect-exempt 3 2>&1); cl=$?
$SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/ok.txt" --expect-exempt x >/dev/null 2>&1; cm=$?
{ [ "$ci" -eq 0 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'exempt=2' && [ "$ck" -eq 0 ] && [ "$cl" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCL" | grep -q 'HIT exempt-count expected=3 got=2' && [ "$cm" -eq 3 ]; } && ok "scrub S1: allowlisted file -> exempt=2 counted; --expect-exempt 2 clean, 3 -> HIT exempt-count, non-numeric -> exit 3" || no "scrub exempt count rc=$ci/$ck/$cl/$cm: $SCL"
rm -f "$SCD/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt"
# D10 (S4): a site pattern with a leading/trailing blank, tab or BOM is rejected (exit 3), text not shown
sc_bad=""
printf '%s\n' '# c' 'zzemployertok' ' zzblankpat' > "$PD/b1.txt"
printf '%s\n' '# c' 'zzemployertok' "$(printf 'zzblankpat\t')" > "$PD/b2.txt"
printf '# c\nzzemployertok\n\357\273\277zzblankpat\n' > "$PD/b3.txt"
for bf in b1 b2 b3; do
  SCI=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/$bf.txt" 2>&1); ci=$?
  { [ "$ci" -eq 3 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'SCRUB-SETUP site pattern #2 has a leading or trailing blank' && ! printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -qF -- zzblankpat; } || sc_bad="$sc_bad $bf:$ci"
done
[ -z "$sc_bad" ] && ok "scrub S4: a site pattern with a leading blank, a trailing tab or a BOM -> exit 3 (pattern text never shown)" || no "scrub blank/BOM pattern handling:$sc_bad"
rm -f "$PD/b1.txt" "$PD/b2.txt" "$PD/b3.txt"
# D10 (S5): a file-name hit does not print the file path (entry#K instead)
printf 'x\n' > "$SCD/zzemployertok-secret.txt"
SCI=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/ok.txt" 2>&1); ci=$?
{ [ "$ci" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'HIT entry#[0-9]* name pattern#1' && ! printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -qi 'zzemployertok\|secret'; } && ok "scrub S5: a site-pattern file-name hit is reported as entry#K name pattern#N; the path never appears in the output" || no "scrub name-hit output rc=$ci"
rm -f "$SCD/zzemployertok-secret.txt"
# D11 (S7): the exemption is matched on the RELATIVE path, so a same-named file in a sub-directory is not exempt
mkdir -p "$SCD/sub"; printf 'ip %s %s\n' "$IPB" "$TOK" > "$SCD/sub/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt"
SCI=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/ok.txt" 2>&1); ci=$?
{ [ "$ci" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'HIT sub/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt:1 exempt-token-misuse' && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'HIT sub/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt:1 IP'; } && ok "scrub S7: a file named like the allowlisted check but in a sub-directory is NOT exempt (misuse HIT, line still scanned)" || no "scrub relative-path exemption rc=$ci: $SCI"
rm -rf "$SCD/sub"
# D11 (S9): the exempt count has its own channel; a file named like the old in-stream marker cannot forge or break it
printf 'ip %s\n' "$IPA" > "$SCD/#EXEMPT 9"
SCI=$($SCX "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/ok.txt" 2>&1); ci=$?
{ [ "$ci" -eq 1 ] && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'HIT #EXEMPT 9:1 IP' && printf '%s\n' "$SCI" | grep -q 'exempt=0'; } && ok "scrub S9: a file named '#EXEMPT 9' is an ordinary hit and does not change exempt=0 (count is not in the hit stream)" || no "scrub exempt channel rc=$ci: $SCI"
rm -f "$SCD/#EXEMPT 9"
# empty directory and failing tools fail closed
mkdir -p "$T/scrub-empty"; $SCX "$T/scrub-empty" --site-file "$PD/ok.txt" >/dev/null 2>&1; ce=$?
mkdir -p "$T/scrub-brk"; printf '#!/bin/bash\nexit 2\n' > "$T/scrub-brk/grep"; printf '#!/bin/bash\nexit 1\n' > "$T/scrub-brk/awk"; chmod 755 "$T/scrub-brk/grep" "$T/scrub-brk/awk"
env PATH="$T/scrub-brk:$PATH" bash "$SUITE/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt" "$SCD" --site-file "$PD/ok.txt" >/dev/null 2>&1; cg=$?
env PATH="$T/scrub-brk:$PATH" bash "$SUITE/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt" "$SCD" --generic-only >/dev/null 2>&1; ca=$?
{ [ "$ce" -eq 3 ] && [ "$cg" -eq 3 ] && [ "$ca" -eq 3 ]; } && ok "scrub: empty directory -> 3; grep status 2 -> 3; awk failure -> 3 (no false clean)" || no "scrub tool-failure rc empty=$ce grep2=$cg awk1=$ca"
rm -rf "$SCD" "$PD" "$T/scrub-empty" "$T/scrub-brk"
# D10 (S1): the number of lines that carry the exemption token in the one allowlisted file is pinned here (updated only on purpose, at freeze)
EXPECT_EXEMPT=3
SCS=$(bash "$SUITE/workstationadmin-scrub-check.txt" "$SUITE" --expect-exempt "$EXPECT_EXEMPT" --generic-only 2>&1) && ok "scrub (generic rules) on the suite files: $(printf '%s\n' "$SCS" | tail -1)" || no "scrub generic on suite: $(printf '%s\n' "$SCS" | head -5 | tr '\n' ' ')"
grep -q 'WSP_ALLOW_OPERATOR' "$SUITE"/UserSpace-*.txt && no "operator override present" || ok "no operator override"
finish
